Using variational inference, we show how approximate joint likelihood optimization over reference and target domains can be performed using unpaired data, providing a principled basis for paired-sample generation and cross-domain analysis. We validate CASL-VAE on semi-synthetic neuroimaging data, demonstrating improved subtype recovery and paired-sample generation compared to baseline clustering and generative models. We also validate its ability to reveal biologically plausible heterogeneity in Alzheimer's disease.

</details>


### [63] [Classifier Chain-based Pathological Test Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08299)
*Abu Rafe Md Jamil,Nayan Malakar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于分类器链（CC）的病理检测推荐系统，利用患者症状在医生问诊前推荐检测项目。逻辑回归+CC模型准确率达98.83%，多数投票集成模型Precision 0.93、Recall 0.85、F1-score 0.89。采用SHAP进行可解释性分析，模型推理与医学知识一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诊断检测的延迟推荐和医生的主观判断会影响有效诊疗，需要一种能够根据患者症状在医生问诊前加速检测选择的推荐系统。

Method: 将检测推荐建模为多标签分类问题，采用分类器链（CC）技术考虑检测间的依赖关系。使用SOUTHERN.IML病理数据构建数据集，对比逻辑回归、决策树、随机森林等算法，并利用SHAP进行可解释性分析。

Result: 逻辑回归+CC模型准确率最高达98.83%；多数投票集成模型效果最佳，Precision 0.93、Recall 0.85、F1-score 0.89。SHAP解释显示模型推理与医学知识一致。

Conclusion: 分类器链能够提升传统算法在诊断过程中的效率，提供准确的检测推荐，且模型具有临床可解释性，可作为辅助工具帮助医生在关键场景中做出合理决策。

Abstract: Accurate and timely diagnoses are essential for quality patient care. However, delayed recommendation of diagnostic tests and physicians' subjective interpretations can hinder effective care. This study introduces a pathological test recommendation system that speeds up the test selection process using patient symptoms before physician consultation. The recommendation task is framed as a multi-label classification problem utilising the Classifier Chain (CC) technique to consider dependencies between tests. We collected data from the SOUTHERN.IML pathology and then created a custom dataset with the help of the expertise. Multiple machine learning algorithms, including Logistic Regression, Decision Tree, and Random Forest, were applied to compare models and identify the best fit for our study context. The Logistic Regression with CC model had the highest overall accuracy at 98.83%, while the Majority Voting ensemble model provided the best balance with a precision of 0.93, recall of 0.85, and F1-score of 0.89. To ensure transparency of the models and clinical interpretability, we used Explainable AI (XAI) techniques utilising SHAP (SHapley Additive Explanations), which identifies how each symptom is contributing to a test recommendation. The diagnostic reasoning revealed by the model was consistent with established medical knowledge of symptoms for the recommended tests, which further adds confidence to the model's reliability for diagnostic purposes. The reasoning could help physicians make logical decisions in critical scenarios. Overall, our findings suggest that CC can improve the efficiency of the traditional algorithms in diagnostic process providing accurate test recommendations.

</details>


### [64] [Learning $\mathsf{AC}^0$ under Locally Sampleable Graphical Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08303)
*Weiming Feng,Xiongxin Yang,Yixiao Yu,Yiyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在满足局部采样条件的图模型下，对AC⁰电路进行拟多项式时间学习的算法，通过截断Glauber动力学建立新的低度近似，摆脱了先前工作中对多项式增长条件的要求，并将其应用于硬核模型和Ising模型等双自旋系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将AC⁰电路的学习从均匀分布扩展到更广泛的相关分布（如图模型）一直是计算学习理论的长期挑战。先前的工作需要图模型同时满足强空间混合与多项式增长条件，后一限制过于严格，亟需突破。

Method: 通过模拟并适当截断经典的Glauber动力学，为Gibbs分布建立了一种新的低度近似，以此替代对多项式增长条件的需求。

Result: 在仅需存在有效局部采样器的图模型下，实现了对AC⁰的拟多项式时间学习器；在任意有界度图上，将学习器推广至硬核模型和Ising模型，且覆盖范围接近各自采样阈值。

Conclusion: 本文通过截断Glauber动力学的新低度近似技术，显著放宽了图模型下AC⁰可学习性的条件，为相关分布下的电路学习开辟了新路径。

Abstract: The problem of learning constant-depth circuits holds profound implications for computational learning theory. In a seminal result, by introducing the low-degree algorithm, Linial, Mansour, and Nisan (J. ACM 1993) presented a quasipolynomial-time learner for $\mathsf{AC}^0$ under the uniform distribution. However, obtaining comparable learning guarantees for broader classes of correlated distributions has remained a longstanding challenge. Recently, Chandrasekaran, Gaitonde, Moitra, and Vasilyan (arXiv 2026) extended these guarantees to Gibbs distributions on bounded-degree graphical models with both strong spatial mixing and polynomial growth.
  In this paper, we give a quasipolynomial-time learner for $\mathsf{AC}^0$ under graphical models that admit efficient local samplers, circumventing the polynomial-growth requirement in prior work. The key ingredient is a new low-degree approximation for Gibbs distributions, established by simulating and suitably truncating the classical Glauber dynamics. As applications, this framework yields learners for two-spin systems, including the hard-core model and Ising model, on arbitrary bounded-degree graphs, in regimes approaching their respective sampling thresholds.

</details>


### [65] [Write-Protected Discrete Bottlenecks for Language-Grounded World Models: A Structural Limitation and Sufficient Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08312)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言梯度直接注入离散符号瓶颈会导致结构性问题（符号坍缩），并提出三重约束方案：切断梯度链、非参数语义记忆表、DP-Means聚类，实现97.2%的语义绑定准确率，零符号坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑语言特征直接注入机器人世界模型离散符号系统的安全性假设，发现该假设存在结构性缺陷（Gumbel-Softmax瓶颈下语言梯度的注入会导致符号坍缩或语义学习失败），亟需最小化架构约束来防止这种失败。

Method: 提出三重约束：(1) z.detach()切断符号瓶颈处的梯度链；(2) 零参数零梯度的非参数化记忆表（Dict[symbol→Counter[label]]），用共现计数替代梯度绑定；(3) 基于DP-Means流式聚类处理符号碰撞，自动分裂子簇。

Result: Vanilla估计器坍缩至2.2/64个符号（4/5种子），五种抗坍缩策略语义准确率≤9.2%；三重约束在74次独立运行中实现32种子零符号坍缩，97.2%语义绑定准确率（vs无Layer3的22.2%），跨三种编码器架构、两种环境、三种纹理条件达79-100%绑定率，仅训练<2M参数，无需微调LLM。

Conclusion: 在GumbelBottleneck类离散瓶颈中，语言梯度与符号系统的冲突是结构性的而非优化问题；所提三重约束是防止该失败的最小充分条件，无需端到端梯度注入即可实现高效语义绑定。

Abstract: How should language interface with a world model's discrete symbol system? The dominant paradigm -- end-to-end injection of LLM/VLM features into robot world models (RT-2, Octo, PaLM-E) -- implicitly assumes that language gradients can directly shape physical symbol representations. We ask whether this assumption is safe, find that it is not, and characterize the minimal architectural constraint that prevents the failure. Any language gradient entering a Gumbel-softmax-based discrete symbol bottleneck forces a structural trade-off: the vanilla estimator collapses to 2.2/64 symbols (4/5 seeds), while five anti-collapse strategies maintain diversity but fail to learn semantic labels (all <= 9.2% accuracy). No tested GumbelBottleneck variant achieves both objectives simultaneously. Within this family of discrete bottlenecks, the failure is structural rather than a matter of optimization. We characterize a sufficient set of three constraints that prevent the failure: (1) cut the gradient chain (z.detach()), preventing language signals from reaching the symbol bottleneck; (2) provide a gradient-free semantic channel -- a non-parametric Memory Table (Dict[symbol -> Counter[label]], zero parameters, zero gradients) where co-occurrence counting replaces gradient-based binding; (3) handle symbol collisions via DP-Means streaming clustering for automatic sub-cluster splitting. All three layers together achieve 97.2% grounding accuracy vs. 22.2% without Layer 3. Across two experiments spanning 74 independent runs, we demonstrate zero symbol collapse in all 32 seeds, with the blackboard achieving 79-100% semantic binding across three encoder architectures (CNN, V-JEPA 300M, CLIP ViT-L), two environments, and three texture conditions. The fix trains fewer than 2M parameters and requires no LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [66] [ArtMine: Discovering and Formalizing Artistic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08331)
*Kaustubh Kumar,Ashutosh Ranjan,Vivek Srivastava,Blessin Varkey,Shirish Karande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ArtMine框架，通过整合碎片化历史证据（档案、草稿、通信等）并利用皮尔士溯因推理代理，自动推断并形式化艺术创作过程，生成可解释、可审计的创作流程表示，并可通过自我反思优化生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI仅能模拟最终成品分布，无法建模艺术创作背后的迭代决策、材料操作和语境影响；而艺术工作流仅被碎片化文档记录，缺乏形式化计算方法。

Method: 将异构历史证据整合为结构化知识库，利用皮尔士溯因推理代理推断基于证据的生产步骤，将其转换为组合图与渲染提示，并通过自我反思（比较生成与参考艺术品之间的偏差）进行优化。

Result: 通过跨艺术家和艺术运动的开放域历史来源案例研究，证明碎片化文档证据能够支持连贯、可解释且可审计的艺术工作流表示。

Conclusion: 通过建模创作过程而非仅最终成品，推动了以过程为中心的人机协同创意系统发展，可支持艺术解读、创意教育、反思协作及文化生产的计算研究。

Abstract: Understanding how artworks are created requires reasoning about the iterative decisions, material operations, and contextual influences that shape artistic production. While recent generative AI systems can synthesize artworks with high fidelity, they primarily model distributions over finished artifacts rather than the creative processes underlying their creation. In practice, artistic workflows are only partially documented through fragmented sources such as archival records, preparatory studies, correspondence, etc., making process-level understanding difficult to formalize computationally. In this work, we introduce ArtMine, a framework for discovering and formalizing artistic processes from heterogeneous historical evidence. Our approach synthesizes heterogeneous artwork evidence into a structured repository, from which a Peircean abductive agent infers evidence-grounded production steps. These steps are converted into a compositional graph and rendering prompt, then optimized through self-reflection over deviations between the generated and reference artworks. We provide a preliminary proof-of-concept case study using open-domain historical sources across multiple artists and artistic movements, demonstrating that fragmented documentary evidence can support coherent, interpretable, and auditable representations of artistic workflows. By modeling creative processes rather than only final artifacts, our work moves toward process-centred human-AI co-creativity systems that can support artistic interpretation, creative education, reflective collaboration, and computational studies of cultural production.

</details>


### [67] [AutoAnchor: Stable Diffusion Unlearning Using Cross-Attention as a Manifold Surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08337)
*Siyuan Wen,Jiahao Zeng,Ningning Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对文本到图像扩散模型中的遗忘学习（unlearning）不稳定性问题，提出了一种两阶段框架MYSYS，通过自动合成流形近端锚点（manifold-proximal anchors）并结合交叉注意力一致性损失，实现了更稳健、无偏的概念移除与模型效用保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散遗忘技术中，基于锚点的方法依赖手动语义选择的锚点导致有偏遗忘，而无锚点方法因潜在空间更新不受约束而存在不鲁棒的遗忘问题。本文在流形假设下理论证明了缺乏流形近端锚点会导致显著的法向空间漂移，从而降低遗忘性能。

Method: 提出MYSYS两阶段框架：1）自动合成流形近端锚点；2）引入交叉注意力一致性损失作为流形邻近性的高效替代，避免直接几何流形优化的计算不可行性。

Result: 在多个最先进基线方法上，目标概念移除提升高达31.04%（CLIP分数），非目标效用提升高达4.18%；可轻松集成到现有扩散遗忘方法中，平均提升概念移除6.30%、效用6.65%。

Conclusion: MYSYS有效实现了稳健且无偏的遗忘，证明了流形近端锚点对于稳定扩散遗忘学习的关键作用，且具有良好的通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Diffusion unlearning is essential for mitigating the generation of harmful or copyrighted content in text-to-image models. Current diffusion unlearning techniques determine the model update direction by either using alternatives of the target concept as an anchor or using empty prompts. The anchor-based method relies on manually and semantically-chosen anchors that risk biased unlearning, while the anchor-free method inherently suffers from unrobust unlearning due to unconstrained latent updates. In this work, we theoretically formalize such unstable diffusion unlearning issues under the manifold hypothesis and prove that lacking a manifold-proximal anchor inevitably induces significant normal-space drift that degrades unlearning performance. To achieve stable unlearning, we propose \mysysn, a two-stage framework that automatically synthesizes manifold-proximal anchors. However, direct geometric manifold optimization is computationally intractable. To address this challenge, \mysys introduces a novel cross-attention consistency loss which serves as a highly efficient surrogate of manifold proximity. Experimental results demonstrate that \mysys effectively achieves robust and unbiased unlearning across various state-of-the-art baselines, significantly improving targeted concept removal (by up to 31.04\% in CLIP score) and non-target utility (by up to 4.18\% in CLIP score). Moreover, \mysys can also be easily integrated into existing diffusion unlearning methods to enhance their unlearning performance (by 6.30\% for concept removal and 6.65\% for utility on average).

</details>


### [68] [Spectral Analysis of Dueling Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08340)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对去中心化表格型对偶Q-learning进行了理论分析，建立了确定性情形下的精确切换线性系统表示和随机采样情形下的有限时间误差界，阐明了价值和优势更新对Q函数不同分量的增益作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶Q-learning将Q函数分解为价值函数和优势函数，能提升学习效率，但其理论理解仍然有限。现有工作仅分析了正则化版本的对偶Q-learning，纯表格更新的收敛性尚未完全理解。

Method: 首先对中心化表格分解给出直接解释；推导了确定性对偶Q-learning的精确切换线性系统表示；对随机采样版本给出了期望意义下的有限时间误差界。

Result: 建立了无正则化、无投影的常数步长对偶Q-learning递归的收敛保证；阐明了价值更新和优势更新对Q函数的动作公共分量（价值函数）和动作差分分量（优势函数）的不同增益作用。

Conclusion: 本文的理论分析增强了对对偶Q-learning的理解，揭示了其价值-优势分解如何影响学习动态和收敛行为。

Abstract: Q-learning is a fundamental algorithm in reinforcement learning (RL) for solving discounted Markov decision processes (MDPs) when the transition kernel is unknown. The deep Q-network (DQN) extends Q-learning by using a deep neural network for Q-function approximation, which makes Q-learning applicable to more practical high-dimensional problems. Dueling Q-learning decomposes the Q-function into a value function and an advantage function and learns the two components jointly, which can improve learning efficiency. However, the theoretical understanding of dueling Q-learning is still limited. Recent work has initiated an analysis of tabular dueling Q-learning, but existing guarantees focus on a regularized formulation and leave the pure tabular update less completely understood. This paper strengthens that line of analysis by adding a direct interpretation of the centered tabular decomposition and by establishing convergence guarantees for the unregularized, unprojected constant step-size recursion. In particular, we derive an exact switching linear system representation for deterministic dueling Q-learning and a finite-time error bound in expectation for the sampled stochastic version. The analysis clarifies how the value and advantage updates act as different gains on the action-common (value function) and action-differential (advantage function) components of the Q-function.

</details>


### [69] [Certified Interventional Fidelity: Anytime-Valid, Adaptive Evaluation of Causal Claims in Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08349)
*Amir Asiaee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Certified Interventional Fidelity (CIF)，一个用于可解释性评估的统计框架，通过将干预实验的报告量化为因果估计量并提供置信区间和任意有效的置信序列，包括针对自适应干预采样的有界混合重要性加权，从而解决当前可解释性评估中缺乏统计严谨性的问题。实验表明方差自适应投注序列可将认证成本降低 10-30 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机械可解释性中的干预实验（如激活交换、消融等）通常仅报告点估计值，而评估过程可能随时间监控或自适应调整，使得报告的保真度或补丁效应是稳定的因果断言还是有限采样和评估选择的偶然结果难以判断。

Method: CIF 首先将报告量写为因果估计量（即关于指定输入分布和干预分布的有界得分的期望），然后提供置信区间和任意有效的置信序列，并采用有界混合重要性加权处理自适应干预采样，实例化包括 Hoeffding 风格序列和方差自适应投注序列。

Result: 在 MNIST 抽象和 GPT-2 Small IOI 电路上，CIF 能够认证高保真度的断言，揭示方法间表观差异在统计上不显著的情况，并使用显干预分布敏感性；方差自适应投注序列将认证成本降低 10-30 倍。

Conclusion: CIF 为可解释性评估提供了统计严谨性保障，使得干预实验的因果断言更加可靠，同时通过自适应方法提高了统计效率。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability often evaluates explanations by intervening on a model: swapping hidden states, patching activations, ablating components, or comparing a compressed model to the original one. These experiments are usually summarized by a point estimate, even though the evaluation may be monitored while it runs or adapted toward suspected failures. This makes it hard to tell whether a reported fidelity or patching effect is a stable causal claim or a consequence of finite sampling and evaluation choices. We introduce Certified Interventional Fidelity (CIF), a statistical layer for interventional interpretability evaluations. CIF first writes the quantity being reported as a causal estimand: an expectation of a bounded score over a stated input distribution and a stated intervention distribution. It then provides confidence intervals and anytime-valid confidence sequences for this estimand, including under adaptive intervention sampling via bounded mixture importance weighting. We instantiate CIF with Hoeffding-style sequences and variance-adaptive betting sequences, the latter reducing certification cost by 10-30x in our experiments. On MNIST abstractions and GPT-2 Small IOI circuits, CIF certifies high-fidelity claims, shows when apparent method differences are not statistically supported, and makes sensitivity to the intervention distribution explicit.

</details>


### [70] [Self-Adaptive Anomaly Detection with Reinforcement Learning and Human Feedback in Connected Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08373)
*Matthias Weiß,Athreya Hosahalli Prakash,Maurice Artelt,Falk Dettinger,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向自主网联车辆系统（CPS）的在线异常检测框架，集成了注意力增强的深度Q网络检测器选择、三统计漂移探测器协同报警、以及基于人类反馈的优先重放重训练机制。在自动代客泊车微服务测试平台上的实验表明，该方法F1可达0.69，远优于单一检测器（最高0.11），并在软件更新后通过操作员触发重训练实现持续适应而不会灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网联车辆作为自主CPS，运行中需持续监测异常；但系统本身会因OTA更新、配置变化和工作负载迁移而不断演化，导致静态诊断方法性能悄无声息地退化。现有方法要么只关注自动模型适应，要么只关注操作员集成，缺乏统一的协调监督闭环。

Method: 提出三层协调机制：(1) 因子化深度Q网络+自注意力机制，从候选池中为每个微服务选择最优检测器，利用服务间依赖关系；(2) 三个统计漂移检测器组成的集成，三者一致时才报警，优先保证精确率；(3) 人在环重训练机制，采用待定过渡缓冲区和60/40优先重放策略，允许操作员注入专家知识同时保留对历史数据分布的学习。

Result: 在自动代客泊车应用的7个后端微服务测试平台上，注意力增强智能体的F1为0.69，而任何单一检测器统一应用最高仅0.11。真实软件更新导致概念漂移后F1降至0.52；经操作员触发重训练后，在新分布上恢复至0.65，同时在旧分布上保持0.69，证明了持续适应能力且无灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 所提出的在线异常检测框架通过检测器自适应选择、漂移感知报警和人在环重训练的协调机制，能够有效应对自主CPS的演化特性，实现了持续适应与历史知识保持的平衡。

Abstract: Connected vehicles are autonomous cyber-physical systems whose behavior must be continuously monitored during operation to detect deviations from normal operation before they propagate into failures. Such evaluation is challenging because the systems themselves evolve: over-the-air updates, configuration changes, and shifting workloads alter the definition of normal behavior, causing static diagnostic methods to degrade silently over time. Existing approaches typically address either automated model adaptation or operator integration in isolation, rather than as a single coordinated supervisory loop.
  This paper presents an online anomaly detection framework for autonomous CPS that integrates three coordinated mechanisms. A factorized deep Q-network with self-attention selects the most suitable detector from a candidate pool for each monitored service, exploiting inter-service dependencies in the microservice topology. An ensemble of three statistical drift detectors monitors the input distribution and raises an alarm only when all three concur, prioritizing precision over recall. A human-in-the-loop retraining mechanism, built around a pending transition buffer and a 60/40 prioritized replay strategy, allows the operator to incorporate expert knowledge while preserving the system's learned response to prior data distributions.
  The framework is evaluated on a connected-vehicle testbed running an automated valet parking application across seven backend microservices. The attention-augmented agent achieves an F1 score of 0.69, compared to at most 0.11 for any single detector applied uniformly. Following a real software update that induces measurable concept drift, F1 drops to 0.52; after operator-triggered retraining, performance recovers to 0.65 on the new distribution while remaining at 0.69 on the prior one, demonstrating sustained adaptation without catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [71] [Eigenvalue Calibration for Semantic Embeddings of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08377)
*Sebastian G. Gruber,Nassim Walha,Francis Bach,Florian Buettner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过温度缩放校准语义嵌入特征值的新框架，建立了特征值的校准理论，并在多种真实场景中验证了LLM存在系统性过度自信问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对分类概率的校准方法无法直接迁移到语义嵌入的特征值上，而特征值已成为LLM不确定性量化的关键工具，因此需要专门的理论框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 将LLM及其生成答案的语义嵌入视为密度矩阵预测器，提出对特征值应用温度缩放进行校准；建立了熵-风险等价关系，推导了特征值校准不等式，并证明了温度缩放特征值在最小化适当评分风险时达到最优校准。

Result: 在多种真实场景下，当前LLM系统性地表现出过度自信；实验结果验证了所提理论框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了语义嵌入不确定性量化的理论基础与实践应用，为LLM的可靠部署提供了新的校准工具。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is central to the reliable deployment of large language models (LLMs), and eigenvalues of semantic embeddings have recently emerged as a key tool in state-of-the-art methods. However, conventional calibration results developed for classification probabilities cannot be directly transferred to eigenvalues. We address this gap by proposing a novel framework for calibrating the eigenvalues of semantic embeddings. We interpret LLMs combined with semantic embeddings of their generated answers as density matrix predictors, and we propose a novel approach to calibrate density matrix predictors by applying temperature scaling to their eigenvalues. We establish entropy-risk equivalence under calibration, derive a central calibration inequality specific to eigenvalues, and prove that temperature-scaled eigenvalues optimize calibration when minimizing proper score risks. Experiments on a variety of real-world settings show that current LLMs are systematically overconfident, and validate our theoretical findings. Together, these results advance the foundations and practice of uncertainty quantification for semantic embeddings.

</details>


### [72] [Dynamics of Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Near a Manifold of Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08380)
*Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将梯度下降中sharpness的理论从标量输出过参数化最小二乘扩展到了向量输出情形，并将平坦最小值从孤立点推广到流形邻域，成功应用于深度矩阵分解，证明了平坦最小值集构成球面乘积上的纤维丛，且sharpness沿该流形是Morse-Bott的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典梯度下降理论要求步长小于sharpness倒数的两倍，但深度神经网络训练中常违反该条件。现有工作仅针对标量输出过参数化最小二乘的孤立平坦最小值建立了理论，需要推广到更一般的向量输出场景和流形上的平坦最小值（如矩阵分解），以弥合理论与实践之间的差距。

Method: (1) 将规范形式和三个收敛定理从标量输出推广到向量输出过参数化最小二乘；(2) 将平坦最小值从孤立点推广到流形邻域；(3) 通过一种新颖的方法求解奇异偏微分方程，克服技术挑战；(4) 将理论应用于深度矩阵分解，分析其结构性质。

Result: 建立了向量输出场景下的规范形式和三个收敛定理；证明了深度矩阵分解中平坦最小值集构成球面乘积上的纤维丛；证明了sharpness沿该流形是Morse-Bott的。

Conclusion: 该工作显著扩展了大步长梯度下降的理论适用范围，为理解深度神经网络训练中违反经典步长条件的现象提供了更一般的理论框架。

Abstract: An important quantity in the theory of gradient descent (GD) is the \emph{sharpness}, defined as the largest eigenvalue of the objective Hessian. Classical analyses typically require the step size to be uniformly smaller than twice the reciprocal of the sharpness, but this condition is frequently violated in the training of deep neural networks. Recent work bridges this gap in the setting of overparametrised least-squares with a \emph{single scalar output}, providing a normal form for large-step GD in a neighbourhood of an \emph{isolated} flat minimum and establishing three corresponding convergence results. In this paper, we extend this theory in two directions: (1) to overparametrised least-squares with \emph{vector-valued outputs} (including regression with arbitrarily many observations), and (2) to a neighbourhood of a \emph{manifold} of flat minima (which we show is essential for applications such as matrix factorisation). We generalise both the normal form and all three convergence theorems of \cite{macdonaldeos} to this broader setting, overcoming several technical challenges, including the solution of a singular partial differential equation via a novel method that may be of independent interest. We further show that our framework applies to deep matrix factorisation under mild assumptions, yielding several new structural results. In particular, we prove that the set of flat minima forms a fibre bundle over a product of spheres, and that the sharpness is Morse-Bott along this manifold.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond Backpropagation: Monte Carlo Method Can Train Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08406)
*Hong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明最简单的蒙特卡洛算法——随机突变一个参数，若损失下降则保留，否则重试——可以在单GPU上有效训练深层网络，无需梯度、批归一化或残差连接，甚至支持剪枝训练、离散权重、高斯激活函数等非传统场景，并在超过20层的深度网络、宽网络（16384隐藏神经元）以及简单Transformer架构上验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播依赖梯度带来了固有的梯度消失和梯度爆炸问题，追求无梯度方法一直是人工智能领域的目标。本文旨在探索一种极简的、无需梯度的蒙特卡洛方法是否能实际训练深度网络。

Method: 采用最简单的蒙特卡洛算法：随机突变网络中的一个参数，如果损失函数下降则保留该突变，否则恢复原参数并重试。该方法不使用批归一化或残差连接，在单GPU上实现。

Result: 成功训练了超过20层的深度网络、单隐藏层宽网络（最多16384个隐藏神经元）以及简单Transformer架构（在MNIST图像分类和Tiny Shakespeare字符级语言建模上验证）。该方法还支持纯剪枝训练、离散权重、高斯激活函数等非传统场景。

Conclusion: 这种简单的无梯度方法为理解神经网络的自组织和学习机制提供了补充视角，也为构建受物理启发的深度学习系统提供了替代路径。

Abstract: Backpropagation (BP) dominates deep learning training, but its reliance on gradients brings inherent troubles -- vanishing and exploding gradients. The pursuit of gradient-free methods has long been a goal in the field of artificial intelligence. This paper shows that indeed the simplest Monte Carlo algorithm implemented on a single GPU -- randomly mutate a parameter, keep it if the loss decreases, otherwise retry -- can practically train deep networks. This gradient-free method does not even need common techniques such as batch normalization or residual connections to directly train sufficiently deep networks. More remarkably, its flexibility extends to several nontrivial scenarios: it enables pure pruning training, supports discrete weights, accommodates unconventional transfer functions such as Gaussian, and reveals the substantial redundancy of deep networks. We have demonstrated its feasibility on deep networks with more than 20 layers, single-hidden-layer wide networks with up to 16,384 hidden neurons, and even a simple Transformer architecture trained on both image classification (MNIST) and character-level language modeling (Tiny Shakespeare). This simple gradient-free method may offer a complementary perspective for understanding the self-organization and learning mechanisms of neural networks, and also provides an alternative route for building physically inspired deep learning systems.

</details>


### [74] [Predicting Male Fertility Using Machine Learning: A Semen Parameters Based Analysis with the VISEM Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08429)
*Shahnawaz Qureshi,Raja Khurram Shahzad,Muhammad Fozan,Emal Kawal,Syed Aziz Shah,Sattam Al-Anazi,Syed MuhammadZeeshan Iqbal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习算法和VISEM数据集，基于精子浓度、活力和形态等关键精液参数对男性生育状态（可育、亚不育、不育）进行分类，其中Nearest Centroid分类器以94.2%的准确率优于其他模型，表明机器学习可提供快速、客观的精液质量评估，辅助临床决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 男性不育是生殖健康中常被忽视的问题，精液分析是临床评估的基石。本文旨在利用机器学习算法基于关键精液参数对男性生育状态进行分类，以提供更快速、客观的评估手段。

Method: 使用VISEM数据集（85名参与者，按WHO标准分为可育、亚不育、不育三类），经过预处理和特征工程后，利用LazyPredict框架训练并评估了40多种分类模型，采用5折交叉验证和多类ROC-AUC分析验证模型鲁棒性。

Result: Nearest Centroid分类器达到了94.2%的准确率，优于支持向量机和二次判别分析等其他模型。

Conclusion: 机器学习模型能够提供快速、准确、客观的精液质量评估，有望支持男科和辅助生殖技术中的临床决策，增强生育诊断并为患者个性化治疗策略提供信息。

Abstract: Male infertility is a significant yet often underdiagnosed aspect of reproductive health, with semen analysis serving as the cornerstone of clinical evaluation. To address this problem, this study investigates the use of machine learning algorithms to classify male fertility status based on key semen parameters, i.e., sperm concentration, motility, and morphology, using the VISEM dataset. This dataset includes semen samples from 85 participants, classified into three categories, i.e., Fertile, Sub-Fertile, and Infertile, according to the World Health Organization's criteria. After pre-processing and feature engineering, the dataset was used to train and assess multiple classification models using the LazyPredict framework. Among the more than 40 algorithms tested, the Nearest Centroid classifier achieved an accuracy of 94.2%, outperforming other models such as Support Vector Machines and Quadratic Discriminant Analysis. The model's robustness was validated using 5-fold cross-validation and multiclass ROC-AUC analysis. This study illustrates that machine learning models can provide fast, accurate, and objective assessments of semen quality, potentially supporting clinical decision-making in andrology and assisted reproductive technologies. These findings emphasize the growing potential of machine learning to enhance fertility diagnostics and inform patient-specific treatment strategies.

</details>


### [75] [MatBind: A Shared Embedding Space for Multimodal Materials Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08470)
*Le Yang,Anoop K. Chandran,Jona Östreicher,Evgenii Sovetkin,Adrian Mirza,Sebastien Bompas,Bashir Kazimi,Pascal Friederich,Stefan Kesselheim,Kevin Maik Jablonka,Stefan Sandfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MatBind是一个对比学习框架，将晶体结构、粉末X射线衍射、电子态密度和文本四种材料模态对齐到统一嵌入空间，以晶体结构为物理锚点，实现零样本跨模态检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统材料科学中，原子结构、衍射图谱、电子态密度和自然语言描述等异质数据模态被孤立存储和分析，难以跨表征边界关联或查询材料，需要统一表征框架。

Method: 提出MatBind对比学习框架，以晶体结构为物理锚点，将四种材料模态（晶体结构、模拟pXRD、DOS、文本）对齐到统一嵌入空间；在训练过程中从未显式配对的模态间诱导对齐，实现零样本跨模态检索。

Result: 学习到的嵌入空间无需显式监督即可按物理有意义的属性组织材料；查询时组合多模态可系统提升检索性能；实现了零样本跨模态检索。

Conclusion: 将异构材料数据视为同一物理实在的互补投影，而非孤立数据源，不仅是实践选择，也符合底层物理一致性。

Abstract: Fully characterizing a crystalline material requires integrating heterogeneous data sources -- atomic structures, diffraction patterns, electronic density of states, and natural language -- each of which captures a different facet of the same physical object. In practice, however, these modalities are stored and analyzed in isolation, making it difficult to relate or query materials across representational boundaries. We present MatBind, a contrastive learning framework that aligns four materials modalities -- crystal structure, powder X-ray diffraction (pXRD) simulated from structures, density of states (DOS), and text -- into a unified embedding space using crystal structure as the central physical anchor. The framework induces alignment between modalities never explicitly paired during training, enabling emergent zero-shot cross-modal retrieval as a direct consequence of the shared representation. The learned embedding space organizes materials according to physically meaningful properties without explicit supervision, and retrieval performance improves systematically when modalities are combined at query time. These results demonstrate that treating heterogeneous materials data as complementary projections of a single physical reality, rather than as isolated data sources, is not a practical choice but is consistent with the underlying physics.

</details>


### [76] [Frequency-Domain Multi-Modality Transportation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08475)
*Jiewen Deng,Hangchen Liu,Junchen Li,Boyuan Zhang,Renhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FreMo是一种轻量级的频域多模态交通预测方法，通过解耦模态内频谱精炼和跨模态频率引导协同，在多个真实数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要在时域操作或依赖粗粒度特征融合，难以处理多模态交通数据中不同模态的频谱特性差异以及频率维度上不均匀的交互问题。

Method: FreMo包含两个核心模块：(1) Modality-Wise Frequency Filter (MFF)，自适应精炼每个模态内的频谱成分，增强信息频率并抑制噪声；(2) Frequency-Guided Synergy Integrator (FSI)，基于各模态在各频率上的相对贡献选择性聚合跨模态信息。FreMo支持即插即用，可与通用时间序列骨干网络集成。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，FreMo在多个预测场景中一致优于最先进的基线方法，具有优越的性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: FreMo通过显式利用频域实现自适应和选择性的跨模态协同，为多模态交通预测提供了一种轻量级且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-modality transportation refers to urban systems composed of multiple transportation modes, such as traffic flow and public transit, whose dynamics are coupled by shared temporal patterns. Accurate multi-modality transportation forecasting remains challenging because (1) different modalities exhibit distinct spectral characteristics and (2) interact unevenly across frequencies, whereas most existing methods operate primarily in the time domain or rely on coarse feature fusion. To address these limitations, we propose a lightweight yet effective Frequency-Domain Multi-Modality modeling (FreMo) that explicitly exploits the frequency domain to enable adaptive and selective cross-modality synergy. FreMo disentangles modality-wise spectral refinement from cross-modality synergy and supports plug-and-play integration with general time series backbones. Specifically, FreMo introduces a Modality-Wise Frequency Filter (MFF) to adaptively refine spectral components within each modality, emphasizing informative frequencies while suppressing noise. FreMo further incorporates a Frequency-Guided Synergy Integrator (FSI) that selectively aggregates information across modalities based on their relative contribution at each frequency, facilitating effective cross-modality knowledge sharing while mitigating negative transfer. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that FreMo consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with superior performance and generalization across diverse forecasting scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/beginner-sketch/FreMo.

</details>


### [77] [Ensemble Diversity Optimization for Subjective Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08493)
*Xia Cui,Ziyi Huang,N. R. Abeynayake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出集成多样性优化（EDO）框架，通过可微的联合优化目标学习集成权重、有效基数与校准，利用符号多样性正则化器控制集成坍缩，在四个主观文本分类基准上显著改进概率校准（交叉熵降低40-78%），同时保持竞争性F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主观性NLP任务中标注者之间常存在系统性的不一致，传统模型倾向于坍缩这种不确定性，需要能够表达而非消除不确定性的模型。

Method: 提出EDO框架：通过Gumbel-Softmax松弛实现端到端学习集成组成与大小，引入符号多样性正则化器（在验证集上调参）引导优化方向，集成软F1替代损失、类别加权交叉熵处理不平衡，以及可靠性加权多样性调控集成内部变异性。

Result: 在ArMIS、ConvAbuse、HS-Brexit、MD-Agreement四个基准上，相比Soft-CE、Soft-MD、Top-5 Voting、WEL等方法，EDO在交叉熵上降低40-78%，Brier分数更低，F1保持竞争力，且与标注者分布对齐更好。

Conclusion: 联合优化集成结构与符号多样性正则化器是一种高效、模型无关的方法，适用于监督学习中建模人类主观性。

Abstract: Subjective NLP tasks often exhibit systematic annotator disagreement, requiring models that represent uncertainty rather than collapse it. We introduce Ensemble Diversity Optimization (EDO), a prediction-space framework that jointly optimizes ensemble weights, effective cardinality, and calibration through a unified differentiable objective. EDO learns ensemble composition and size end-to-end via Gumbel-Softmax relaxation and incorporates a signed diversity regularizer, tuned on validation data, to steer optimization toward either preserving or suppressing disagreement. This regularization prevents ensemble collapse and enables controlled navigation of the utility-calibration trade-off. The framework integrates a soft F1 surrogate, class-weighted cross-entropy to address imbalance, and reliability-weighted diversity to regulate intra-ensemble variability. Experiments on four subjective text-classification benchmarks (ArMIS, ConvAbuse, HS-Brexit, MD-Agreement) show that EDO substantially improves probabilistic calibration, reducing cross-entropy (40-78% depending on baseline) and lowering Brier scores relative to Soft-CE, Soft-MD, Top-5 Voting, and WEL, while maintaining competitive F1 and better alignment with annotator distributions. These results demonstrate that jointly optimizing ensemble structure with a signed diversity regularizer provides an efficient, model-agnostic approach for modeling human subjectivity in supervised learning.

</details>


### [78] [Systematic Evaluation of Learning Rate Scheduling Strategies Across Heterogeneous Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08511)
*Hafsa Mateen,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了30种代表性神经网络架构在25种学习率调度策略下的表现，共测试3,938个模型变体。结果表明调度器的选择高度依赖架构，CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts和CyclicLR普遍优于基础衰减策略，最佳配置在CIFAR-10上达到86.45%的top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习率调度策略对神经网络训练至关重要，但手动选择成本高且难以穷举。传统AutoML方法常将调度器视为次要超参数，本文旨在系统性地研究其实际影响。

Method: 从LEMUR数据集中选取30种代表性架构（卷积和Transformer家族），通过自动化源码注入应用9个PyTorch调度器家族中的25种配置，在CIFAR-10上评估共3,938个模型变体。

Result: 最佳配置top-1准确率86.45%，237个变体超过80%；CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts和CyclicLR一致优于基础衰减策略；调度器选择高度依赖架构。

Conclusion: 学习率调度器选择对模型性能有显著影响且与架构高度耦合，实验得到的精度景观可作为调度器选择的原则性参考，相关数据已贡献至LEMUR nn-dataset。

Abstract: Choosing a learning rate scheduling strategy is critical to neural network training, but manual selection is costly and rarely exhaustive. While classical AutoML approaches often treat the scheduler as a secondary hyperparameter, we systematically investigate its impact on classification accuracy across a diverse pool of architectures. We evaluated 30 representative architectures from convolutional and transformer families within the LEMUR neural network dataset. Through automated source-code injection, we applied 25 scheduler configurations across nine PyTorch families, evaluating a total of 3,938 model variants on CIFAR-10. Our best configuration achieved a top-1 accuracy of 86.45%, with 237 variants exceeding 80%. The results show that the choice of scheduler depends heavily on the architecture: CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts and CyclicLR consistently outperform basic decay strategies. The resulting accuracy landscape, contributed to the LEMUR nn-dataset, provides a practical reference for principled scheduler selection.

</details>


### [79] [Stop Guessing When to Stop Testing: Efficient Model Evaluation with Just Enough Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08522)
*Ofir Arviv,Kristjan Greenewald,Yotam Perlitz,Hadar Mulian,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Leshem Choshen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于序贯检验的自适应评估框架，通过动态调整样本量来解决固定基准在模型评估中效率与可靠性之间的矛盾，在Open VLM Leaderboard上实现了80%的计算成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定大小的基准测试因样本量固化而无法灵活适应模型排序、选择、开发测试等不同评估目标对统计功效的差异化需求，导致计算成本过高或可靠性不足。

Method: 将统计学的序贯检验范式引入模型评估，结合针对常见评估需求（如收益递减检测、最小可检测效应量）定制的停止准则，构建自适应评估框架。

Result: 在Open VLM Leaderboard上，该方法在2.5分置信区间宽度允许范围内，相比固定样本量评估实现了80%的计算成本降低，同时保持统计显著性。

Conclusion: 序贯检验的自适应评估框架能有效平衡模型评估中的效率与可靠性，是替代传统固定基准的可行方案。

Abstract: The inherent rigidity of fixed-size benchmarks makes them an inefficient tool for model evaluation. Diverse evaluation objectives, including model ranking, model selection and testing throughout development, demand varying levels of statistical power. The mismatch between fixed sample sizes and these diverse needs results in either excessive computational cost or compromised reliability - a critical concern for model evaluation. To overcome these limitations, we call for adoption of sequential testing in our field. We provide an adaptive evaluation framework, that provides a principled way to navigate the trade-off between efficiency and reliability in model evaluation. Our framework combines the established statistical paradigm of sequential testing with stopping criteria tailored to common evaluation needs such as diminishing returns detection, and minimum detectable effect size. We demonstrate its ability to adaptively manage the efficiency-reliability trade-off on the Open VLM Leaderboard, including, for example, a 80% reduction in computational cost compared to fixed-size evaluation (with a 2.5-point CI width allowance) while maintaining statistical significance.

</details>


### [80] [CAAD: Causality-Aware Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Alignment and Structural Causal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08555)
*Xin Wang,Yunshi Wen,Yanan He,Haotian Xu,Youlan Zhao,Michel Ferreira Cardia Haddad,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAAD框架，通过Granger因果一致性检验和外生变量残差分析进行工业系统异常检测，在多尺度对齐和梯度基矩阵监控下捕获因果关系的动态演化与拓扑结构偏差，在真实工业数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法仅关注表征的时间相似性，忽视了系统故障和潜在异常中内部因果关系的破坏，导致检测精度不足。

Method: 将异常检测重构为通过外生变量持续验证Granger因果一致性；将外生时间序列变量建模为残差，识别外部干预引发的显著偏差；利用多尺度对齐内化系统动态，使用梯度基矩阵监控内部因果关系的破坏。

Result: 在真实工业数据集上的大量实验表明，CAAD实现了高精度异常检测，优于大多数最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过量化动态演化和关系拓扑的因果偏差，CAAD能够捕获微妙的因果偏移，实现精确的异常检测。

Abstract: The operational integrity of complex industrial systems relies on precise anomaly detection and diagnosis. The vast majority of existing methods narrowly focus on capturing temporal similarities of representations, often overlooking the disruption of internal causal relationships, which characterizes system failures and latent anomalies. In this paper, we propose a novel framework (CAAD) that reframes anomaly detection as the continuous verification of Granger causality consistency through exogenous variables.
  Specifically, the CAAD framework models exogenous time-series variables as residuals, identifying anomalies as significant deviations caused by external interventions. The proposed framework leverages multi-scale alignment to internalize system dynamics and utilizes a gradient-based matrix to monitor internal causal relationship breakdowns. By quantifying causal deviations of both dynamic evolution and relational topology, the CAAD is able to capture subtle causal shifts to achieve precise anomaly detection. Extensive experiments on real-world industrial datasets demonstrate that the CAAD achieves high-precision anomaly detection, outperforming most state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [81] [Contravariance Theory: Strong Alignment for Minimal Solutions to Hard Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08561)
*Dan Yamins,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于任何解决足够困难任务的最小深度神经网络，网络表征之间的“弱”对齐（基于仿射映射）保证了“特权轴”的“强”对齐，且这种对齐会沿网络层级向上“拉链式”传播。这形式化了Cao和Yamins的逆变概念，并表明：在足够强的任务下，网络间比较的度量选择不再敏感，且趋同进化几乎是必然的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十五年的NeuroAI研究提出了两个核心问题：如何将深度神经网络模型与大脑进行比较，以及人工网络与真实脑网络之间应预期存在多大程度的趋同进化。本文旨在为这些问题提供理论层面的形式化回答。

Method: 通过数学推导证明：对于任何两个解决足够困难任务的最小DNN，弱对齐（仿射映射下的表征对齐）可推导出强对齐（特权轴的对齐），并且这种对齐从网络末端向上层级传播，形成“拉链”效应。

Result: （i）弱对齐保证强对齐；（ii）对齐沿网络层级向上传播，产生从端到端任务优化中涌现的特权轴；（iii）形式化了Cao和Yamins提出的逆变概念。

Conclusion: 在任务足够强（困难）的条件下，网络间比较的度量选择并不敏感，且趋同进化不可避免。这为NeuroAI中DNN与大脑的比较研究提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: A series of results from the NeuroAI over the past fifteen years have raised core questions both about how to compare Deep Neural Network (DNN) models to the brain, and about how much convergent evolution to expect between artificial networks and real brain networks. Here, we show that for any two minimal DNN solutions to a sufficiently hard task: (i) "weak" alignment of network representations based on affine mappings guarantees "strong" alignment of privileged axes, and (ii) alignment "zippers" up the network hierarchy, causing the emergence of privileged axes from end-to-end task optimization. These results formalize the notion of contravariance from Cao and Yamins [2024], and illustrate important consequences for the theory of NeuroAI: with sufficiently strong tasks, choice of metric for inter-network comparison is not all that sensitive, and that convergent evolution is probably inevitable.

</details>


### [82] [Spectral Stability of Pseudoinverse-Based Extreme Learning Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08581)
*Bich Van Nguyen,Ngoc Anh Khong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从谱角度研究基于伪逆的极限学习机（ELM），揭示了最小奇异值和条件数对输出权重稳定性的影响，比较了SVD与迭代超幂方法的数值鲁棒性，并指出隐藏层矩阵的奇异值结构是ELM稳定性的根本决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ELM虽然训练快速，但其数值稳定性高度依赖于隐藏层矩阵的条件数，现有研究对此缺乏深入的谱分析，本文旨在从奇异值分解的角度系统揭示ELM不稳定的根本原因。

Method: 从谱视角分析伪逆型ELM，推导最小奇异值对输出权重扰动放大的控制作用；通过随机特征解释讨论宽度相关的条件数；对比SVD与迭代超幂方法在不同病态条件下的表现。

Result: SVD方法在病态条件下最可靠，迭代方法对谱特性更敏感；隐藏层矩阵的奇异值结构从根本上决定了ELM的稳定性。

Conclusion: ELM稳定性本质上受隐藏层矩阵奇异值结构支配，SVD伪逆计算是病态条件下最稳健的选择，迭代方法需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Extreme Learning Machine (ELM) computes output weights analytically using the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse. Although this leads to fast training, its numerical stability depends strongly on the conditioning of the hidden layer matrix. This paper studies pseudoinverse-based ELM from a spectral perspective. We show that the smallest singular value governs perturbation amplification in the output weights, while the condition number provides a quantitative measure of hidden-layer instability. We compare SVD-based pseudoinverse computation with iterative hyperpower methods and discuss width-dependent conditioning through a random feature interpretation. Experiments on synthetic matrices and ELM benchmarks show that SVD-based methods remain the most reliable under ill conditioning, while iterative methods are more sensitive to spectral properties. The results suggest that ELM stability is fundamentally governed by the singular value structure of the hidden layer matrix.

</details>


### [83] [Robust Bayesian Decision Making under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08590)
*Haripriya Harikumar,Sammie Katt,Yasir Zubayr Barlas,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种序列式对抗鲁棒的决策感知实验设计方法，通过明确考虑对抗性变量下的最坏情况扰动，构建贝叶斯实验设计准则以追求决策稳定性而非名义最优性，实验结果表明该方法相比传统方法能产生更稳定可靠的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策感知实验设计方法通常假设结果模型正确设定，并隐含依赖最优决策在现实扰动下的稳定性，但实际中实验结果常受隐藏或弱建模效应影响，可能显著改变决策最优性并导致误导性结论，因此需要一种对未知扰动具有鲁棒性的实验设计方法。

Method: 基于贝叶斯决策理论，形式化了对抗鲁棒最优决策，推导了原则性的贝叶斯实验设计准则，该准则明确针对决策稳定性而非名义最优性，通过序列方式获取数据以应对对抗性变量引起的最坏情况变化。

Result: 在合成和真实科学数据集上的实验表明，传统决策感知设计可能快速收敛到高置信度但脆弱的决策，而所提出的鲁棒感知方法在对抗扰动下能产生显著更稳定可靠的决策。

Conclusion: 考虑对抗鲁棒的决策感知实验设计能够有效提升决策在未知扰动下的稳定性和可靠性，为科学实验设计提供了更稳健的框架。

Abstract: Scientific experiments are often designed to maximize information gain, yet in many applications the primary objective is to support reliable downstream decision-making. Existing decision-aware experimental design and active learning methods typically assume well-specified outcome models and implicitly rely on the stability of the optimal decision under real-world perturbations. In practice, however, experimental outcomes are frequently influenced by hidden or weakly modeled effects, which can substantially alter decision optimality and lead to misleading conclusions. We study sequential adversarially robust decision-aware experimental design, where data acquisition has to take into account information gain against plausible worst-case unexpected effects, modeled here as variation in adversarial variables. Building on Bayesian decision theory, we formalize an adversarially robust optimal decision under this setting and derive a principled Bayesian experimental design criterion. The criterion explicitly targets decision stability rather than nominal optimality. Experiments on synthetic and real-world scientific datasets show that conventional decision-aware design can converge rapidly to high confidence yet fragile decisions, while our robustness-aware approach yields decisions that are significantly more stable and reliable under adversarial variation.

</details>


### [84] [Federated Deep Learning for Privacy-Preserving Cardiovascular Disease Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08595)
*Hyunho Mo,Djura Smits,Mahlet A. Birhanu,Maarten J. G. Leening,Daniel Bos,Pim van der Harst,Esther E. Bron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种联邦深度学习方法，用于在保护隐私的前提下整合两个异质性人群队列（Lifelines和Rotterdam Study）进行心血管疾病风险预测。联邦学习训练的深度生存模型在预测性能上优于本地训练模型，Rotterdam Study的C-statistic从0.728提升至0.739，Lifelines从0.783提升至0.787，证明了跨异质性队列的联邦学习能改善预测效果并保护患者数据隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病风险预测模型通常依赖单中心或集中式数据，跨机构扩展受限于隐私法规和数据共享约束。联邦学习虽能解决隐私问题，但在医疗领域面临数据集规模、人群特征和结局定义异质性的挑战。本研究旨在探索联邦深度学习能否在保护隐私的同时，整合异质性队列以提升心血管疾病风险预测性能。

Method: 采用联邦深度学习方法，整合两个异质性人群队列：Lifelines（148,230名参与者，自我报告结局）和Rotterdam Study（10,155名参与者，临床记录结局）。训练深度生存模型，通过联邦学习协同优化，并在Rotterdam Study（因随访完整）上主要评估性能，比较联邦学习与本地训练模型的C-statistic。

Result: 联邦学习训练的模型在两项队列中均取得更高预测性能：Rotterdam Study的C-statistic从0.728（95% CI: 0.717-0.739）提升至0.739（95% CI: 0.728-0.749）；Lifelines的C-statistic从0.783（95% CI: 0.775-0.791）提升至0.787（95% CI: 0.780-0.792）。

Conclusion: 联邦深度学习能够跨异质性人群队列改善心血管疾病风险预测，同时保护个体层面患者数据的隐私。

Abstract: Cardiovascular disease risk prediction models often rely on data from a single institution or centrally pooled datasets. Extending these models across institutions could be limited by privacy regulations and constraints on sharing patient-level data. Federated learning enables collaborative model development without transferring sensitive patient data, but its application in healthcare remains challenging because datasets often differ in size, population characteristics, and outcome definitions. In this study, we present a federated deep learning approach for privacy-preserving cardiovascular disease risk prediction that integrates two population-based cohorts with different characteristics: Lifelines, including 148,230 participants meeting the study inclusion criteria with self-reported outcomes, and the Rotterdam Study, including a smaller cohort of 10,155 participants with digitally linked clinical outcomes. Model performance was primarily evaluated on the Rotterdam Study because of its complete follow-up. Deep survival models trained using federated learning achieved higher predictive performance than models trained locally without federation. For the Rotterdam Study, the C-statistic increased from 0.728 (95% CI: 0.717-0.739) to 0.739 (95% CI: 0.728-0.749). For Lifelines, the C-statistic increased from 0.783 (95% CI: 0.775-0.791) to 0.787 (95% CI: 0.780-0.792). These findings suggest that federated deep learning across heterogeneous cohorts can improve cardiovascular disease risk prediction while preserving the privacy of individual-level patient data.

</details>


### [85] [Steering Neural Network Training through Interpretable Constraints Based on Partial Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08641)
*Yann Claes,Pierre Geurts,Vân Anh Huynh-Thu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于偏依赖的神经网络引导方法，通过将模型对特定特征的平均响应与先验功能知识对齐，在回归问题（包括动力系统预测）上比无约束模型表现更好、数据效率更高，且解释与用户知识一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究多关注解释技术本身，缺乏对解释质量的评估，更少研究如何调整模型以产生符合先验知识的解释（即解释引导学习），且大多数方法集中于分类问题并假设已知重要特征/区域。

Method: 提出一种基于偏依赖的解释引导学习方法，通过约束神经网络的偏依赖函数，使模型对特定输入特征的平均响应与用户提供的领域功能知识对齐，从而在训练过程中引导模型学习。

Result: 在多个回归问题（包括动力系统预测）上，该方法训练的模型在性能和数据效率上均优于无约束模型；且前者得到的解释与用户先验知识一致，而后者得到的解释则不匹配。

Conclusion: 基于偏依赖的解释引导学习能有效将领域知识注入神经网络训练，提升模型性能、数据效率及解释的可信度，尤其适用于回归任务。

Abstract: Over the last few years, there has been an increased interest in making machine learning models more interpretable. Although a great deal of effort goes into developing techniques for interpreting the interactions learned by a given model, fewer studies focus on assessing the quality of such explanations. Even fewer focus on how to adjust the model to produce explanations faithful to prior knowledge, a process known as explanation-guided learning. Furthermore, most approaches in this area focus on classification problems and usually assume prior knowledge about which input features or regions are most important. In this work, we introduce a new approach to steering neural networks based on partial dependence, such that their average response to certain features aligns with specific functional domain knowledge about the problem. We empirically demonstrate on a range of regression problems, including dynamical systems forecasting, that models whose training has been controlled using our method perform better than unconstrained models and are more data-efficient. Moreover, we highlight that interpretations obtained from the former actually align with the user-provided knowledge, whereas those obtained from the latter do not.

</details>


### [86] [BiSCo-LLM: Lookup-Free Binary Spherical Coding for Extreme Low-Bit Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08643)
*Yuantian Shao,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Chuangyi Li,Yuanteng Chen,Pengcheng Xie,Yiwu Yao,Zhihui Wei,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiSCo-LLM提出了一种无码本的二元球面编码框架，用于极端低比特（接近2比特/权重）的大语言模型权重压缩，通过球面二值化、残差BSQ编码和类别恢复蒸馏，在不依赖显式码本的情况下实现高效压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低比特压缩方法存在瓶颈：标量/分组量化在接近2比特/权重时表示能力不足；向量量化虽提供更丰富的表示，但需要显式码本、索引查找和额外存储。目标是在极端低比特下实现高效压缩且避免码本开销。

Method: 1) 将局部权重块映射到单位超球面并二值化为紧凑球面编码，主载荷为位打包的符号流而非显式VQ质心；2) 残差BSQ阶段编码基球面编解码器留下的重建误差，提供无存储码本的率失真路径；3) 替换每个Transformer模块类别后进行类别恢复蒸馏，减少局部重建与组装模型行为之间的差异；4) 使用8位保护通道路径作为敏感通道的辅助稳定机制，与BSQ载荷分开计算。

Result: 存储预算包括二元码、神经解码器、保护通道载荷、LoRA适配器和元数据。在接近2比特/权重的极端低比特下实现了有效的权重压缩。

Conclusion: BiSCo-LLM通过无码本二元球面编码框架，在极端低比特LLM权重压缩中达到了比现有方法更优的率失真性能，避免了传统VQ的码本存储开销。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly constrained by memory capacity, weight bandwidth, and checkpoint storage during deployment. Existing low-bit compression methods mainly follow two directions. Scalar or group-wise quantization is simple and compatible with efficient low-precision kernels, but its representation capacity becomes limited when the target budget approaches 2 bits per weight. Vector-quantized weight compression provides a richer block-level representation, but usually introduces explicit codebooks, index lookup, and additional storage accounting. This paper presents BiSCo-LLM, a codebook-free binary spherical coding framework for extreme low-bit LLM weight compression. The core pipeline is built on three components. First, local weight chunks are mapped onto a unit hypersphere and binarized into compact spherical codes, so that the main payload is a bit-packed sign stream rather than explicit VQ centroids. Second, a residual BSQ stage encodes the reconstruction error left by the base spherical codec, providing an explicit rate-distortion path without stored codebooks. Third, category-wise recovery distillation is performed after replacing each Transformer module category, reducing the mismatch between local weight reconstruction and assembled model behavior. A small 8-bit protected-channel path is used as an auxiliary stabilization mechanism for sensitive channels and is counted separately from the BSQ payload. The reported storage budget includes binary codes, neural decoders, protected-channel payloads, LoRA adapters, and metadata.

</details>


### [87] [Multi-Modal, Multi-Environment Machine Teaching for Robust Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08647)
*Ali Larian,Qian Lin,Chang Zong Wu,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究逆强化学习中如何通过多环境、多模态反馈教学学习鲁棒的奖励函数，提出了一种分层机器教学算法，先贪婪选择互补约束的环境，再在环境内策略性查询低成本反馈，实验证明该方法在泛化性和低后悔值上优于统一教学基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IRL的最优教学分析局限于单一环境、仅示范的设置，未探索异构反馈模态和环境动态如何联合约束能跨环境泛化的奖励函数。单一MDP中的示范会纠缠奖励信息与特定环境结构，导致部署到新环境时奖励泛化失败。

Method: 首先理论分析不同反馈模态对奖励的约束能力，证明在无限数据下比较型反馈施加的全局约束严格强于其他模态。然后提出分层机器教学算法：第一层贪婪选择能暴露互补奖励约束的信息性环境，第二层在选定环境内策略性查询低成本反馈。

Result: 在相同反馈预算下，该方法相比统一教学基线实现了显著更低的后悔值和更强的留出环境泛化能力，验证了多环境、多模态教学对学习动态鲁棒奖励函数的重要性。

Conclusion: 跨环境的、多模态的教学策略对于学习能够泛化到新动态环境的奖励函数至关重要，分层教学框架能有效提升IRL的鲁棒性和样本效率。

Abstract: As autonomous agents are increasingly deployed across diverse operational contexts, aligning their behavior with human intent demands reward functions that remain robust to such changes rather than overfitting to any single environment. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a principled way to infer such objectives from human feedback. However, existing analyses of optimal teaching approaches for IRL focus on single-environment, demonstration-only settings, leaving underexplored how heterogeneous feedback modalities and environment dynamics jointly constrain reward functions that generalize across multiple environments. Because demonstrations in one MDP entangle reward information with that environments specific structure, the resulting rewards frequently fail to generalize when the agent is deployed in a new setting. We first analyze how different feedback modalities constrain rewards, showing that, in the unlimited-data regime, comparisons impose strictly stronger global constraints than other modalities. Beyond this theoretical analysis, we introduce a hierarchical machine teaching algorithm for reward learning that operates across multiple MDPs. The algorithm first greedily selects informative environments that expose complementary reward constraints, then strategically queries low-cost feedback within those environments. Empirically, our method achieves substantially lower regret and stronger generalization to held-out environments than uniform teaching baselines under identical feedback budgets, demonstrating the importance of multi-environment, multi-modal teaching for learning dynamics-robust reward functions.

</details>


### [88] [Secure Decentralized Federated Learning via Gossip and Virtual Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08651)
*Amirhossein Taherpour,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出gspDAG-FL，一种去中心化联邦学习框架，利用与模型传播相同的gossip历史构建拓扑有向无环图（DAG），通过Hashgraph式虚拟投票实现共识，无需中心化协调，同时支持拜占庭和懒惰节点的检测，在MNIST和Penn Treebank实验上验证了其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化联邦学习（DFL）基于gossip的方法缺乏最终性和对拜占庭/懒惰节点的鲁棒性，而基于账本的FL虽提升了可审计性但区块链等机制会引入全局协调开销，与DFL的局部性相矛盾。

Method: 节点仅与邻居交换模型负载，全节点收集事件证书和接收者认可的gossip证明，重构紧凑的拓扑DAG，执行Hashgraph式虚拟投票并生成紧凑的全节点证书。最终性基于唯一的模型-来源元组而非相同参数状态。结合负载验证、认可证明验证和私有语义审计来提高鲁棒性。

Result: 在MNIST分类和Penn Treebank语言建模上，使用公平的保留验证/审计数据，网络规模N≤100，gspDAG-FL实现了接近基于验证的账本FL的学习质量，同时减少了协调瓶颈、提高了吞吐量，并在混合拜占庭和懒惰参与下保持高无效来源检测率。

Conclusion: gspDAG-FL通过从gossip历史中导出共识，实现了安全、高效、可审计的去中心化联邦学习，在保持局部性的同时提供了最终性和鲁棒性保障。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) removes the central server by letting nodes exchange model updates through peer-to-peer gossip, but existing gossip-based methods often lack provenance finality and resilience to Byzantine or lazy participants. Ledger-assisted federated learning (FL) improves auditability, yet blockchains, shards, or settlement committees can reintroduce global coordination costs that conflict with DFL locality. This paper proposes \emph{gspDAG-FL}, a secure DFL framework that derives consensus from the same gossip history used to disseminate models. Nodes exchange model payloads only with neighbors, while full nodes collect event certificates and receiver-endorsed accepted gossip proofs, reconstruct a compact Topology directed acyclic graph (DAG), and run Hashgraph-style virtual voting followed by compact full-node certificates. Finality is over unique model-origin tuples, not identical local parameter states. To improve resilience, gspDAG-FL combines payload validation, accepted-proof validation, and private semantic audit before aggregation. We formalize the adversarial setting, prove safety and conditional liveness of the control plane, and give a convergence guarantee for certified perturbed gossip under time-varying effective mixing. Experiments on MNIST classification and Penn Treebank language modeling, using fair held-out validation/audit data and networks up to \(N=100\), show that gspDAG-FL achieves learning quality close to validation-based ledger FL while reducing coordination bottlenecks, improving throughput, and maintaining high invalid-origin detection under mixed Byzantine and lazy participation.

</details>


### [89] [EdgeRefine: Privacy-Utility Balance for Graphs via Jaccard Sampling under Edge Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08659)
*Wenxiu Ding,Muzhi Liu,Zheng Yan,Mingjun Wang,Yifan Zhao,Qiao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EdgeRefine通过自适应边精炼框架，利用Jaccard相似度估计边存在概率并排序，基于隐私预算ε确定真假边比例、分离采样，并用采样率k控制总边数，在节点分类和图表征任务中实现了优于现有隐私保护方法的精度-隐私权衡，同时对图重构攻击具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在隐私敏感领域的应用受限，因为图结构可能泄露敏感的边信息。现有的边级差分隐私方法通过向邻接矩阵注入噪声来混淆边存在性，但强隐私需要更多噪声，导致效用下降，使得隐私-效用平衡成为实际应用的主要障碍。

Method: 提出EdgeRefine局部差分隐私框架：1) 用Jaccard相似度估计边存在概率并排序；2) 隐私预算ε决定真/假边比例；3) 基于概率排序分离采样真边和假边；4) 用额外采样率k控制总边数，保证图的稀疏性和可靠性。

Result: 在ε=2.5时，节点分类准确率比SOTA基线：ACM(GAT)提升17.8%，Cora(GCN)提升19.7%。图分类平均精度仅下降约5%。图重构攻击下，相对绝对误差在所有隐私预算下均>1，平均Cora 1.962、AMAP 1.472，表现出强抗泄露能力。

Conclusion: EdgeRefine通过自适应边精炼有效改善了隐私-效用权衡，在多种GNN架构和数据集上达到了接近无噪声基线的精度，显著优于现有隐私保护方法，且对图重构攻击具有强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have shown considerable success in learning from graph-structured data, but their use in privacy-sensitive areas remains difficult because graph structure can leak sensitive link information. To satisfy edge-level differential privacy, a common approach is to inject noise into all elements of the graph's adjacency matrix, thereby obfuscating the existence of any single edge. However, stronger privacy requires more noise, and excessive noise reduces utility, making the privacy-utility balance a major barrier to practical privacy-preserving graph learning.
  To address this issue, we propose EdgeRefine, a local differential privacy framework that improves this trade-off through adaptive edge refinement. EdgeRefine first estimates edge-existence probabilities using Jaccard similarity and ranks edges for noisy edge removal. To ensure the sparsity and reliability of the final graph, it uses the privacy budget $ε$ to determine the ratio of true to false edges, samples them separately based on this probability ranking, and controls the total number of edges with a separate sampling rate $k$. Extensive experiments show that EdgeRefine achieves accuracy comparable to the noise-free baseline and substantially outperforms other privacy-preserving methods across datasets and GNN architectures. Under privacy budget $ε= 2.5$, EdgeRefine improves node classification accuracy over state-of-the-art baselines by 17.8\% on ACM under GAT and 19.7\% on Cora under GCN. In graph classification, it achieves an average accuracy degradation of around 5\% compared to the noise-free baseline. Under graph reconstruction attacks, EdgeRefine maintains relative absolute error levels above 1 across all privacy budgets, averaging 1.962 on Cora and 1.472 on AMAP, indicating strong resilience against privacy leakage.

</details>


### [90] [Resample or Reroute? Budget-Aware Test-Time Model Selection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08665)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预算感知的测试时模型选择策略——在线重采样或重路由（RoR），在给定每查询预算和不完美验证器条件下，动态分配预算于重采样和重路由之间以最大化期望正确性。在11个开源模型、4个基准上的实验表明，RoR相比单路由、单次提交路由、预算感知Best-of-K、级联和随机分配基线，实现了更优的成本-质量Pareto前沿，增益在最具异质性的基准上最大，且效果受验证器质量门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由系统在单次提交路由器与逐实例oracle之间存在性能差距，且测试时重采样虽能恢复部分性能，但其保证依赖于有正确标签的理想化oracle和无约束预算，不符合实际部署条件。此前工作未将重采样已提交模型和重路由到替代模型视为同一成本预算下的竞争性使用方式。

Method: 提出预算感知测试时模型选择框架，给定每查询预算和不完美验证器，在重采样（resampling）与重路由（rerouting）之间分配预算单位。设计在线重采样或重路由（RoR）分配策略，由每单位成本的边际期望正确性驱动，利用选择与采样之间的可恢复性不对称性。在新生成的多轮次正确性张量上基于11个开放权重模型和4个难度不同的基准进行回放实验。

Result: RoR策略在测试的模型池中相比单路由、单次提交路由器、预算感知Best-of-K、级联和随机分配基线，实现了更优的成本-质量Pareto前沿；在最具异质性的基准上增益最大；消融实验表明增益受验证器质量门控，随验证器质量下降而缩小；在供应商价格向量和无标签一致性验证器下的鲁棒性回放实验划定了结论的适用范围。

Conclusion: 预算感知的在线重采样或重路由策略能有效利用有限预算在多个LLM间进行测试时模型选择，显著优于现有基线，但效果依赖于验证器质量。

Abstract: Routing among large language models (LLMs) trades response quality against serving cost, motivated by the reported gap between deployed routers and a per-instance oracle. Recent analysis shows that test-time resampling can recover per-instance selection headroom that no single-commit router captures; however, that guarantee holds only under an idealized oracle equipped with correctness labels and an unconstrained budget, neither of which a deployed system has. To the best of our knowledge, no previous work treats resampling the committed model and rerouting to an alternative model as competing uses of a single per-query cost budget. Therefore, this work formulates budget-aware test-time model selection: given a per-query budget and an imperfect verifier, allocate each unit of budget between resampling and rerouting so that expected correctness is maximized. An online resample-or-reroute (RoR) allocation policy driven by estimated marginal correctness per unit cost is proposed, and its behavior is grounded in the recoverability asymmetry between selection and sampling. Replay experiments on newly regenerated multi-draw correctness tensors from an eleven-model open-weight pool over four benchmarks of differing difficulty show that the proposed RoR policy attains a favorable cost-quality Pareto front relative to single-route, one-commit-router, budget-aware best-of-K, cascade, and random-allocation baselines for the tested pools, with the largest gains on the most heterogeneous benchmark; an ablation further shows the gains are verifier-gated, shrinking as verifier quality degrades, and robustness replays under a provider price vector and a label-free agreement verifier delineate where the conclusions carry over.

</details>


### [91] [A Practical Investigation of Training-free Relaxed Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08690)
*Guoxuan Xia,Luka Ribar,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文对无训练松弛推测解码技术进行了系统性研究，统一了现有方法框架，并在当代环境下进行基准测试。主要发现：松弛方法需要大量能力评估，且多数方法依赖高质量的草案模型，不适合轻量级专用多token预测草案器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准推测解码是无损的，但近期研究表明放松这一严格保证可以带来进一步加速、可控的能力-速度权衡甚至能力提升。作者希望实践性地研究无训练松弛推测解码技术，为从业者提供指导意见。

Method: 统一现有松弛推测解码方法至共享框架，在当代环境下进行基准测试，系统性地比较不同松弛方法的性能表现。

Result: 松弛方法不能像无损推测解码那样无需大量能力评估；多数松弛方法要求草案器本身是好的语言模型，不适合轻量级专用多token预测草案器。

Conclusion: 从业者应注意：松弛推测解码需要额外的能力评估成本，且其适用性受限于草案器的质量要求。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates sampling from an autoregressive LLM by using a faster auxiliary model to draft tokens which are then verified in parallel by the LLM. Standard speculative decoding is lossless: its rejection and resampling steps exactly preserve the LLM's sampling distribution. Recent work argues that relaxing this strict guarantee can yield further speed-ups, controlled capability-speed trade-offs, or even capability gains. We practically investigate training-free relaxed speculative decoding techniques, unify existing approaches within a shared framework, benchmark them on contemporary settings, and distil takeaways and empirical findings for practitioners. Important takeaways include: relaxation can require considerable capability evaluation unlike lossless speculative decoding, and many relaxed approaches rely on a drafter that is a good language model, making them unsuited for lightweight dedicated multi-token-prediction drafters.

</details>


### [92] [MPFlow: Learning Budgeted Max-Flow Optimization on the Lightning Network with Deep Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08703)
*Harrison Rush,Vincent Davis,Simone Antonelli,Vikash Singh,Jesse Shrader,Emanuele Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究比特币闪电网络中流动性放置问题，提出基于图强化学习的预算约束优化方法，通过最大化s-t最大流来选择k条边添加，使用消息传递策略网络与PPO算法，并在实际闪电网络快照上验证了有效性，已部署生产环境执行4640次通道开放决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在比特币闪电网络中，给定固定预算，节点应如何选择开通通道以最大化其路由容量，这是一个预算约束下的组合优化问题，现有方法缺乏理论坚实的路由容量度量。

Method: 将问题建模为图上的预算约束组合优化问题，选择k条边添加以最大化s-t最大流；设计结合消息传递策略网络、PPO算法和动作掩蔽的轻量级强化学习智能体；采用中心节点排除课程训练策略，迫使智能体学习容量感知放置而非简单依附中心节点。

Result: 在真实闪电网络快照上的大量实验中，该方法在最大流目标上始终优于强启发式基线，并在多个随机种子和未见图上保持优势。生产部署中执行了4640次通道开放决策，累计分配267.3 BTC（超1600万美元），覆盖30个管理节点。

Conclusion: 所提出的图强化学习方法能够有效学习容量感知的流动性放置策略，优于传统启发式方法，并已在生产环境中验证了其实际价值。

Abstract: We address liquidity placement in the Bitcoin Lightning Network (LN): given a fixed budget, which channels should a node open to maximize its routing capacity? We cast this as a budget-constrained combinatorial optimization problem on graphs, selecting $k$ edge additions that maximize $s$--$t$ max-flow, a theory-grounded measure of routing capacity, and solve it with graph reinforcement learning. Our lightweight agent combines a message-passing policy network with proximal policy optimization (PPO) and action masking, and is trained under a hub-exclusion curriculum: the network's top hubs are removed from training subgraphs, forcing the policy to learn capacity-aware placement rather than hub attachment. In extensive experiments on real Lightning Network snapshots, our method consistently outperforms strong heuristic baselines on the max-flow objective across multiple seeds and unseen graphs. The agent has been deployed in production for peer recommendations, executing 4640 channel-open decisions that cumulatively allocate 267.3 BTC over $16 million across 30 managed nodes.

</details>


### [93] [Deep Learning for Joint Narrowband Interference Cancellation and Soft Demodulation in OFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08717)
*Emmanouil Kavvousanos,Francky Catthoor,Vassilis Paliouras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的深度学习框架，包含NBI-CNet（物理信息卷积网络，单次前向传播估计并消除多音干扰）和LLR-CNet（结构白化器，将非高斯残差映射为校准软度量），联合解决OFDM系统中窄带干扰抑制与鲁棒软解调问题，消除传统方法的错误平层，计算复杂度降低60%，在严重干扰下接近最优迭代基线（0.2-0.5dB差距），在轻度干扰下获得超过3dB编码增益，且支持跨FFT尺寸的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统压缩感知NBI抑制方法存在高顺序延迟，且残留结构化非高斯残差导致LLR不可靠、译码器饱和及严重错误平层，高斯解映射器失效。需要解决NBI抑制与软解调之间的流水线不匹配问题。

Method: 提出统一深度学习框架：(1) NBI-CNet：物理信息卷积架构，单次前向传播估计NBI参数并消除多音干扰，无需先验干扰数，计算复杂度较EOMP-IDS降低60%；(2) LLR-CNet：结构白化器，将非高斯后抑制残差映射为校准良好的软度量。

Result: 严重干扰(SIR=-10dB)下，在BLER=10^{-4}时与最优迭代基线仅差0.2-0.5dB SNR；轻度干扰(SIR=10dB, Q=12)下，避免传统贪婪算法误减有效数据分量，获得超过3dB编码增益；消除由干扰估计误差触发的2×10^{-4}错误平层；尺度不变设计支持跨任意FFT尺寸的鲁棒泛化。

Conclusion: 联合深度学习框架有效消除了传统基线中固有的错误平层，在密集干扰网格下表现出色，具有显著的计算效率和泛化能力，为OFDM系统的NBI抑制与软解调提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Narrowband interference (NBI) severely degrades orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) systems by corrupting subcarriers and rendering classical soft demodulation ineffective. Conventional compressed-sensing (CS) mitigation exhibits high sequential latency and leaves structured, non-Gaussian residuals that cause log-likelihood ratio (LLR) unreliability, decoder saturation, and severe error floors when employing classical Gaussian demappers. We resolve this pipeline mismatch using a unified deep learning framework for joint NBI cancellation and robust soft demodulation. First, NBI-CNet employs a physics-informed convolutional architecture to estimate NBI parameters and remove multi-tone interference in a single forward pass. Without requiring prior knowledge of the active interferer count, NBI-CNet reduces computational complexity by up to 60% ($N{=}2048, Q{=}64$) compared to the state-of-the-art EOMP-IDS algorithm. Second, LLR-CNet acts as a structural whitener by mapping non-Gaussian post-mitigation residuals onto well-calibrated soft metrics. Simulations demonstrate that this joint framework eliminates the error floors inherent to traditional baselines across dense grids. Under severe interference ($\text{SIR}{=}{-}10$ dB), the pipeline operates within a $0.2$ to $0.5$ dB SNR margin of the optimal iterative baseline at a target block error rate (BLER) of $10^{-4}$. Under mild interference ($\text{SIR}{=}10$ dB) with heavy spectral overlap ($Q{=}12$), where classical greedy algorithms erroneously subtract valid data components and corrupt the payload, NBI-CNet avoids signal-peak confusion to deliver a coding gain exceeding $3$ dB. Finally, the architecture circumvents the $2{\times}10^{-4}$ error floor triggered by interferer-estimation errors, while its scale-invariant design enables robust generalization across arbitrary FFT sizes without retraining.

</details>


### [94] [Latent Memory Palace: Reasoning for Control as Autoregressive Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08724)
*Chuning Zhu,Eva Xu,Jose Barreiros,Krishnan Srinivasan,Paarth Shah,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Memory Palace (LMP)方法，将推理形式化为自回归潜变量分布上的变分推断，通过潜空间强化学习优化变分下界，得到LMP-π策略（在模拟和真实环境中表现优异且具备自适应测试时计算分配能力）和LMP-tok变长动作分词器（提升下游自回归策略性能），为控制策略的潜在推理提供了变分推断视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类决策具有高度灵活性——有些动作立即执行，有些需要更长时间的 deliberation。语言模型已展现出类似的适应性"推理"能力，但将其迁移到连续控制策略具有挑战性，因为直接在语言空间中推理可能缺乏空间理解和精确运动的粒度。

Method: 将推理形式化为具有自回归潜变量分布的变分推断，推导潜空间强化学习技术以可追踪地优化其变分下界。

Result: LMP-π策略在仿真和真实世界领域取得强经验性能，同时展现出可解释、自适应的测试时计算分配能力；LMP-tok变长动作分词器显著提升下游自回归策略的性能。

Conclusion: 这些结果通过变分推断的视角，为控制策略的潜在推理提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Human decision-making is highly flexible -- some actions are taken immediately; others require longer deliberation. Language models have exhibited a similar capacity for adaptive "reasoning." However, transferring this capability to continuous control policies has been challenging, as directly reasoning in language space may lack the granularity for spatial understanding and precise motions. In this work, we show that reasoning for control policies can emerge by organizing information in an autoregressive latent space reminiscent of a memory palace, where retrieval is iterative and adaptive. Our method, Latent Memory Palace (LMP), formulates reasoning as variational inference with an autoregressive latent distribution. We derive a latent-space reinforcement learning technique to tractably optimize its variational lower bound. The resulting policy, LMP-$π$, achieves strong empirical performance in simulation and real-world domains while exhibiting interpretable, adaptive allocation of test-time compute. We further show that the same framework yields a variable-length action tokenizer, LMP-$\texttt{tok}$, which significantly improves the performance of downstream autoregressive policies. Together, these results present a new perspective on latent reasoning for control through the lens of variational inference.

</details>


### [95] [Super Weights in LLMs and the Failure of Selective Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08733)
*Shreyas Subramanian,Adewale Akinfaderin,Akarsha Sehwag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现"超级权重"（Super Weights）对模型性能的影响并非普遍适用，且单独训练这些参数会导致性能崩溃至随机猜测水平，而结构化低秩更新（如LoRA）在完整层上的微调才有效，表明参数重要性不等同于参数可训练性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究指出存在"超级权重"——单个参数的移除会使模型性能下降数个数量级。作者希望验证这一现象的普适性，并探究是否可以通过针对性训练这些超级权重来提升微调效率。

Method: 在OLMo-1B和OLMo-7B模型上进行实验：(1) 孤立训练超级权重（100到8,192个参数）；(2) 扩大训练到局部邻域（最多36K参数）；(3) 在相同down_proj层中随机选取相同数量的参数进行训练；(4) 使用vanilla LoRA（仅0.16%参数）进行全层结构化低秩更新；(5) 将LoRA更新约束在超级权重坐标位置进行10次随机种子消融实验。

Result: 孤立训练超级权重导致准确率降至随机猜测水平；扩大邻域训练也无改善。随机位置训练反而提升性能。Vanilla LoRA仅用0.16%参数即可成功微调。将LoRA约束在超级权重位置效果与随机位置无统计显著差异。

Conclusion: 参数重要性（对模型性能的影响）不等于参数可训练性（能否通过孤立训练有效学习）。有效的微调依赖于对完整层进行结构化分解更新，而非针对单个重要参数。

Abstract: Recent work identified Super Weights, individual parameters whose removal degrades model performance by orders of magnitude. We show that this degradation due to pruning Super Weights does not universally apply to all LLMs. Furthermore, if these parameters are so important, Super Weight-aware training should be effective. We show the opposite. Training Super Weights in isolation (100 to 8,192 parameters) drops accuracy to random-guessing levels on both OLMo-1B and OLMo-7B, and expanding to local neighborhoods of up to 36K parameters provides no improvement. The failure is specific to Super Weight coordinates: training an equal number of randomly chosen positions in the same down_proj layers instead improves over the baseline, so the collapse comes from targeting Super Weights, not from sparsity itself. Vanilla LoRA, updating every position in attention weight matrices through low-rank structure, succeeds with only 0.16% of parameters, and applying the same low-rank update to down_proj succeeds as well. A 10-seed ablation confirms that constraining LoRA updates at positions corresponding to Super Weight coordinates yields statistically indistinguishable results. These findings establish that parameter importance does not imply parameter trainability in isolation, and that effective fine-tuning relies on structured decompositions over entire layers rather than targeting individually important weights.

</details>


### [96] [Dimensionality Reduction Meets Network Science: Sensemaking on UMAP's kNN Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08746)
*Duen Horng Chau,Donghao Ren,Fred Hohman,Dominik Moritz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了UMAP内部构建的k近邻图在数据探索中的巨大潜力，通过在其上应用PageRank、k-core分解和聚类系数等标准图算法，可以识别代表性数据点、发现密集核心与稀疏外围区域以及检测紧密邻域，在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上的实验表明这些图分析方法与专用方法相比具有竞争力或互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UMAP工作流过度关注其低维嵌入结果，忽略了内部构建的k近邻图所包含的丰富数据流形信息，因此作者希望挖掘该内部图表示在数据理解中的潜在价值。

Method: 在UMAP内部构建的k近邻图上应用三种标准图算法：(1) PageRank用于识别代表性数据点，(2) k-core分解用于发现密集核心区域与稀疏外围区域，(3) 聚类系数用于检测具有高度相似数据点的紧密邻域。在MNIST和Fashion MNIST数据集上进行定量和定性评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，这些基于图的分析方法不仅实用，而且与专用方法（如用于示例选择的k-medoids、用于密度聚类的HDBSCAN）相比具有竞争力或互补性。

Conclusion: UMAP内部构建的k近邻图蕴含未被充分利用的流形结构信息，通过标准图算法可增强数据理解，无需额外计算开销，为高维数据探索提供了一种新的有效途径。

Abstract: While UMAP is widely used for exploring high-dimensional data, typical workflows focus on its lower-dimensional embedding, largely overlooking the rich k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) graph that UMAP constructs internally. This graph encodes the data manifold in its original high-dimensional space, before the distortion that UMAP's 2D projection introduces. We demonstrate the untapped potential of this internal representation, showing how standard graph algorithms applied to this graph enhance data sensemaking: (1) PageRank identifies representative data points, (2) k-core decomposition reveals dense core regions versus sparse periphery, and (3) clustering coefficient detects tight-knit neighborhoods with highly-similar data points. Through quantitative and qualitative evaluation on MNIST and Fashion MNIST, we show that these graph-based analyses are not only practical but also competitive with or complementary to purpose-built methods (e.g., k-medoids for exemplar selection, HDBSCAN for density-based clustering).

</details>


### [97] [SLORR: Simple and Efficient In-Training Low-Rank Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08754)
*David González-Martínez,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLORR是一种简单、无状态、保持架构不变的低秩正则化框架，基于Hoyer稀疏度量和核范数，在前向和反向传播中使用GPU友好的近似方法，在ImageNet-1K和LLM预训练中有效提升模型可压缩性，且训练开销低（<8%和<1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练时低秩正则化方法存在以下局限：需要对大型权重矩阵进行SVD分解、修改模型架构引入额外可训练参数、或依赖有状态的缓存量。SLORR旨在克服这些限制，提供一种简单高效的替代方案。

Method: SLORR框架基于Hoyer稀疏度量和核范数两种变体，直接对原始权重矩阵进行正则化，采用GPU友好的近似计算前向和反向传播，并提供近似保证。无需SVD、不修改架构、无需状态缓存。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上（ResNet-50, ViT-B/16, ViT-L/16短周期继续训练，ResNet-18预训练）训练开销<8%；在LLM预训练（135M和560M规模）中，SLORR训练的压缩模型性能显著优于未正则化模型，平均训练开销<1%。

Conclusion: SLORR是一种高效的低秩正则化方法，在图像分类和大语言模型预训练场景下均能以极低开销诱导模型可压缩性，保持架构不变且无需复杂计算。

Abstract: Low-rank factorization is widely used to compress neural networks, but modern models are often not naturally amenable to aggressive factorization without significant accuracy loss. Existing training-time low-rank regularizers can improve compressibility, but they often require SVDs of large weight matrices, modify the model architecture (introducing additional trainable parameters), or rely on stateful cached quantities. To address these limitations, we introduce SLORR, a simple, stateless, and architecture-preserving framework for in-training low-rank regularization, instantiated with two main variants based on the Hoyer sparsity metric and the nuclear norm. SLORR directly regularizes the original weight matrices using GPU-friendly approximations for the forward and backward passes of the regularizers, for which we provide approximation guarantees. We first evaluate SLORR on ImageNet-1K across short-horizon continued training of ResNet-50, ViT-B/16, and ViT-L/16, and pretraining of ResNet-18, where SLORR induces compressibility while introducing less than 8% training overhead. We further evaluate SLORR-Hoyer in LLM pretraining at 135M and 560M scales: SLORR-trained compressed models preserve performance substantially better than unregularized models while adding less than 1% average training overhead.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Comment on 'Quantum Monge-Kantorovich Problem and Transport Distance between Density Matrices'](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07764)
*Tomasz Miller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文否证了Friedland等人关于量子p-Wasserstein距离的猜想，证明其对于特定量子成本矩阵及其邻域并非真正的距离（三角不等式不成立）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Friedland等人提出了一种基于量子成本矩阵和量子耦合的量子p-Wasserstein距离的类比，并猜想该量在特定成本矩阵及其邻域内是真正的距离。作者旨在检验并否证这些猜想。

Method: 通过构造一个明确的三态族，直接验证三角不等式在该情况下的失败，从而反证猜想的正确性。

Result: 成功构造了一个反例族，其中三角不等式不成立，表明该量在声称的条件下并非真正的距离。

Conclusion: Friedland等人的猜想被证伪——所提出的量子p-Wasserstein距离在指定成本矩阵及其邻域内仅是一种半距离，而非真正的度量距离。

Abstract: Friedland et al. [PRL 129, 110402 (2022)] proposed and studied a quantum analogue of the $p$-Wasserstein distance based on quantum cost matrices and quantum couplings. They conjectured that, despite being only a semidistance in general, this quantity is a true distance for a particular quantum cost matrix and for cost matrices in a small neighborhood of it. We disprove these conjectures by exhibiting an explicit family of triples of states for which the triangle inequality fails.

</details>


### [99] [Observation of coherent flux-charge interaction in a gate-tunable fluxonium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07798)
*Brian D. Isakov,Shikhar Singh,Adrian Parra-Rodriguez,David Feldstein-Bofill,Zhenhai Sun,Anders Kringhøj,Svend Krøjer,Alexandre Blais,Morten Kjaergaard,András Gyenis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用电压可调约瑟夫森结和参量调制临界电流，在超导电路中实现了通量-电荷耦合——两种共轭变量之间的直接相互作用，并通过混合超导-半导体磁通量子比特中的宇称选择规则隔离了寄生电容贡献，实现了跨正交相位的相干控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
