Motivation: 超导电路的基本元件（电容器连接电荷、电感器连接磁通）无法直接耦合共轭变量（通量与电荷），这限制了非互易性、保护模式和新型读出等应用的实现。因此，需要一个原生的通量-电荷耦合元件来填补这一空白。

Method: 利用电压可调约瑟夫森结，通过参量调制其临界电流来介导经典电荷变量与量子通量算符之间的相互作用；在混合超导-半导体磁通量子比特中依赖宇称选择规则，隔离通量-电荷耦合与其他寄生电容贡献。

Result: 实现了与驱动幅度线性相关的通量-电荷耦合，同时保持跃迁能量对栅极电压一阶不敏感；实现了跨正交相位的相干态控制。

Conclusion: 这种非常规耦合扩展了超导电路工具箱，提供了一个缺失的关键组件，实现了共轭变量的相干耦合，为非互易性、保护模式和新型读出等应用开辟了直接途径。

Abstract: Interactions that mix conjugate variables, such as the flux through a circuit element and the charge across it, lie outside the reach of the elementary couplings of superconducting circuits. Capacitors connect charge to charge, and inductors connect flux to flux, while no two-terminal element couples flux to charge directly. A native flux-charge coupling would thus serve as a circuit primitive in its own right, opening direct routes to non-reciprocity, protected modes, and unconventional readout. In this work, we demonstrate a flux-charge coupling by harnessing a voltage-tunable Josephson junction with parametrically modulated critical current, which mediates the interaction between a classical charge variable and a quantum flux operator. Relying on parity-selection rules in a hybrid superconducting-semiconductor fluxonium, we isolate the flux-charge coupling from other parasitic capacitive contributions and perform cross-quadrature-activated coherent control of states. Critically, we realize a flux-charge coupling that scales linearly with driving amplitude while keeping the transition energy first-order-insensitive to gate voltage. Such unconventional interaction broadens the toolbox of superconducting circuits with a critical missing component that enables the coherent coupling of conjugate variables.

</details>


### [100] [Topology from Decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07801)
*Alexandre Chaduteau,Derek Lee,Frank Schindler,Abhinav Prem*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，在开放多体系统中，去相干（退相干）不仅不是实现拓扑量子相的障碍，反而可以通过环境诱导的退相干效应产生拓扑现象。在一个受到环境退相干影响的晶格系统中，噪声平均动力学由相互作用量子主方程描述，实现了具有缠绕数和非厄米趋肤效应的拓扑相。该效应由相互作用驱动，且没有等效哈密顿量描述，是真正的开放系统现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为退相干是实现拓扑量子相的障碍，人们为此投入大量精力抑制噪声。本文旨在挑战这一传统认知，探究退相干是否可能本身成为诱导拓扑现象的新机制。

Method: 作者研究了一个受环境诱导退相干影响的晶格系统，利用相互作用量子主方程描述噪声平均动力学。通过分析缠绕数和非厄米趋肤效应来表征拓扑相，并比较了后选择测量结果与全平均动力学行为的差异。

Result: 噪声平均动力学实现了具有缠绕数和非厄米趋肤效应的拓扑相。关联随机噪声产生非对称扩散，其方向由缠绕数固定，且只能通过拓扑相变逆转。该效应由相互作用驱动，不同于以往的自由单粒子系统研究；后选择测量结果时效应消失，证实其为真正的开放系统现象。

Conclusion: 关联量子噪声可作为开放多体系统中实现拓扑相的路径，超越了自由粒子和非厄米哈密顿量范式。该模型具有可解析处理的优势，为理解拓扑与耗散相互作用提供了新视角。

Abstract: Decoherence is conventionally regarded as an obstacle to realizing topological quantum phases. This has motivated extensive efforts to suppress noise in candidate topological materials and devices. Here, we show that decoherence can instead induce topological phenomena. We demonstrate this in a lattice system subject to environment-induced dephasing. The noise-averaged dynamics, governed by an interacting quantum master equation, realize a topological phase characterized by a winding number and the non-Hermitian skin effect. The dynamical consequence is striking: the correlated nature of the stochastic noise yields asymmetric diffusion, whose direction is fixed by the winding number and is reversible only through a topological phase transition. This effect is induced purely by interactions, distinguishing it from previous studies of free, effectively single-particle systems. It also disappears upon postselecting measurement outcomes, confirming that it is a genuinely open-system phenomenon with no effective Hamiltonian description. Remarkably, the model remains analytically tractable. Our results establish correlated quantum noise as a route to topology in open many-body systems, beyond free-particle and non-Hermitian Hamiltonian paradigms.

</details>


### [101] [Shortcuts to Adiabaticity for non-Hermitian systems in Krylov Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07802)
*Ankit W. Shrestha,Budhaditya Bhattacharjee,Adolfo del Campo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个通用的、无需对角化的非厄米系统绝热捷径（STA）框架，通过在Krylov空间中表示绝热规范势（AGP），将精确或截断的AGP简化为稀疏三对角或上Hessenberg矩阵方程，并在多个非厄米模型上验证了其快速收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米系统的绝热捷径（STA）推广至今仍高度依赖具体模型，缺乏通用的、无需对角化的构建框架，且反绝热驱动依赖的绝热规范势（AGP）在多体系统中难以计算和实现。

Method: 从反绝热控制的积分表示出发，将AGP重写为具有可控局域性的嵌套交换子级数；利用bi-Lanczos和Arnoldi算法生成Krylov基，将精确或截断的AGP方程简化为稀疏三对角（bi-Lanczos）或上Hessenberg（Arnoldi）矩阵方程，推广了厄米构造方法。

Result: 在衰减二能级原子中恢复了精确驱动并标识了奇异点；在相互作用Hatano-Nelson模型中，截断控制快速抑制非绝热激发；在PT对称海森堡链中，AGP范数检测到PT对称性破缺转变。仅需完整Krylov空间的一小部分即可实现收敛。

Conclusion: 该框架为非厄米多体系统提供了一条实用、高效的控制路径，能够快速准确地实现绝热捷径，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Shortcuts to adiabaticity (STA) reproduce adiabatic dynamics in finite time, but their counterdiabatic implementation relies on the adiabatic gauge potential (AGP), which is difficult to compute and implement in many-body systems and whose extension to open and non-Hermitian settings has remained largely model-specific. Here, we develop a general, diagonalization-free framework for engineering STA in non-Hermitian systems by representing the AGP in Krylov space. Starting from an integral representation of the counterdiabatic control, we recast the AGP as a nested-commutator series with controlled locality and generate the associated Krylov basis using the bi-Lanczos and Arnoldi algorithms. This reduces the exact or truncated AGP to a sparse tridiagonal or upper-Hessenberg matrix equation that generalizes the Hermitian construction. We demonstrate the method on a decaying two-level atom, where it recovers the exact drive and signals the exceptional point; on the interacting Hatano-Nelson model, where truncated controls rapidly suppress nonadiabatic excitations; and on a PT-symmetric Heisenberg chain, whose AGP norm detects the PT-symmetry-breaking transition. Throughout, the expansion converges with only a small fraction of the full Krylov space, offering a practical route to fast, accurate control of many-body non-Hermitian systems.

</details>


### [102] [Robust Ion-Photon Entanglement via Polarization-to-Time-Bin Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07805)
*Ana Luiza Ferrari,Denton Wu,Mika A. Zalewski,Norbert M. Linke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验实现了首个纠缠保持的偏振-时间箱转换，将光子量子比特从偏振编码转换为时间箱编码，并与物质量子比特保持纠缠。基于$^{88}$Sr$^{+}$离子1092 nm跃迁，测得状态保真度$0.906 \pm 0.011 \le \mathcal{F} \le 0.934\pm 0.011$，转换误差$< 0.028$，且保真度不受退极化噪声影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间箱光子量子比特因对光纤链路中偏振不稳定性具有鲁棒性且适用于异构网络，非常适合量子网络应用。然而，已有技术缺乏将偏振编码光子与物质量子比特的纠缠态转换为时间箱编码的保偏方案，因此需要实现首个纠缠保持的偏振-时间箱转换。

Method: 通过偏振歧视的非对称马赫-曾德尔干涉仪，将初始偏振编码的光子量子比特转换为时间箱基。光子量子比特通过$^{88}$Sr$^{+}$离子的1092 nm跃迁产生，实现与物质量子比特的纠缠保持转换。

Result: 测得状态保真度界限为$0.906 \pm 0.011 \le \mathcal{F} \le 0.934\pm 0.011$，转换误差低于0.028。保真度不受退极化噪声影响，即使在完全退极化强度下仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 成功实现了首个纠缠保持的偏振-时间箱转换，验证了该方案对退极化噪声具有鲁棒性，为量子网络中异构编码方式的互联提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Time-bin photonic qubits are well-suited for quantum network applications due to their robustness to polarization instability in fiber links and potential for heterogeneous networks. In this work, we implement the first entanglement-preserving polarization-to-time-bin conversion of a photon qubit in an entangled state with a matter qubit. Photons initially generated with polarization encoding are converted to the time-bin basis through a polarization-discriminating asymmetric Mach-Zehnder interferometer. The photonic qubits are generated via the $1092$ nm transition of a $^{88}$Sr$^{+}$ ion. We measure state fidelity bounds of $0.906 \pm 0.011 \le \mathcal{F} \le 0.934\pm 0.011$, with conversion error $< 0.028$, and find this fidelity is unaffected by depolarizing noise even at full depolarization strength.

</details>


### [103] [Non-Local Magic from the Entanglement Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07808)
*Gianpaolo Torre,Fabio Franchini,Salvatore Marco Giampaolo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了非局域魔法（non-local magic）在纠缠谱的Walsh-Hadamard自相关函数下的表示形式，揭示了其谐波结构，并证明了非局域魔法可由纠缠熵上界约束。作者对体积律态、一维带隙和临界系统的基态等典型量子态类得到了精确解析结果，指出纠缠谱的谱组织是决定非局域魔法的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域魔法是表征真正非局域非稳定子关联的基本资源，但其直接计算在除小系统外是难以处理的数值问题，且对其理解仍然有限。因此需要找到更有效的表示方法和分析框架。

Method: 推导非局域魔法在纠缠谱的Walsh-Hadamard自相关函数下的表示，利用谐波结构进行系统分析；通过纠缠熵建立上界约束；对体积律态、一维带隙和临界系统基态等典型态类进行精确解析计算。

Result: 得到了非局域魔法的Walsh-Hadamard自相关表示形式；证明了其可由纠缠熵上界约束；对体积律态以及一维带隙和临界系统基态等广泛量子态类给出了精确解析结果，刻画了非局域魔法的标度行为。

Conclusion: 纠缠谱的谱组织是支配非局域魔法的关键因素，该表示框架为未来系统的解析研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Non-local magic has recently emerged as a fundamental resource for characterizing genuinely non-local non-stabilizer correlations. However, its direct calculation is an intractable numerical problem, except for small systems, and its understanding remains limited. We derive a representation of non-local magic in terms of the Walsh--Hadamard autocorrelations of the entanglement spectrum. Our representation makes the underlying harmonic structure explicit and enables a systematic analysis of its behaviors for various scenarios. We prove that non-local magic can be upper-bounded by an entanglement entropy and we derive exact analytical results for broad classes of quantum states, characterizing the scaling of non-local magic for volume-law states, as well as ground states of one-dimensional gapped and critical systems. Our results identify the spectral organization of the entanglement spectrum as the key ingredient governing non-local magic and provide a framework for further systematic analytical investigation.

</details>


### [104] [A Sparse and Truncated State Vector Simulator for Peaked Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07816)
*Diogo R. Ferreira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对"峰值电路"（peaked circuits）的经典模拟方法，通过稀疏状态向量表示来预测最可能的输出比特串，并实现了开源代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 峰值电路的输出分布具有尖锐峰值，理论上只需保留状态向量中有限数量的非零振幅即可进行近似模拟，避免传统量子模拟器中密集表示的高计算成本。

Method: 采用稀疏表示存储状态向量中非零振幅，对操作进行尽可能的向量化处理，并可利用硬件加速，从而在经典计算机上高效模拟峰值电路。

Result: 实现了开源模拟器，验证了该方法的可行性，并讨论了性能表现与局限性。

Conclusion: 稀疏状态向量方法能有效模拟峰值电路，但仍存在一定的性能瓶颈和适用范围限制。

Abstract: In a class of quantum circuits known as peaked circuits, the goal is to predict the most probable bit string at the output of the circuit. Since these circuits are designed to have a sharp peak in their output distribution, in principle it should be possible to simulate them using a truncated state vector with a limited number of terms, or a fraction of the total probability mass. This approximate simulation can be carried out on a classical computer with a sparse representation that stores only the nonzero amplitudes of the state vector, in contrast to the dense representations that are common in most quantum simulators. For efficiency, all operations on the state vector should be vectorized to the furthest possible extent and, if available, hardware acceleration can also be used. This work describes how these requirements were met in an open-source implementation, and discusses its performance and limitations.

</details>


### [105] [Routing Techniques for Error Corrected Silicon Spin Qubit Quantum Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07822)
*Julian Shen,Ludwig Schmid,Robert Wille*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对自旋量子比特平台，提出了一种基于"蛇行平面"模型的编译框架，包含最短路径和旋转两类量子比特路由算法，并评估了不同架构和缺陷密度下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自旋量子比特编译方法要么忽略纠错（对大规模量子计算至关重要），要么专注于底层调度而缺少对逻辑纠错算法的高层编译和路由，存在明显空白。

Method: 基于"蛇行平面"模型（2D表面码+量子比特隐形传态），提出最短路径和旋转两类路由算法，并结合缺陷处理和初始映射策略，构建完整的编译框架。

Result: 最短路径方法在稀疏、低缺陷场景表现出色，旋转方法在高密度环境中更优；框架在不同架构和问题规模下均进行了评估。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效弥补了自旋量子比特编译中高层纠错路由的空白，为大规模容错量子计算提供了实用的编译工具，代码已开源。

Abstract: Silicon spin qubits have emerged as a promising qubit technology due to their favorable scaling and fabrication properties. However, efficiently compiling quantum circuits onto spin qubit platforms remains challenging, particularly when accounting for hardware constraints and the high sensitivity to static defects. Existing compilation approaches for spin qubits either largely ignore error correction, despite its critical role for large-scale quantum computation, or focus on low-level schedule constructions, missing a high-level compilation and routing for logical, error-corrected algorithms. To address this gap, we introduce a compilation framework for spin qubits based on the recent snakes on a plane model, which utilizes a 2D surface code and qubit teleportation to mitigate errors. Building on this model, we propose shortest-path and rotation-based algorithms as two novel classes of qubit-routing techniques, along with additional defect-handling and initial-mapping strategies. We evaluate both algorithms across diverse architectural settings and problem sizes, demonstrating that shortest-path methods excel in sparse, low-defect scenarios, while rotation-based approaches perform better in high-density environments. The implementation is publicly available on GitHub as open source.

</details>


### [106] [Improved GKP magic states from error-corrected non-Gaussian quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07833)
*Sharon David,Jack Davis,Ulysse Chabaud,Francesco Arzani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究玻色量子计算中，通过将GKP纠错框架应用于非高斯输入态（如相干态叠加或Fock态叠加），可以显著提高编码魔态的质量，将魔态蒸馏所需资源降低约3倍。研究发现，输入态的优劣并非主要源于对称性，而是取决于纠错过程后是否避免了向稳定子态的投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于GKP码的玻色量子计算中，魔态蒸馏是实现容错通用量子计算的关键步骤。先前工作表明对高斯态（如真空态）进行纠错可产生可蒸馏的编码魔态，但如何进一步提高魔态质量和降低资源开销仍是重要问题。

Method: 将Baragiola等人提出的GKP纠错框架应用于多种非高斯输入态（包括相干态叠加和Fock态叠加），比较不同输入态经纠错后产生的编码魔态质量，并分析其内在机制。

Result: 许多非高斯输入态能够显著提升编码魔态的质量，在某些情况下可将魔态蒸馏所需资源降低约3倍。最优输入态并非由对称性解释，而是那些在纠错过程中避免投影到稳定子态附近的态表现最佳。

Conclusion: 通过选择合适的非高斯输入态，可以显著改善GKP框架下的魔态生成效率，降低容错通用量子计算的整体资源开销。

Abstract: Gate teleportation, together with magic state distillation, is a promising route towards fault-tolerant, universal computation. In the context of bosonic quantum computation, Baragiola et al. PRL 123(20).200502 (2019) showed that within the framework of Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill codes, encoded magic states suitable for distillation can be produced by error correcting Gaussian states, such as the vacuum. Here, we show that applying the same framework to simple non-Gaussian input states can significantly improve the quality of the magic states obtained, reducing the overall resources for the complete distillation procedure. We focus on superpositions of coherent states or Fock states, showing that many can lead to improvements in the generation of high-quality encoded magic states, which in some cases reduces the resources required for magic state distillation by about a factor $3$. We also investigate the primary source of these improvements and find that, unlike what was previously conjectured, the suitability of input states is not fully explained by symmetry arguments. Instead, the best states seem to avoid projection near stabilizer states as a result of the error correction procedure.

</details>


### [107] [Super-Logarithmic Entanglement Scaling in a Monitored Superconducting Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07835)
*Rui-Jing Guo,Zhi-Yuan Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Keldysh-replica非线性sigma模型，研究了受监测一维自旋s波BCS链在稀测量下的纠缠动力学，发现稳态纠缠熵呈超对数标度S(L)~ln²L，并解释了伴刊中数值证据的物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解受监测量子系统中测量反作用与配对振幅对纠缠动力学的联合影响，揭示拓扑平凡s波超导体中无Wess-Zumino-Witten项的SO(R)弱反局域化临界相的场论机制。

Method: 构建Keldysh-replica非线性sigma模型，从辛鞍点出发分析测量反作用和配对振幅对涨落通道的质量约束，推导出SO(R)靶流形上的有效理论，并进行单圈重整化群分析。

Result: 在复制极限R→1下，beta函数为负，产生弱反局域化流，稳态纠缠熵呈超对数标度S(L)~ln²L；证明了无需Wess-Zumino-Witten项即可实现SO(R)弱反局域化临界相。

Conclusion: 拓扑平凡的s波超导体在稀测量下可呈现弱反局域化临界相，场论结果与数值证据一致，拓展了对受监测超导系统纠缠动力学的理解。

Abstract: We develop a Keldysh-replica non-linear sigma model (NLSM) for the entanglement dynamics of a monitored one-dimensional spinful $s$-wave BCS chain in the rare-measurement regime, $γ\ll J,Δ$. Although the clean spinful $s$-wave BCS Hamiltonian belongs to symmetry class CI, spin-resolved measurements and projection to a conserved $f$-sector reduce the effective problem to class C. Starting from the corresponding parent symplectic saddle, we show that measurement backaction and the pairing amplitude impose complementary mass constraints that gap out different fluctuation channels. Their interplay dynamically projects the surviving massless modes onto an $\textrm{SO(R)}$ target manifold in replica space. A one-loop renormalization group analysis of this $\textrm{SO(R)}$ NLSM shows that, in the replica limit $R\to1$, the beta function becomes negative, producing a weak-anti-localization flow. This flow yields a super-logarithmic steady-state entanglement scaling $S(L)\sim \ln^2 L$ in the rare-measurement regime. Our field-theoretic result explains the numerical evidence reported in the companion Letter [arXiv:2604.04375] and shows that a topologically trivial monitored $s$-wave superconductor can realize an $\textrm{SO(R)}$ weak-anti-localizing critical phase without relying on a Wess-Zumino-Witten term.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum Sensors for Chemistry and Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07848)
*Piotr Put,Arjun Pillai,Xuan Hoang Le,Mikhail D. Lukin,Hongkun Park*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了以光泵磁力计（OPMs）和金刚石氮空位（NV）中心为代表的量子传感器在分子与材料分析中的应用，对比了宏观OPMs的高磁灵敏度与固态NV中心的原子级分辨率和多模态能力，讨论了它们在零场/超低场核磁共振、实时反应监测、瞬态自由基检测、高通量化学分析及非破坏性材料诊断（如电池原位监测）中的部署，并指出商业化进程将推动其解决复杂现实分析挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分析技术在灵敏度、空间分辨率和通量方面存在局限，亟需变革性的分析工具来推动化学与材料科学的发展。

Method: 以光泵磁力计（OPMs）和金刚石氮空位（NV）中心为两类代表性量子传感器平台，分别利用其宏观磁灵敏度和原子尺度分辨率及多模态能力，开展零场至超低场核磁共振、实时反应监测、瞬态自由基检测、pH检测、高通量化学分析及非破坏性材料诊断（如电池原位监测）等应用研究。

Result: OPMs与NV中心分别在超灵敏磁探测和纳米级分辨率分析中展现出优势，成功应用于零场/超低场NMR、反应动力学监测、自由基检测、高通量筛选及电池原位无损诊断等场景。

Conclusion: 随着量子传感器技术的商业化和量子增强灵敏度的提升，这类平台有望常规化地解决复杂的实际分析难题，成为下一代分析化学与材料科学的关键工具。

Abstract: The advancement of chemistry and materials science relies on transformative analytical tools which can overcome the sensitivity, spatial resolution, and throughput limitations of conventional techniques. This review explores the application of quantum sensors - specifically optically pumped magnetometers (OPMs) and nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond - as robust platforms for molecular and materials analysis. We contrast the extreme magnetic sensitivity of macroscopic OPM ensembles with the atomic-scale resolution and multimodal capabilities of solid-state NV centers. We highlight their deployment in zero- to ultralow-field and nanoscale NMR spectroscopy, real-time reaction monitoring, and transient radical and pH detection. Furthermore, we discuss their integration into high-throughput chemical assays and non-destructive materials diagnostics, such as operando battery monitoring. With the ongoing commercialization of these technologies and advances in quantum-enhanced sensitivities, quantum sensors are poised to routinely address complex real-world analytical challenges.

</details>


### [109] [Multi-agent Autoformalization of Tensor Network Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07857)
*Sirui Lu,Erickson Tjoa,J. Ignacio Cirac*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个由多个专业大语言模型智能体组成的团队，以矩阵乘积态基本定理的自动形式化验证为案例，实现了理论物理领域研究级数学形式化的自主工作流。智能体通过结构化数学蓝图和周期性人工评审协调，自主完成全部形式化过程，并探索了非标准证明路径，生成了Mathlib中此前不存在的张量网络与量子信息库。形式化工作还延伸至一维对称保护拓扑相。研究发现大规模自动形式化的主要瓶颈在于数学意图的强制执行，并提供了详细过程分析和代码库TNLean。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型应用于理论物理中研究级数学形式化的自动推理，探索AI在严格数学证明领域的可行性，并识别自动形式化的主要瓶颈。

Method: 构建多智能体LLM团队，通过结构化数学蓝图和周期性人工评审协调工作流，以Lean证明助手为平台，对矩阵乘积态基本定理进行完整的自动形式化验证。

Result: 成功自主完成了矩阵乘积态基本定理的完整形式化证明；智能体探索了文献中不存在的新的证明路径；生成了张量网络和量子信息库（补充至Mathlib）；形式化工作延伸至一维对称保护拓扑相；识别出大规模自动形式化的主要瓶颈是数学意图的强制执行。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM团队能够完成研究级理论物理的形式化推理，但数学意图的强制执行仍是当前自动形式化的核心挑战；代码和蓝图已开源为TNLean库。

Abstract: We build a team of specialized large language-model agents and present an agent-driven workflow for research-level formalization in theoretical physics, with the autoformalization of the fundamental theorem of matrix-product states as a demonstration. The agents, coordinated through a structured mathematical blueprint and periodic human review, orchestrated and executed the full formalization autonomously. For some statements, the agents were able to explore new proof routes that are not part of the standard literature. Along the way the agents produced extensive tensor-network and quantum-information libraries not previously available in Mathlib, Lean's mathematical library. As a physical application, the formalization also extends towards symmetry-protected topological phases in one dimension. We find that the main bottleneck in large-scale autoformalization is enforcing mathematical intent and we provide a detailed study of the full process and various subtleties involved. We release the codebase as the library \href{https://github.com/LionSR/TNLean}{TNLean}, together with a \nChapters{}-chapter \href{https://lionsr.github.io/TNLean/blueprint/}{blueprint} of the formalization effort.

</details>


### [110] [Unveiling Semiclassical Structures in Quantum Chaotic Eigenstates Using Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07874)
*J. Montes,F. Borondo,Gabriel G. Carlo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于过完备自编码器的"量子字典"方法，通过无监督学习从量子混沌系统的本征态中自发提取出半经典结构——字典原子自发局域在经典周期轨道上并形成疤痕结构，表明仅施加相空间局域化约束即可从谱数据中恢复非平庸的半经典组织信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究能否通过无监督学习揭示量子混沌系统本征函数的半经典结构，即验证是否仅通过学习约束就能自发发现周期轨道和疤痕等量子混沌特征，而无需显式提供经典周期轨道信息。

Method: 使用过完备自编码器（overcomplete autoencoder）作为"量子字典"，以量子烘烤映射（quantum baker map）为示例系统。对字典原子仅施加相空间局域化约束，不提供任何关于经典周期轨道的信息，学习本征态的稀疏表示。

Result: 模型对未参与训练的本征态实现了高保真度重构；将学习到的原子与独立构建的"半经典字典"对比发现，原子自发局域在经典周期轨道上并发展出疤痕（scar-like）结构。

Conclusion: 仅凭局域化约束就足以从谱数据中恢复非平庸的半经典组织信息，同时验证了周期轨道在量子混沌本征函数结构中的基础性作用。该架构开辟了学习表示的新路径，其原子可优化其他选定的物理性质。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks and neural quantum states have consolidated a new paradigm to analyze and discover physical phenomena through constrained neural parametrizations. In this context, we investigate whether the semiclassical structure of the eigenfunctions of a quantum chaotic system can be unveiled through unsupervised learning. To this end, we train a "quantum dictionary", formulated as an overcomplete autoencoder, that sparsely represents the eigenstates of the system, using as an illustration the quantum baker map. The only explicit physical information imposed on the dictionary atoms is their localization in phase space, without providing any kind of information about the periodic orbits of the corresponding classical system. The model achieves high fidelity in reconstructing eigenstates not used during training. By comparing the learned atoms with an independently constructed "semiclassical dictionary", we find that they spontaneously localize on the periodic orbits and develop scar-like structures. This result is interesting in two ways: a localization constraint is sufficient to recover nontrivial semiclassical organization from spectral data and at the same time periodic orbits confirm their fundamental role in the structure of quantum chaotic eigenfunctions. More generally, our proposed architecture opens a new route to learning representations whose atoms optimize other chosen physical properties.

</details>


### [111] [Complex spacing ratio statistics in the partially open asymmetric quantum baker map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07878)
*Leonardo Ermann,Pablo Sesin,Alejandro M. F. Rivas,Pablo D. Bergamasco,Gabriel G. Carlo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非对称量子贝克映射在部分投影开放下的复特征值统计，发现从准一维谱区间到二维Ginibre-like区间的平滑交叉行为，且三种开放几何模型在大开口数下均收敛于随机矩阵理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索开放量子混沌系统中复特征值统计行为的普适性，特别是非对称混沌系统在不同开放几何下的谱统计特征，以及从准一维到二维谱的交叉机制。

Method: 采用非对称量子贝克映射（q=2/3处不连续），考虑三种开口几何（局域化、随机、均匀），通过可调反射率ρ在完全开放（ρ=0）和封闭（ρ=1）之间插值。使用部分截断圆酉系综（PTCUE）作为随机矩阵理论基准，分析复间距比z（复平面上特征值到最近邻和次近邻距离之比）的联合分布。

Result: 发现了从准一维谱区间（特征值聚集在单位圆附近，z相位分布尖锐）到二维Ginibre-like区间（分布近乎均匀，能级排斥充分发展）的平滑交叉。开口通道数M和反射率ρ共同调控该交叉，ρ提供了固定开口大小下的额外连续控制。三种开口模型在大M下收敛于PTCUE统计，局域化模型在小M下差异最显著，未发现突变转变。

Conclusion: 该交叉行为具有普适性，对开放量子系统和波混沌实验有深远影响。

Abstract: We study the complex eigenvalue statistics of the asymmetric quantum baker map with partial projective openings. The classical asymmetric baker map, with its discontinuity at $q=2/3$, is fully chaotic, has no reflection symmetry, and provides a clean setting with tunable escape rate and fractal repeller dimension. We consider three distinct opening geometries in position space: localized (contiguous channels), random, and uniform (equispaced channels), all controlled by a tunable amplitude reflectivity parameter $ρ$ that interpolates between the fully open ($ρ=0$) and the closed ($ρ=1$) limits. We use the partially truncated circular unitary ensemble (PTCUE) as the random matrix theory benchmark. The main focus is on the joint distribution of the complex spacing ratio $z$, defined as the ratio of the distances from an eigenvalue to its nearest and next-nearest neighbors in the complex plane. We find a smooth crossover from a quasi-1D spectral regime, where eigenvalues cluster near the unit circle and the phase distribution of $z$ is peaked, to a two-dimensional Ginibre-like regime, where the distribution becomes nearly uniform and level repulsion is fully developed. Both the number of open channels $M$ and the reflectivity $ρ$ modulate this crossover, and $ρ$ provides an additional continuous control even at fixed opening size. All three opening models converge to PTCUE statistics at large $M$, while differences are most pronounced for the localized model at small $M$. No evidence of an abrupt transition is found. This crossover which suggests a universal behavior, has deep consequences for open quantum and wave-chaotic experiments.

</details>


### [112] [Measurement-Based Quantum Computing on a Photonic Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07890)
*Jeldrik Huster,Louis L. Hohmann,Kevin Edelmann,Stefanie Barz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在集成硅光芯片上实现了基于测量（MBQC）的四光子图态量子计算，包括星型（保真度83.5%）和线型（保真度75.6%）图态，并演示了单/两量子比特门、Grover搜索算法和Deutsch-Jozsa算法，验证了可重构集成光子MBQC的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成光子学为量子信息处理提供可扩展平台，而基于测量的量子计算（MBQC）通过自适应测量高纠缠图态实现量子计算，避免了确定性光子-光子相互作用的复杂需求。此前在集成光子平台上实现MBQC的可行性和可重构性尚未被验证。

Method: 在集成硅光芯片上生成最高四光子的星型和线型图态资源，通过自适应测量实现MBQC；使用量子态层析方法表征图态保真度，并搭建相应的单/两量子比特门以及Grover搜索算法和Deutsch-Jozsa算法的光学线路。

Result: 四光子星型图态保真度为(83.5±1.8)%，线型图态保真度为(75.6±1.1)%；成功演示了基于MBQC的单/两量子比特门以及Grover搜索算法和Deutsch-Jozsa算法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在可重构集成光子平台上实现四光子MBQC的可行性，为未来更大规模的集成光子量子计算奠定了实验基础。

Abstract: Integrated photonics provides a scalable platform for quantum information processing. In this context, measurement-based quantum computing (MBQC) offers an attractive approach in which quantum computation is realised by adaptive measurements on highly entangled graph states, circumventing the need for deterministic photon-photon interactions. Here, we demonstrate MBQC on an integrated silicon photonic chip capable of generating photonic graph states with up to four qubits. We achieve fidelities of $F_{Star} = (83.5 \pm 1.8)\,\%$ and $F_{Lin} = (75.6 \pm 1.1)\,\%$ for four-photon star and linear graph states, respectively. We use these resource states to implement MBQC-based single- and two-qubit gates and to demonstrate Grover's search algorithm and the Deutsch-Jozsa algorithm. These results establish the feasibility of reconfigurable four-photon MBQC on an integrated photonic platform and provide a foundation for future larger-scale implementations.

</details>


### [113] [Environmental Memory Effects and Quantum Resource Hierarchies in Polarized Hyperon--Antihyperon Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07902)
*Omar Bachain,Mohamed Amazioug,Rachid Ahl Laamara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了e⁺e⁻→J/ψ→YȲ过程中产生的超子-反超子对在退相位环境下的量子关联特性，发现非马尔可夫环境能有效保持量子资源，且量子资源的鲁棒性呈现层级关系：量子相干性最强、几何量子失协次之、对数负性最脆弱，同时束流极化可显著增强可存取的量子关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用超子-反超子对作为高能平台，研究量子关联在环境退相干下的演化特性，探索非马尔可夫记忆效应对量子资源的保护作用。

Method: 理论上分析了对数负性、几何量子失协和l₁模量子相干性在纵向和横向束流极化下的稳态与动力学性质，结合BESIII实验输入考察了对产生角、方位角、极化度和记忆参数的依赖关系。

Result: 非马尔可夫环境下信息回流产生量子关联的周期性复苏并显著延缓退相干；马尔可夫演化导致不可逆的量子信息损失达到渐近稳态。束流极化可显著增强量子关联，且量子资源呈现明确的层级关系：量子相干性最鲁棒，几何量子失协次之，对数负性最敏感。

Conclusion: 预测效应与BESIII及未来高亮度设施（STCF、CEPC）的精度兼容，为高能物理实验中的量子资源研究提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Hyperon--antihyperon pairs produced in $e^{+}e^{-}\rightarrow J/ψ\rightarrow Y\bar{Y}$ ($Y=Λ,Σ^{+},Ξ^{-},Ξ^{0}$) constitute a unique high-energy platform for probing quantum correlations through experimentally accessible spin observables. We investigate the impact of correlated dephasing environments on the stationary and dynamical properties of logarithmic negativity, geometric quantum discord, and $l_{1}$-norm quantum coherence under both longitudinal and transverse beam polarizations. Our analysis reveals that environmental memory plays a crucial role in preserving quantum resources. In the non-Markovian regime, information backflow generates recurrent revivals of quantum correlations and significantly delays decoherence, whereas Markovian evolution drives the system toward asymptotic stationary states through an irreversible loss of quantum information. The influence of beam polarization is found to be strongly channel dependent and can substantially enhance the amount of accessible quantum correlations. A comparative investigation of different quantifiers uncovers a clear hierarchy of quantum resources. Quantum coherence remains robust over the widest parameter range, geometric quantum discord survives even in regions where entanglement is strongly reduced, while logarithmic negativity is the most sensitive to environmental degradation. This hierarchy persists for all considered hyperon channels and under both polarization configurations. The dependence of quantum resources on the production angle, azimuthal angle, polarization degree, and memory parameter is examined using experimental inputs from BESIII. The predicted effects are found to be compatible with the precision expected at BESIII and future high-luminosity facilities such as STCF and CEPC.

</details>


### [114] [Invariance Audits for Quantum Kernels and Variational Rewinding: A Real-to-Hermitian Taxonomy of Projector, Flag, Anchor, and Density Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07927)
*Azadeh Alavi,Fatemeh Kouchmeshki,Hossein Akhoundi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统建立了量子机器学习中表示学习的实-厄米特分类学，证明了几何和量子提升方法在不变性与任务匹配时有效，反之则失败。无硬件加速或量子优势声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型常用归一化方向、投影子、协方差、子空间、有序旗、量子态或密度算子替代原始向量，这一替代本质上是不变性决策。需要对这类表示进行系统性审计，理解何时有效、何时失效。

Method: 建立实到厄米特的分类学框架：实侧形式化Grassmann和旗投影核，证明加权旗核的正半定性和块规范不变性，给出同跨块交换证据；量子侧证明无噪声保真度核等价于秩一厄米特投影子的Hilbert-Schmidt内积，QVR返回概率等价于输入投影子与学习锚算子间的重叠分数。

Result: 秩约束返回对应复Grassmann锚；混合或多模态类模型由密度或正半定锚自然表示。受控向量、子空间、态向量、异常、有限样本和商迹实验均支持同一结论：几何和量子提升在不变性与任务匹配时有效，丢弃标签承载信息时正确失败。

Conclusion: 量子与几何提升方法的有用性取决于其不变性是否与任务匹配，当丢弃的信息包含标签信息时方法会失效。论文不做硬件加速或量子优势声明。

Abstract: Machine-learning models often replace vectors by normalized directions, projectors, covariances, subspaces, ordered flags, quantum states, or density operators before any classifier is fitted. This replacement is an invariance decision: it determines which distinctions are kept and which are quotiented out. We develop a self-contained real-to-Hermitian taxonomy for auditing such representations in quantum machine learning. On the real side, we formalize Grassmann and flag projector kernels, prove positive semidefiniteness and block-gauge invariance of a weighted flag kernel, and give a same-span block-swap witness showing when whole-span Grassmann geometry must fail while ordered flags succeed. On the quantum side, we prove that a noiseless fidelity kernel is exactly the Hilbert--Schmidt inner product between the associated rank-one Hermitian projectors, and that a QVR-style return probability is exactly an overlap score between the input projector and a learned anchor operator. Rank-constrained returns correspond to complex Grassmann anchors, while mixed or multimodal class models are naturally represented by density or positive-semidefinite anchors. Controlled vector, subspace, statevector, anomaly, finite-shot, and quotient-witness experiments support the same conclusion: quantum and geometric lifts are useful when their invariances match the task, and fail correctly when discarded information is label-bearing. The paper makes no hardware-speedup or quantum-advantage claim.

</details>


### [115] [A hardware-efficient variational ansatz with an exact diagonal metric for real- and imaginary-time evolution and Haar sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07942)
*Dario Picozzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种硬件高效的n量子比特树形参数化量子电路，其Fubini-Study度量为闭式对角阵，无需辅助测量或矩阵求逆即可实现量子自然梯度、虚/实时演化及精确Haar采样。当目标态支持在k个计算基态子空间时，冗余参数可被剪枝编译器转化为两量子比特门数线性增长于k的电路，变体可达近最优O(nk/log n)缩放。在小分子电子结构和半填充Hubbard淬火动力学中，该方法以比领先方案少1-3个数量级的两量子比特门达到参考级精度。该ansatz可精确控制、良态且无贫瘠高原，单独使用时可在k上经典模拟；与经典难解修饰组合后可实现分子基态、热关联函数等，具有量子优势潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法的性能高度依赖于参数化电路的几何结构，但度量感知优化和时间演化所需的Fubini-Study度量传统上需要昂贵的辅助测量和病态矩阵求逆，严重限制了算法的效率和可扩展性。本文旨在设计一种硬件高效的电路结构，使得Fubini-Study度量具有闭式对角形式，从而避免辅助电路和矩阵求逆的计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出基于二叉树的n量子比特参数化ansatz，其Fubini-Study拉回度量为闭式对角矩阵；引入剪枝编译器消除冗余参数，将电路转化为两量子比特门数线性于k的结构；变体通过Schur变换修饰或内部重参数化实现精确自旋适配，固定总自旋无需惩罚项。

Result: 在小分子电子结构和半填充Hubbard模型淬火动力学中，以1-3个数量级更少的两量子比特门达到参考级精度；单独使用时可在k上经典模拟；与经典难解修饰组合后具备量子优势潜力。

Conclusion: 树形参数化ansatz通过闭式对角度量和剪枝编译，显著降低了变分量子算法的硬件开销，同时保持了精确可控性和经典可模拟性边界，为度量感知量子优化和量子优势探索提供了高效基础模块。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms depend on the geometry of their parametrised circuits: metric-aware optimisation and time evolution require the Fubini-Study metric, which has hitherto demanded costly auxiliary measurements and ill-conditioned inversions. This work introduces a hardware-efficient $n$-qubit ansatz, which parametrises states by a binary tree and whose Fubini-Study pullback metric is diagonal in closed form. Quantum natural gradient on the tree parameters, variational imaginary- and real-time evolution, and exact unitary-invariant (Haar) sampling on a symmetry sector run with no auxiliary metric circuits or matrix inversion. When the target state is supported on a subspace of $k$ computational-basis states, the redundant tree parameters carry a gauge freedom a pruning compiler converts into circuits whose two-qubit count provably grows linearly in $k$; a variant reaches near-optimal $O(nk/\log n)$ scaling with the closed-form metric intact. On electronic-structure calculations for small molecules and half-filled Hubbard quench dynamics, the method reaches reference-level accuracy with one to three orders of magnitude fewer two-qubit gates than leading alternatives. Interchangeable constructions (a Schur-transform dressing or internal reparameterisations) make the ansatz exactly spin-adapted, with fixed total spin at every parameter and no penalty terms. The bare ansatz is an exactly controllable, well-conditioned and barren-plateau-free primitive for preparing and sampling sector states: on its own, it is classically simulable in $k$ (a boundary proved for a general class of sector-sparse ansätze); composed with a classically hard dressing, it yields molecular ground states, sector-Haar benchmarking, thermal correlators, and exact effective Hamiltonians trained from energy measurements alone, with the composed circuit carrying the potential for quantum advantage.

</details>


### [116] [Associating Trajectories with Quantum Processes by Equivalent Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07970)
*Elliott Tammaro,Sriram Gundla,Arunabh Pratik,Shyla Rathore*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种替代玻姆力学的方法：通过Liénard-Wiechert势计算点电荷沿经典轨迹运动时产生的辐射频谱，并拟合轨迹参数以匹配量子过程（如原子跃迁）的频谱分布，从而找到“等效频谱”轨迹。该方法旨在建立与量子力学等效的经典运动方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻姆力学虽然为量子过程提供了轨迹解释，但其固有的非定域性与狭义相对论存在张力。作者希望寻找一种既保留轨迹概念又与相对论相容的替代方案。

Method: 利用Liénard-Wiechert势计算经典点电荷沿给定轨迹运动的推迟电磁场，数值求解推迟时间并得到辐射功率谱；通过拟合轨迹族参数，使辐射频谱匹配量子过程的频率分布（如原子跃迁），从而确定“等效频谱”轨迹。

Result: 成功找到了能够再现量子过程频率分布的等效频谱经典轨迹，表明经典辐射机制可以在频谱层面模拟量子现象。

Conclusion: 该方法为量子现象的经典轨迹描述提供了新途径；未来工作将探究这些轨迹是否由某个微分方程支配，从而构建一个量子等效的经典运动方程。

Abstract: Quantum mechanics abandoned the classical notion of a particle trajectory, yet trajectories remain conceptually appealing for resolving foundational issues in quantum mechanics. Bohmian mechanics offers one route to associating trajectories with quantum processes, but it requires explicit non-locality to remain consistent with EPR-type experiments, placing it in tension with special relativity. We propose an alternative approach: rather than deriving trajectories from a quantum guidance equation, we search for classical trajectories whose radiated frequency spectra match those of quantum processes such as atomic transitions. Using the Liénard-Wiechert potentials, we compute the electric and magnetic fields generated by a point charge along a given trajectory, numerically solving the retarded-time and from these fields obtain the power spectrum of emitted radiation. By fitting the parameters of a chosen family of trajectories, we identify "equivalent spectrum" trajectories that reproduce a target frequency distribution, including ones of quantum mechanical origin. We discuss the implications of this method and propose future work to determine whether equivalent spectrum trajectories belong to a family governed by a differential equation, which would constitute a quantum-equivalent equation of motion.

</details>


### [117] [A Quantum Reservoir Architecture for Chaotic Forecasting and a Test of Whether Its High Dimension Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07978)
*Tushar Pandey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完整的量子储层计算方案用于混沌系统预测，并引入了一种诊断方法来判断高维特征空间是否真正有效。通过同时增大预测问题和量子储层的规模，追踪拟合稳定性指标，在时空链和浅水流体模型上验证了量子储层相比经典储层具有更平坦、更稳定的误差表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子储层计算使用固定量子电路生成特征，仅训练简单的线性读出层，成本低且无优化难题。但高维特征空间可能导致虚假性能提升，需要检验其是否真正有效。

Method: 提出完整的可复现量子储层计算方案，包括数据输入方式、电路构建和读出层训练。通过同步增大预测问题规模和量子储层规模，使用稳定性指标衡量读出层拟合质量，并在时空链和浅水流体模型两个混沌测试系统上与经典储层进行对比。

Result: 在时空链和浅水流体模型上，量子储层在规模和问题同步增大时保持平坦、稳定的误差，而匹配的经典储层则表现不佳。论文也诚实地报告了经典基线在某些情况下更强的结果。

Conclusion: 提供了一套清晰的量子储层计算规范和一个可用于任何已知特征尺度储层的诊断方法，帮助判断高维特征空间是否真正贡献了性能提升。

Abstract: Quantum reservoir computing uses a fixed quantum circuit as a feature generator and trains only a simple linear readout on top of it. This makes it cheap to train and free of the optimisation problems that affect many quantum machine-learning models. A natural worry is that the very large feature space the circuit produces might inflate apparent performance without adding anything real. This paper provides two things. First, it gives a complete, reproducible recipe for one such reservoir applied to forecasting chaotic systems, including how data is fed in, how the circuit is built, and how the readout is trained. Second, it gives a way to tell whether the reservoir's high dimension is actually doing useful work. We grow the size of the prediction problem and the size of the quantum reservoir together, so that extra capacity cannot be the explanation for any improvement, and we track a single stability number that measures how well behaved the readout fit is. On two chaotic test systems, a spatiotemporal chain and a shallow-water fluid model, the quantum reservoir keeps a flat, stable error as both sizes grow, while a matched classical reservoir does not. We report where the classical baseline is in fact stronger, so the comparison is honest. The result is a clean specification plus a diagnostic that other groups can apply to any reservoir whose features have a known scale.

</details>


### [118] [Procrustes Tomography -- reconstructing noisy quantum channels made easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07988)
*Josey Stevens,Reece Robertson,Sebastian Deffner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Procrustes断层扫描方法，用于高效表征NISQ设备中的噪声量子动力学，在多个方面优于已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子过程断层扫描是量子设备表征中最昂贵的部分，但对于NISQ计算机而言，全面理解噪声量子动力学对于解释计算输出至关重要，因此需要更高效的方法。

Method: 提出Procrustes断层扫描方法，并通过教学性和建设性的介绍来阐述该方法，随后在代表性噪声量子通道示例上展示其效用。

Result: Procrustes断层扫描在多个方面优于已有方法，能够高效表征噪声量子通道。

Conclusion: Procrustes断层扫描是一种高效的量子设备表征方法，特别适用于NISQ设备中的噪声量子动力学分析。

Abstract: What is the most expensive part of quantum device characterization? Clearly, the answer is quantum process tomography. However, especially for noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) computers, a comprehensive understanding of the noisy quantum dynamics is essential in interpreting the computational output. In this work, we introduce an efficient method -- Procrustes tomography -- that outperforms established methods in a number of aspects. After a pedagogical and constructive introduction, we demonstrate the utility of the method for representative examples of noisy quantum channels.

</details>


### [119] [Optimizing LZSM protocol for high-fidelity gates in open-system fluxonium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07999)
*Santiago Ferreyra,Valentın Reparaz,Maria Jose Sanchez,Leandro Tosi,Daniel Dominguez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于单周期Landau-Zener-Stückelberg-Majorana（LZSM）驱动协议在fluxonium架构上实现快速、高保真度的量子门，推导了解析表达式以简化参数空间探索，分析了泄漏问题及抑制策略，并建立了适用于强驱动下开放系统量子门性能分析的鲁棒形式化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于共振Rabi振荡的量子门对于小频率量子比特速度慢，且易受反旋转项误差影响。当非谐性足够高时（如fluxonium架构），需要开发替代操控协议以超越标准共振驱动。

Method: 采用单周期LZSM驱动协议实现快速量子门；推导考虑电路多能级结构的解析表达式以探索参数空间；分析泄漏误差并提出抑制策略；开发适用于强驱动下开放系统量子门性能分析的鲁棒形式化方法。

Result: 实现了基于LZSM协议的快速、高保真量子门；得到了简化参数空间探索的解析表达式；识别了泄漏成为主导误差源的参数区域；建立了强驱动下考虑耗散对门保真度影响的鲁棒分析框架。

Conclusion: 在高非谐性fluxonium架构中，LZSM驱动协议可有效替代传统共振Rabi驱动，实现更快的量子门操作，同时可通过解析指导参数选择和泄漏抑制，且所提出的开放系统形式化方法适用于分析强驱动下的门性能。

Abstract: Quantum gates based on resonant Rabi oscillations are inherently slow for small-frequency qubits. They are also prone to errors due to counter-rotating terms. However, when the anharmonicity is sufficiently high, as in the fluxonium architecture, alternative manipulation protocols can outperform standard resonant driving. In this work, we implement fast, high-fidelity quantum gates based on a one-period Landau-Zener-Stückelberg-Majorana (LZSM) driving protocol. We derive analytical expressions that simplify the exploration of the parameter space while accounting for the multi-level structure of the circuit. Furthermore, we analyze the role of leakage, discussing strategies to mitigate it and identifying regimes in which it becomes the dominant source of error. Finally, to evaluate the impact of dissipation on gate fidelity, we develop a robust formalism suitable for analyzing the open-system performance of quantum gates in the strong driving regime.

</details>


### [120] [Robust Quantum Learning through Hamiltonian Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08037)
*Youya Xu,Chengyong Yu,Sanjib Ghosh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种哈密顿量编码的量子储备池计算框架，通过将输入数据直接映射到固定哈密顿量上，利用量子动力学演化生成非线性特征。该框架在模拟超导阵列处理器和数字门基量子电路两种平台上均实现了可比的表征能力与学习性能，同时发现有限耗散能抑制量子 scrambling 引起的不稳定性并增强学习性能，从而同时解决了量子学习中的可训练性、硬件效率和信息稳定性三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子学习中的三大关键挑战：可训练性（规避贫瘠高原问题）、硬件效率（降低门分解的时间开销以充分利用有限相干时间）和信息稳定性（抑制量子 scrambling 导致的长时间演化不稳定性）。

Method: 将输入数据直接映射到固定哈密顿量上，通过量子动力学演化将输入转化为高维非线性特征；采用储备池计算范式规避贫瘠高原问题；在模拟超导阵列处理器和数字门基量子电路两种平台上实现并对比；引入有限耗散来稳定学习动力学。

Result: 两种平台均展现出可比的表征能力和学习性能；模拟处理器在硬件效率上更优（避免了门分解的时间开销），但牺牲了通用性；有限耗散能抑制量子 scrambling 引起的不稳定性，并提升学习性能。

Conclusion: 哈密顿量编码的储备池计算是一种紧凑、高表达力且硬件高效的量子学习范式，适用于当前一代量子平台。

Abstract: Quantum learning provides a versatile paradigm for information processing by exploiting the intrinsic representational capacity of high-dimensional Hilbert spaces. Here, we investigate a Hamiltonian-encoding framework for quantum reservoir computing that simultaneously addresses three key challenges in quantum learning: trainability, hardware efficiency, and information stability. In this framework, input data are directly mapped onto a fixed Hamiltonian and transformed into expressive nonlinear features through quantum dynamical evolution. By employing the reservoir-computing paradigm, the approach naturally circumvents the barren plateau problem in quantum learning landscapes. We validate the framework across two complementary platforms: an analog superconducting array processor and a digital gate-based quantum circuit implementation. Despite their fundamentally different realizations, both platforms exhibit comparable representational power and achieve competitive learning performance, establishing a unified framework for cross-platform quantum learning. While both implementations achieve comparable performance, the analog processor may offer a more hardware-efficient realization by bypassing the temporal overhead of gate-based decomposition and thereby making more effective use of finite coherence times, albeit at the expense of universality. Furthermore, we find that finite dissipation suppresses quantum-scrambling-induced instabilities at long evolution times and can enhance learning performance, revealing a constructive role for environmental coupling in stabilizing quantum learning dynamics. Collectively, these results establish Hamiltonian-encoded reservoir computing as a compact, expressive, and hardware-efficient paradigm for quantum learning on current-generation quantum platforms.

</details>


### [121] [Nuclear Many-Body Systems as Benchmarks for Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08047)
*Sota Yoshida,Alessandro Baroni,Takayuki Miyagi,Ermal Rrapaj*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于现实核哈密顿量（如手征有效场论）的量子算法基准测试框架，系统构建了从核多体问题到量子比特哈密顿量的映射流程，并对量子相位估计、量子Krylov方法和可观测量动态模式分解三种本征值算法进行了T门数量和系统规模方面的资源估计与比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为核多体系统的量子算法提供一个标准化的基准测试框架，以便系统比较不同量子算法（如QPE、量子Krylov方法、ODMD）在真实核哈密顿量下的资源需求和可扩展性。

Method: 1）将第二量子化形式的核相互作用映射为量子比特哈密顿量；2）构建涵盖无芯和价空间形式、包含两体（NN）和三体（3N）相互作用的基准实例；3）对三种本征值算法进行资源估计，分析Trotterization、Qubitization和量子奇异值变换等原语的影响。

Result: 量化了不同算法和问题类别之间的扩展趋势，提供了T门计数和系统规模方面的比较结果，揭示了模型空间选择和多体相互作用对资源需求的影响。

Conclusion: 该框架为量子方法在核多体问题中的一致性比较提供了基础，所实现的NuQuLib软件栈可支持后续更广泛的量子算法研究。

Abstract: We present a framework for benchmarking quantum algorithms for nuclear many-body systems based on realistic nuclear Hamiltonians such as chiral effective field theory. To this effect we introduce a workflow that maps nuclear interactions in second quantization formalism to qubit Hamiltonians. This enables the systematic construction of benchmark instances spanning no-core and valence-space formulations with two-body (NN) and selected three-body (3N) interactions. Then, we proceed to provide resource estimates for three representative eigenvalue algorithms: Quantum Phase Estimation, Quantum Krylov methods, and Observable Dynamic Mode Decomposition. We compare their resource requirements in terms of T-gate counts and system size, and examine the impact of model-space choices and many-body interactions. The primitives included in our analysis are Trotterization, Qubitization, and Quantum Singular Value Transformation. Our results quantify scaling trends across algorithms and problem classes, and provide a basis for consistent comparisons of quantum approaches to nuclear many-body problems. The implementation is provided by the NuQuLib software stack.

</details>


### [122] [(2,m)-threshold quantum data hiding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08070)
*Donghoon Ha,Jeong San Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多方量子数据隐藏方案，允许多方共享一个经典比特，任意两方合作可通过联合测量完美恢复隐藏信息，但即使所有方执行基于LOCC的测量也只能获取任意少量信息，并给出了多方量子态最优LOCC鉴别的界限，该方案仅使用低维量子系统的可分离态实现，增强了实际可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多方量子态鉴别问题，探索如何在多方之间安全地隐藏经典信息，使得只有合作方才能恢复信息，而基于LOCC的测量无法有效获取信息。

Method: 提出多方量子数据隐藏方案，采用低维量子系统的可分离态实现；通过联合测量与LOCC测量的信息获取量对比分析；推导多方量子态最优LOCC鉴别的理论界限。

Result: 任意两方合作可通过联合测量完美恢复隐藏比特；所有方执行LOCC测量仅能获取任意少量信息；给出了多方量子态最优LOCC鉴别的界限。

Conclusion: 该方案利用低维可分离态实现了多方数据隐藏，兼具安全性和实际可行性，为量子数据隐藏提供了新的理论框架和实用路径。

Abstract: We consider multiparty quantum state discrimination and present a multiparty quantum data-hiding scheme for one classical bit to be shared among multiple parties. In the proposed scheme, any pair of parties can collaborate to perfectly recover the hidden bit through a joint measurement, whereas measurements based on local operations and classical communication(LOCC) performed even by all parties reveal only an arbitrarily small amount of information. We further provide bounds on the optimal LOCC discrimination of multiparty quantum states. The proposed scheme can be implemented using only separable states of low-dimensional quantum systems, enhancing its practical feasibility.

</details>


### [123] [Experimental demonstration of entanglement sudden death induced by natural dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08078)
*Yan Wang,Hao-Long Zhang,Jia-Hao Lü,Ken Chen,Wen Ning,Li-Hua Lin,Zhen-Biao Yang,Shi-Biao Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究首次在超导电路中对两个光子量子比特在自然耗散通道（漏腔谐振器）中实现了纠缠突然死亡（ESD）的实验观测，并通过两个辅助超导量子比特可控制地耦合到漏腔谐振器来监测退纠缠动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确认自然耗散通道（而非人工工程耗散通道）是否会诱导纠缠突然死亡（ESD），该现象理论上预测但此前未在实验中证实。

Method: 将两个光子量子比特分别存储在超导电路的漏腔谐振器中（模拟自然耗散环境），并利用两个辅助超导量子比特可控制地耦合到对应的漏腔谐振器，从而监测两个光子量子比特的退纠缠动力学。

Result: 首次展示了自然耗散诱导的纠缠突然死亡（ESD）现象，即两个光子量子比特在自然耗散通道中纠缠在有限时间内消失。

Conclusion: 实验验证了自然耗散环境可诱导纠缠突然死亡，所发展的技术为在自然环境中探索纠缠动力学铺平了道路。

Abstract: Any quantum system inevitably interacts with its natural environment, which can be modeled as a Markovian reservoir consisting of a continuum of electromagnetic field modes. The quantum coherence of qubits in a zero-temperature natural reservoir decays asymptotically, whereas the quantum entanglement of two qubits coupled to such reservoirs may disappear in a finite time. This phenomenon, referred to as entanglement sudden death (ESD), has been simulated with artificially engineered dissipative channels, but ESD induced by natural dissipative channels has not been confirmed. We here present the first demonstration of natural-dissipation-induced ESD for two photonic qubits, each stored in a leaky resonator of a superconducting circuit. The disentanglement dynamics of the two photonic qubits is monitored with two ancilla superconducting qubits, which can be controllably coupled to the corresponding leaky resonators. The techniques developed in our experiment pave the way for experimental exploration of entanglement dynamics in natural environments.

</details>


### [124] [Equivariant Quantum Clustering with Differential Privacy: Parameter-Efficient Privacy-Preserving Analysis Across Heterogeneous Sensitive Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08092)
*B. M. Taslimul Haq,Md Arifur Rahman,Tawfiq Al Islam Foysal,Abdullah Al Noman,Abir Ahmed*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: EQC是一个结合对称感知量子电路与差分隐私的参数量高效框架，在三个隐私敏感数据集上实现了更好的隐私-效用权衡，在NSL-KDD上达到79.3%聚类准确率，成员推断攻击成功率降至38.3%（ε=1.0, δ=10^-5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护聚类在医疗、网络安全和企业应用中至关重要，但现有方法难以在数据机密性与分析性能之间取得良好平衡。需要一种既能保护隐私又能保持高聚类性能的框架。

Method: 提出Equivariant Quantum Clustering (EQC)，采用p4m等变参数共享机制降低电路复杂度，同时保持信息丰富的特征表示，并结合差分隐私技术实现隐私保护。

Result: 在NSL-KDD数据集上，EQC达到79.3%聚类准确率，成员推断攻击成功率仅38.3%（隐私预算ε=1.0, δ=10^-5），优于经典和量子基线方法。消融实验表明性能提升主要来自参数高效电路设计与差分隐私的结合。

Conclusion: EQC提供了一种实用的量子就绪框架，能够在异构敏感数据集上实现安全且保护隐私的聚类，具有良好的隐私-效用权衡。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving clustering is critical for analyzing sensitive data in healthcare, cybersecurity, and enterprise applications, where maintaining data confidentiality must be balanced with analytical performance. This paper presents Equivariant Quantum Clustering (EQC), a parameter-efficient framework that integrates symmetry-aware quantum circuits with differential privacy to improve the privacy-utility tradeoff. EQC employs p4m equivariant parameter sharing to reduce circuit complexity while preserving informative feature representations. The framework is evaluated on three privacy-sensitive datasets: NSL-KDD, CERT Insider Threat v6.2, and a synthetic MIMIC-III clinical dataset. On the NSL-KDD benchmark, EQC achieves 79.3% clustering accuracy while reducing membership inference attack success to 38.3% under a privacy budget of ε = 1.0 and δ = 10^-5, outperforming representative classical and quantum baselines. Ablation studies indicate that the performance gains primarily arise from parameter-efficient circuit design combined with differential privacy. The results demonstrate that EQC provides a practical quantum-ready framework for secure and privacy-preserving clustering across heterogeneous sensitive datasets.

</details>


### [125] [Center-of-Mass Bounds and Harmonic Extremality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08096)
*Arseny Pantsialei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维平移不变多体系统中质心可观测量的谐波刚性机制，证明了基态质心涨落关于主动能隙的锐利上界，并完全刻画了等式成立的条件：外部位势为具有相同频率的谐波陷阱，而相互作用可任意。同时建立了量化近饱和估计与刚性缺陷度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单体系统中的谐波刚性机制拓展到相互作用多体系统，研究质心可观测量的基态涨落与主动能隙之间的严格关系。

Method: 通过数学分析证明质心涨落的锐利上界，刻画等式成立条件，并引入刚性缺陷度量以量化偏离谐波极端情形的程度。

Result: 证明了质心涨落上界不依赖基态正性假设；等式成立当且仅当外部位势是具有相同频率的谐波陷阱；建立了近饱和估计与刚性缺陷度量。

Conclusion: 谐波约束是固定主动能隙下刚性质心模式的唯一静态极值条件。

Abstract: We study the center-of-mass observable in one-dimensional many-body systems with translation-invariant interactions and extend the harmonic-rigidity mechanism from the one-body setting to an interacting many-body problem. We prove a sharp upper bound on the ground-state center-of-mass fluctuation in terms of the active spectral gap associated with the center-of-mass probe, and show that this bound does not require any positivity assumption on the ground state. In the positivity class, we characterize the equality case completely. Exact saturation occurs if and only if the external one-body traps are harmonic with a common frequency, while the interaction may remain arbitrary within the translation-invariant class. We also identify a natural rigidity defect measuring deviation from the harmonic extremal situation and prove quantitative near-saturation estimates controlling both the variance deficit and the spectral weight outside the first active shell. In this way, the paper establishes harmonic confinement as the unique static extremizer for the rigid interacting center-of-mass mode at fixed active gap.

</details>


### [126] [Decomposition-Based QAOA for Maximum Coverage Location Problem in Satellite Constellation Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08102)
*Divya Sisodiya,Amiratabak Bahengam,Hang Woon Lee,Hao Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对卫星星座设计中最大覆盖选址问题(MCLP)在大规模实例下计算复杂、经典求解器可扩展性受限的难题，本文提出了一种基于分解的量子优化框架，通过将大型MCLP实例分解为子图、独立量子优化以及量子重建策略，在保持竞争性覆盖性能的同时实现了更好的可扩展性和更快的求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测任务增加导致卫星星座设计需求上升，而MCLP作为轨道覆盖建模的基础是NP-hard问题，传统经典求解器（启发式、元启发式、混合整数线性规划）在大规模星座实例下可扩展性有限，现有量子计算硬件（低量子比特数、浅电路深度）仅能解决小规模问题。

Method: 提出可扩展的量子优化框架：1) 经典分解阶段将大规模MCLP实例划分为子图；2) 每个子图通过量子优化电路独立优化；3) 采用量子重建策略将子图结果合并为全局解。

Result: 在不同星座规模下的计算结果表明，与经典求解器相比，该方法在保持竞争性覆盖性能的同时，具有更好的可扩展性和更短的求解时间。

Conclusion: 分解式量子优化方法能有效突破当前量子硬件限制，为大规模卫星星座MCLP问题提供更可扩展的求解方案。

Abstract: An increase in earth observation missions has increased the demand of efficient design and optimization of satellite constellations. Maximizing coverage of the target while effectively utilizing the limited orbital resources is one of the critical design challenges for complex combinatorial optimization problems. The maximal covering location problem (MCLP), serves as a base for orbital coverage modeling, is NP-hard and computationally intractable for large-constellation instances. Using heuristics, metaheuristics, and mixed-integer linear programming, classical solvers have achieved optimal or near-optimal results, yet their scalability is limited as the problem size increases. Quantum computing advancements, including the quantum approximate optimization algorithms, offer a potential solution to NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems. Current quantum hardware limitations, such as low qubit counts and circuit depth, restrict solutions for small-scale instance problems. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a scalable quantum optimization framework for MCLP in satellite constellation design. A decomposition-based quantum methodology is proposed, in which large MCLP instances are partitioned into subgraphs by classical decomposition, optimized independently via quantum optimization circuits, and combined using quantum reconstruction strategies. Computational results across different constellation sizes reveal better scalability in less time while maintaining competitive coverage performance compared to classical solvers.

</details>


### [127] [Communication Advantages from Quantum Dense Network Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08133)
*Ian George,Brian Doolittle*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子密集网络编码协议，通过不传输函数输入的全部信息，以每个发送者仅需经典比特一半的量子比特传输非布尔函数输出，并展示了其在纠缠共享、量子通信需求、噪声鲁棒性及成功概率指数级放大方面的优势，同时衍生出测量设备无关的量子密钥增长协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子资源如何比经典资源更高效地传输信息，是量子信息理论的核心问题之一。

Method: 提出量子密集网络编码协议，通过不传输函数输入的全部信息，以一半的量子比特开销实现非布尔函数输出的传输。

Result: 该协议需要共享纠缠和量子通信；对噪声具有鲁棒性；量子与经典通信的成功概率差距可随发送者数量指数级放大。

Conclusion: 验证了量子资源在通信效率上的优势，并由此衍生出测量设备无关的量子密钥增长这一新型量子密码协议。

Abstract: A central problem in quantum information theory is understanding how quantum resources can be used to communicate information more efficiently than classical resources. We introduce quantum dense network coding -- a protocol that transmits the output of a non-Boolean function to a receiver using provably half as many qubits as bits for each sender by not transmitting the entirety of the function inputs. We show this advantage requires both shared entanglement and quantum communication, is robust to noise, and the gap in success probability between quantum and classical communication can be amplified exponentially in the number of senders. Finally, we show that dense network coding gives rise to a novel, information-theoretically secure, quantum cryptographic protocol, which we call measurement-device-independent quantum key growing.

</details>


### [128] [Adaptive Qubit Freezing Enables Robust Graph Partitioning for Divide-and-Conquer QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08138)
*Sokea Sang,Leanghok Hour,Dongmin Kim,Youngsun Han*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: FrozenLGP是一种自适应分解框架，通过识别并经典冻结阻碍图划分的顶点（基于最大流的最小顶点割计算），将可划分性从假设转变为可强制执行属性，从而在QAOA中实现对稠密或高度连通图（多达10000个顶点）的100%分解覆盖率，同时保持近似质量并提升噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分治QAOA变体依赖于小顶点分隔子的存在，在处理稠密或高度连通图时因缺乏此类分解结构而完全失效，亟需一种不依赖图拓扑结构的鲁棒分解方法。

Method: 提出FrozenLGP框架：当标准图划分失败时，通过基于最大流的最小顶点割计算识别最少数量的阻塞顶点，经典地冻结其自旋赋值，并将移除的相互作用能量折叠为相邻活跃量子比特Ising哈密顿量中的线性偏置项，从而严格保留能量贡献。

Result: 在多达10000个顶点、多种拓扑族上实现100%分解覆盖率（基线方法在高连通性实例上仅4.6%）；端到端MaxCut实验表明在已有实例上保持近似质量，并扩展至先前不支持的图；噪声仿真显示由于降低纠缠门需求而提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FrozenLGP作为一种拓扑鲁棒的前端框架，为近端量子硬件上的分布式QAOA提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Divide-and-conquer variants of the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) provide a promising route for executing combinatorial optimization problems beyond the qubit capacity of near-term quantum devices. However, existing approaches rely on the existence of small vertex separators and fail entirely on dense or highly connected graphs where such decompositions do not exist. We introduce Frozen Large Graph Partitioning (FrozenLGP), an adaptive decomposition framework that transforms partitionability from an assumption into an enforceable property. When standard partitioning fails, FrozenLGP identifies the minimum set of obstructing vertices through a minimum-vertex-cut computation based on max-flow and classically freezes their spin assignments. The energetic contributions of the removed interactions are rigorously preserved by folding them into linear bias terms in the Ising Hamiltonian of neighboring active qubits. Across graph sizes up to 10,000 vertices and multiple topology families, FrozenLGP achieves 100\% decomposition coverage, compared with 4.6\% for the standard divide-and-conquer baseline on high-connectivity instances. End-to-end MaxCut experiments demonstrate that FrozenLGP preserves approximation quality on instances already solvable by conventional divide-and-conquer QAOA while extending applicability to previously unsupported graphs, and outperforming alternative full-coverage decomposition strategies. Noise simulations further show improved robustness arising from reduced entangling-gate requirements. These results establish FrozenLGP as a topology-robust front end for distributed QAOA on near-term quantum hardware.

</details>


### [129] [Continuous-Variable MIMO THz Quantum Secret Sharing: Gaussian-modulation and Passive-modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08158)
*Leixin Wu,Jiayu Pan,Fangzhe Chen,Lingtao Zhang,Bowen Zheng,Tie Qiu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于太赫兹MIMO架构的连续变量量子秘密共享协议，通过收发波束成形将MIMO信道分解为多个并行SISO子信道，从而提升密钥率和传输距离。在理想假设下，高斯调制（32×32天线）和无源调制（1024×1024天线）方案分别在大气信道中达到14.99 m和160 m的传输距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QKD主要针对点对点通信，无法直接支持多用户协作场景；而现有QSS协议大多基于SISO信道，限制了密钥率和传输距离。因此需要一种能同时实现多方密钥共享并提升性能的新方案。

Method: 提出基于太赫兹MIMO架构的CV-QSS协议，利用收发波束成形将MIMO信道分解为多个并行SISO子信道。考虑高斯调制和无源调制两种发送方案，以及零差和外差两种检测方案，共八种协议变体。推导了渐进和可组合有限长密钥率公式，并在理想假设下进行仿真评估。

Result: 在理想假设下，高斯调制协议（32×32天线）达到14.99 m传输距离，无源调制协议（1024×1024天线）达到160 m传输距离。

Conclusion: 该工作为MIMO辅助太赫兹CV-QSS在室内和短距离室外无线网络中的潜在性能增益提供了理想化的理论基准。

Abstract: Although quantum key distribution (QKD) enables information-theoretically secure key distribution, it is mainly designed for point-to-point communication and cannot directly support multi-user collaborative scenarios. To address this limitation, quantum secret sharing (QSS) has been proposed to enable secure multiparty key sharing. However, most existing QSS protocols rely on a single-input single-output (SISO) channel, which limits the achievable secret key rate (SKR) and transmission distance. This paper proposes a continuous-variable (CV) QSS protocol based on a multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) architecture operating in the terahertz (THz) band. In the proposed scheme, transmit-receive beamforming decomposes the MIMO channel into multiple parallel SISO subchannels, thereby improving both the SKR and transmission distance. We describe the QSS transmission procedure and derive the SKR expressions for eight protocol variants under Gaussian collective attacks. Specifically, Gaussian modulation and passive modulation are considered at the transmitter, while homodyne and heterodyne detection are considered at the receiver. Both asymptotic and composable finite-size SKR formulas are derived to characterize the ideal upper-bound performance and the achievable performance under finite resources, respectively. Simulation results show that, under ideal assumptions including perfect channel state information, perfect phase synchronization, and ideal beamforming, the Gaussian-modulation protocol with a 32 x 32 antenna configuration and the passive-modulation protocol with a 1024 x 1024 antenna configuration achieve transmission distances of 14.99 m and 160 m in the atmospheric channel, respectively. These results provide an idealized theoretical benchmark for evaluating the potential performance gains of MIMO-assisted THz CV-QSS in indoor and short-range outdoor wireless networks.

</details>


### [130] [Efficient Pauli-decomposition and multistage state-refinement for tensor network based differential equation solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08166)
*Vishwabhushan Suresh Gholap,Himadri Shekhar Dhar,Siddhartha Santra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两种技术改进张量网络方法求解偏微分方程的特征值问题：通过Pauli-string基解析展开将MPO内存需求从O(2^{n+1})降至O(2n)，并提出多阶段状态细化启发式算法将虚时演化的收敛速度提升两个数量级，成功在20量子比特MPO上计算了维度超10^6的拉普拉斯算子的前32个本征态（保真度>0.95）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典数值方法求解高维PDE时计算成本急剧增加，而现有张量网络方法虽能用量子比特表示大算子，但仍面临两大挑战：1) 生成大型无显式张量积结构算子的MPO需要极大内存；2) 传统虚时演化的Trotter化步数随量子比特数n快速增加，收敛困难。

Method: 1) 在Pauli-string基下展开微分算子，推导Pauli系数的解析表达式，将MPO构造的内存需求从O(2^{n+1})降至O(2n)。2) 提出多阶段状态细化启发式算法，通过逐步逼近目标态加速虚时演化收敛。

Result: 在20量子比特MPO上计算了维度超10^6的拉普拉斯算子前32个本征态，保真度>0.95；收敛速度提升最多两个数量级；在2D非简谐振子和无序系统中验证了方法有效性，但无序强度增大会降低精度。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了大规模稀疏结构化微分算子的张量网络表示和计算效率问题，但该方法在强无序系统中的适用性受限。

Abstract: Classical numerical techniques for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) become computationally expensive as the dimension of the discretized differential operator increases. For PDEs giving rise to Sturm--Liouville problems, tensor network (TN) methods can be highly productive: an operator of dimension $N\times N$ can be represented as a matrix product operator (MPO) using only $n=\log_2(N)$ qubits, enabling computation of eigenvalues and eigenvectors via imaginary time evolution (ITE). However, this remains computationally challenging. First, most methods for generating MPOs of large operators without explicit tensor-product structure require prohibitively large memory. Second, the number of Trotterization steps for convergence in conventional ITE increases rapidly with $n$. We present techniques to mitigate both challenges for certain sparse, structured differential operators. To address the first, we construct the MPO by expanding the operator in the Pauli-string basis, enabled by an analytical expression for the Pauli basis coefficients that reduces the memory requirement from $\mathcal{O}(2^{n+1})$ to $\mathcal{O}(2n)$. To address the second, we propose a multistage state-refinement heuristic that accelerates ITE convergence, reducing convergence time by up to two orders of magnitude. Using this TN framework, we compute the first 32 eigenstates of a Laplacian of dimension exceeding $10^6$ with fidelity above $0.95$ using a 20-qubit MPO. We further validate the method on the 2D anharmonic oscillator and investigate disordered systems, where increasing random potential strength degrades accuracy and limits the approach.

</details>


### [131] [Efficient High-Dimensional Quantum Circuit Synthesis: From Multi-Controlled Gates to Isometries and Quantum Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08200)
*Gui-Long Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出高效的多控量子门电路合成方案，将(n-1)-控单量子比特门的CINC和GCX门复杂度分别降至O(n²)和O(n)，特殊酉算子进一步降至O(n)；首次给出素数维SUM门合成单控门的方案，并建立了SUM和CINC门数量的理论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多控门合成是量子计算（d级量子系统）中的关键问题，现有方案的CINC和GCX门复杂度较高（分别为O(n^{2+log₂ d})和O(n³)），需要更高效的合成方案以降低资源消耗。

Method: 提出高效的电路合成框架，减少多控单量子比特门的基元门数量；利用所提电路构建量子等距映射和量子信道；针对素数维d，首次用SUM门和单量子比特门合成单控门。

Result: (n-1)-控酉算子：CINC门O(n²)、GCX门O(n)；(n-1)-控特殊酉算子：复杂度降至O(n)；n量子比特一般酉算子：CINC门数量优于先前结果；素数维下所有CINC电路可转换为SUM电路且复杂度不变；建立了SUM和CINC门数量的理论下界。

Conclusion: 提出的方案显著降低了多控门合成的资源消耗，并首次实现了素数维下SUM门对单控门的合成，为量子电路优化提供了新的理论下界。

Abstract: Circuit synthesis of multi-controlled gates is crucial for qudit ($d$-level) quantum computing. This paper presents efficient synthesis schemes that reduce the elementary gate count for multi-controlled single-qudit gates. For synthesizing general $(n-1)$-controlled unitaries on $n$ qudits, we reduce the controlled-increment (CINC) and generalized controlled-$X$ (GCX) gate counts to $O(n^2)$, improving upon existing $O(n^{2+\log_2 d})$ CINC and $O(n^3)$ GCX bounds. For $(n-1)$-controlled special unitaries, this complexity is further reduced to $O(n)$. By utilizing the proposed circuit, we present qudit-based circuit constructions for isometries and quantum channels from $n$ to $m$ qudits. When specialized to general $n$-qudit unitaries, our construction requires fewer CINC gates than previous results. Moreover, for the first time, we present a circuit synthesis scheme for single-controlled gates using SUM gates and single-qudit gates when $d$ is prime. This enables all CINC-based circuits for various quantum operations to be converted into SUM-gate circuits while preserving the same asymptotic complexity. Finally, we establish a theoretical lower bound on the number of SUM and CINC gates required to synthesize general $n$-qudit unitaries.

</details>


### [132] [Möbius-Guided Diagonal-Gate Compilation with Native Multiqubit Controlled-Phase Gates on Neutral-Atom Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08212)
*Hairuo Huang,Yanwu Gu,Chen Huang,Xi Zhao,Meng-Jun Hu,Dong E. Liu,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Möbius变换的编译器，将对角相位函数映射为相位超图，利用中性原子硬件原生支持的多量子比特受控相位操作，通过调度原子运动、相互作用区约束和封锁可行性，实现更高效的量子计算编译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准编译器通常将对角门结构分解为单量子比特和双量子比特门，未能充分利用中性原子硬件原生的里德伯介导多量子比特受控相位操作能力，导致硬件资源利用效率低下。

Method: 1) 通过子集格Möbius反演将对角相位函数映射为相位超图，保留多体相位项的支撑和角度；2) 利用中性原子调度器考虑原子运动、相互作用区约束、封锁可行性和错误成本，在原生高阶执行与分解方案之间进行直接比较和路由选择。

Result: 与routed ZAP和ZX-calculus基线相比，在具有可开发的三体和四体相位项的算法实例上展现出更优的估计成功概率，在主要含二体相位项的实例上性能相当。

Conclusion: 该工作为更充分利用中性原子硬件的原生能力提供了一种可行的编译策略，将原子可重构性和里德伯介导的多量子比特相位操作作为实用资源，实现更高效的量子计算。

Abstract: Diagonal gates are ubiquitous primitives in quantum algorithms, from phase oracles, hypergraph-state preparation, and multi-control logic to Hamiltonian simulation of spin models and digitized lattice field theories, where Ising interactions and local potential terms are diagonal in the encoded basis. Standard compilers, however, often lower diagonal structure into one- and two-qubit gates before neutral-atom hardware can exploit native Rydberg-mediated multiqubit controlled-phase operations. We propose a Möbius-guided compiler that maps a diagonal phase function to a phase hypergraph via subset-lattice Möbius inversion. The hypergraph retains the support and angle of each many-body phase term, allowing sparse or local high-order structure to be routed as native multiqubit controlled-phase candidates when feasible and decomposed otherwise. The neutral-atom scheduler accounts for atom motion, interaction-zone constraints, blockade feasibility, and error costs, enabling a direct comparison between native high-order execution and decomposed alternatives. Benchmarks against routed ZAP and ZX-calculus baselines show improved estimated success for algorithmic instances with exploitable three- and four-body phase terms, and comparable performance on predominantly two-body instances. These results provide a feasible compilation strategy for more fully exploiting the native capabilities of neutral-atom hardware, using atom reconfigurability and Rydberg-mediated multiqubit phase operations as practical resources for more efficient quantum computation.

</details>


### [133] [Quantum linear solvers for quantum chemistry: prospects of exponential quantum advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08220)
*Peniel Bertrand Tsemo,Kenji Sugisaki,Ishita Bhattacharjee,V. S. Prasannaa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子线性求解器(QLS)应用于多参考线性化耦合簇(icMRLCC)方法，通过三种互补诊断手段分析条件数(κ)的系统规模标度，发现其呈多对数增长，结合稀疏性的亚线性标度，支持了QLS在LCC问题中实现指数加速的前景；数值计算显示与基准方法的能量误差不超过0.009%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HHL-SRLCC框架仅限于弱关联体系，需要发展能处理强关联区域的通用方法，并系统评估条件数标度以判断量子指数加速的可行性。

Method: 将QLS-SRLCC扩展为基于内收缩多参考LCC方法的QLS-icMRLCC；采用三种互补诊断分析κ标度：直接计算κ、最大最小对角元比值、基于近期猜想的A矩阵结构分析；结合稀疏性亚线性标度论证。

Result: 三种诊断方法一致预测κ呈多对数(polylogarithmic)标度；数值计算中四个原子以内模型体系的势能曲线能量误差不超过0.009%。

Conclusion: 多对数κ标度与亚线性稀疏性标度共同支持了QLS在LCC问题中实现指数量子加速的前景。

Abstract: Quantum linear solvers (QLSs) can offer the potential for exponential quantum advantage in solving quantum chemical problems, but its assessment hinges on determining the condition number ($κ$) scaling, which itself is computationally challenging. While a recent work applied the Harrow-Hassidim-Lloyd (HHL) algorithm to single-reference linearized coupled cluster equations (SRLCC), the validity of the HHL-SRLCC framework is restricted to weakly correlated regimes. A general treatment requires a formulation that can access strongly correlated regions. We thus begin by extending the QLS-SRLCC framework to its multi-reference form, which is based on the internally contracted multi-reference LCC method (QLS-icMRLCC). We then analyze $κ$ scaling using three complementary diagnostics that range from explicit computations to use of indirect structural indicators: (i) direct calculations of $κ$, (ii) scaling of the ratio of maximum to minimum diagonal entries of an A matrix, and (iii) structural analyses of the A matrices based on a recently proposed conjecture, which we adapt to the QLS-LCC problem. The three approaches yield consistent predictions, indicating a polylogarithmic $κ$ scaling in system size. This finding, when combined with our arguments on sub-linear scaling of sparsity, supports the prospects of exponential advantage using QLSs for the LCC problem. Finally, numerical calculations on potential energy curves of model systems containing up to four atoms recover the ground state energies with errors relative to benchmark classical methods not exceeding 0.009$\%$.

</details>


### [134] [Engineering Nonclassical States via the Dynamical Casimir Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08275)
*Maristella Crotti,Luca Razzoli,Giacomo Guarnieri,Luigi Giannelli,Giuseppe A. Falci,Giuliano Benenti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，超强耦合光-物质系统中由动态卡西米尔效应（DCE）产生的非绝热驱动通常被视为误差来源，但实际上可被利用为工程化非经典光态的资源。通过优化量子控制设计驱动协议，在频率可调量子比特与腔模超强耦合系统中，将真空涨落转化为目标态（如福克态、压缩态和薛定谔猫态叠加），展示了一种通用且鲁棒的确定性制备方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为超强耦合系统中非绝热驱动引起的DCE会产生误差；本文旨在颠覆这一认知，探索将DCE从误差源转变为非经典光态制备资源的可能性。

Method: 采用最优量子控制方法，在包含频率可调量子比特与腔模的超强耦合系统中设计驱动协议，通过数值优化实现将真空涨落转换为特定非经典光态。

Result: 数值优化展示了一种通用且鲁棒的方法，能够确定性制备福克态、压缩态以及薛定谔猫态叠加等广泛类别的非经典光态。

Conclusion: 动态卡西米尔效应并非仅能引起误差，在最优量子控制下可作为资源用于确定性制备多种非经典光态，为量子光学和量子信息处理提供了新途径。

Abstract: Nonadiabatic driving in ultrastrongly coupled light--matter systems is commonly regarded as a source of errors, as counter-rotating interactions convert vacuum fluctuations into real excitations through the dynamical Casimir effect (DCE). Here we show that, instead, the DCE can be harnessed as a resource for engineering nonclassical states of light. Considering a cavity mode ultrastrongly coupled to a frequency-tunable qubit, we employ optimal quantum control to design driving protocols that convert vacuum fluctuations into targeted states. Numerical optimization reveals a versatile and robust approach for the deterministic preparation of a broad class of nonclassical states, illustrated here through Fock states, squeezed states, and Schrödinger-cat-state superpositions.

</details>


### [135] [Works on My QPU: Reproducibility in Quantum Computing Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08348)
*Dominik Köster,Maja Franz,Benjamin Zec,Nicole Hoess,Ralf Ramsauer,Wolfgang Mauerer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对量子计算研究的可重复性进行了大规模分析，发现仅约24-27%的论文提供代码工件，且其中大部分无法在干净环境中成功执行。研究提出了一套改进可重复性的实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算研究日益依赖复杂的软件栈，但研究成果的可重复性未得到足够重视，与国际科学机构的建议和软件系统研究的最佳实践存在差距。

Method: 采用人工分析（127篇论文的五维度评估框架：代码可用性、环境规范、文档、硬件描述、可执行性）与自动化大规模筛选（近5000篇论文的可重复性指标分析）相结合的方法。

Result: 人工分析显示仅24.4%的论文提供代码工件，其中64.5%无法在干净环境中成功执行；自动化分析得出代码可用率为26.8%，约三分之一的可用代码缺乏机器可读的环境规范。

Conclusion: 量子计算研究的可重复性尚未得到一致实现，需要采取行动改进；作者提出了针对性的实践建议以解决观察到的失败模式。

Abstract: Quantum computing research increasingly depends on complex software stacks, yet the reproducibility of published results does not receive the priority and longevity mandated by recommendations of large international scientific bodies and best practices in software-centric systems research. In this paper, we present a combined manual and automated large-scale analysis of the reproducibility landscape in quantum computing research, quantify shortcomings, and derive actionable steps forward.
  We manually evaluate a curated sample of 127 papers using a five-question framework that covers code availability, environment specification, documentation, hardware description, and executability. To place these findings in a broader context, we conduct an automated large-scale screening of nearly 5000 quantum computing papers for the same reproducibility indicators. Our manual analysis reveals that only 24.4% of the sampled papers provide code artefacts, and among those, 64.5% fail to execute successfully in a clean environment. This assessment is corroborated by a large-scale automated analysis that yields a consistent code availability rate of 26.8%. Further, it shows that approximately one-third of the papers with accessible code lack machine-readable environment specifications.
  The results in this paper indicate that reproducibility is not yet consistently achieved in quantum computing research. In response, we outline a set of practical recommendations that address the observed failure modes and illustrate how reproducibility can be improved in practice.

</details>


### [136] [Grokking and epoch-wise double descent in quantum neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08350)
*Daniel Pranjić,Marco Roth,Christian Tutschku*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在变分量子机器学习中观察到"顿悟"（grokking）转变和epoch级双重下降现象，揭示了过参数化量子神经网络在训练后期出现的泛化衰退问题，并提出基于权重范数正则化的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子机器学习中的grokk​​ing现象尚未被系统研究，理解其动态机制对于提升量子神经网络的泛化能力至关重要。

Method: 在两量子比特、SU(4)完全参数化的量子神经网络上进行实验，通过增加电路深度实现过参数化，结合算法稳定性理论分析权重范数增长与泛化衰退的关系，并引入弱显式权重范数正则化进行缓解。

Result: 发现grokk​​ing转变和epoch级双重下降；过参数化提高泛化成功率；训练后期出现泛化衰退，与权重范数无约束增长相关；弱权重范数正则化能稳定后grokk​​ing阶段并永久保留泛化收益。

Conclusion: 权重范数正则化是训练过参数化量子电路的有效框架，可防止泛化衰退并保持泛化稳定性。

Abstract: Grokking, the delayed transition from memorization to generalization, is a fundamental phenomenon in gradient-based learning, yet its dynamics within variational quantum machine learning (QML) remain largely unexamined. In this work, we report the empirical observation of both the grokking transition and epoch-wise double descent in a two-qubit quantum neural network (QNN) under a complete parameterization of the SU(4) manifold. We demonstrate that overparameterization via increased circuit depth improves the probability of successful generalization. Notably, these architectures frequently exhibit an epoch-wise double descent in test error, degrading at a critical epoch before recovering into a generalizing state. Crucially, we identify a generalization decay in late-stage training, where the test error increases significantly despite a stagnant training loss. Bridging this behavior with algorithmic stability theory, our analysis reveals that this decay correlates with an unconstrained increase of the weight-norm, drifting away from sparse, phase-aligned harmonic solutions toward overfitted solutions in the Hilbert space. We analyze the underlying temporal dynamics of this transition, demonstrating how the onset of generalization is linked to optimization hyperparameters such as learning rate and weight decay. Finally, to mitigate late-stage decay, we introduce a weak explicit weight-norm regularization into the loss function. We demonstrate that this structural anchor stabilizes the post-grokking phase and permanently preserves generalization gains, providing a robust framework for training overparameterized quantum circuits.

</details>


### [137] [Stroboscopic Stabilization of Cat Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08363)
*Timo Hillmann,Franco Nori,Fernando Quijandría*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于辅助二能级系统重复相互作用的耗散稳定化猫量子比特新范式，无需工程化耗散环境，克服了现有超导平台方案的限制，兼容更广泛的实验平台，并保留噪声偏置特性，可扩展到压缩猫量子比特以部分纠正单光子损失错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于双光子耗散的猫量子比特方案受限于超导平台、杂散衰变通道和有限耗散率，亟需一种更通用且性能更优的稳定化方法。

Method: 利用二次哈密顿量介导的辅助二能级系统与主系统之间的重复相互作用（interaction），取代依赖非线性耦合到有损环境的传统工程耗散方案。

Result: 该方法无需储层工程即可实现耗散稳定化，保留了噪声偏置（bit-flip错误指数抑制与phase-flip错误线性增长），并延伸到压缩猫量子比特，使单光子损失错误部分可纠正。

Conclusion: 提出的新范式在克服现有方案关键局限的同时，兼容更多实验平台，为容错量子计算提供了更鲁棒且可扩展的猫量子比特实现路径。

Abstract: Dissipatively stabilized cat qubits provide a promising route toward fault-tolerant quantum computation, exhibiting exponential suppression of bit-flip errors with increasing phase-space separation of the logical states, while incurring only a linear increase in phase-flip errors. Existing implementations rely on engineered two-photon dissipation via nonlinear coupling to a lossy environment, an approach largely confined to superconducting platforms and limited by spurious decay channels and finite dissipation rates. Here, we propose a fundamentally different stabilization paradigm based on repeated interactions with an auxiliary two-level system mediated by a quadratic Hamiltonian, enabling dissipative stabilization without reservoir engineering. Our approach overcomes key limitations of existing schemes and is compatible with a wider class of experimental platforms.
