Furthermore, it preserves the noise bias and extends to squeezed cat qubits, rendering single-photon loss errors partially correctable.

</details>


### [138] [Parallel QEC Decoding Applied to Distributed Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08386)
*Gabriele Incardona,Davide Ferrari,Michele Amoretti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于置信传播与有序统计译码的并行量子纠错解码方法，通过对晶格子区域局部应用奇异值分解预处理错误向量，在分布式量子计算机上实现，并评估了复杂度、准确性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提升量子纠错解码的效率与可扩展性，以应对分布式量子计算中量子比特规模增长带来的解码挑战。

Method: 采用置信传播结合有序统计译码，并对置信传播所得错误向量在晶格子区域局部进行奇异值分解预处理，实现并行化解码。

Result: 在分布式量子计算机环境中，从复杂度、准确性和可扩展性三个维度对所提并行方法进行了评估。

Conclusion: 提出的并行方法在分布式量子计算场景下能够有效平衡解码的复杂度与准确性，并展现出良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: A novel parallel approach is proposed for QEC decoding based on Belief Propagation with Ordered Statistics Decoding. The main idea is to pre-process the error vectors obtained from Belief Propagation by applying Singular Value Decomposition locally to sub-regions of the lattice. The proposed approach is applied to distributed quantum computers and evaluated in terms of complexity, accuracy, and scalability.

</details>


### [139] [Efficiently simulable quantum circuits with large entanglement, magic, and non-Gaussianity via code-compiled tensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08396)
*Aydin Deger,Stergios Koutsioumpas,Mark Webster,Hasan Sayginel,Joschka Roffe,Dan E. Browne*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类具有高纠缠熵、魔术度和非高斯性等经典模拟困难指标，却可通过矩阵乘积态（MPS）高效经典模拟的量子电路族。该构造利用高码率CSS码的逻辑电路和码自同构与高阶Clifford层次的对角门，实现了非局域逻辑门。模拟效率依赖于：(i)对角横向门不增加键维；(ii)置换通过经典重标号追踪。实验表明键维仅由编码开销决定而与电路深度无关，证明标准资源度量单独不足以指示模拟难度。此外，该方法可用于直接保真度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子电路的经典模拟困难指标（如纠缠熵、魔术度、非高斯性）各自独立时，可能无法可靠地指示实际的经典模拟难度。本文旨在构造一类尽管在上述指标上表现出"困难"特征，却仍可被MPS高效模拟的电路族，从而揭示这些资源度量的局限性。

Method: 利用高码率CSS码的逻辑电路，通过码自同构和来自Clifford层级更高阶的横向对角门实现非局域逻辑Clifford和非Clifford门。对角横向门不增加MPS键维，置换操作通过经典重标号追踪避免SWAP网络开销。对于单项式子族，还提供了精确相位多项式后端。

Result: 在无限极化的CSS码族上进行演示，结果表明键维保持有界且仅由编码开销决定，与电路深度无关。该方法适用于包含密集纠缠、魔术态和非高斯输入在内的广泛初始态类别。提供了保真度估计的应用示例。

Conclusion: 标准资源度量（纠缠熵、魔术度、非高斯性）单独不足以指示经典模拟难度。所提出的构造方法为量子电路的经典模拟提供了新视角，并在近期的量子设备保真度估计中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce a family of quantum circuits that possess standard indicators of classical simulation hardness including high entanglement entropy, magic, and non-Gaussianity, yet admit efficient classical simulation via matrix product states (MPS). Our construction uses logical circuits of high-rate Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes with enhanced symmetries. Using code automorphisms and transversal diagonal gates from higher levels of the Clifford hierarchy, we realize nonlocal logical Clifford and non-Clifford gates, showing how error-correcting codes can compile complex logical circuits into simple physical operations. Simulation efficiency rests on two properties: (i) diagonal transversal gates do not increase bond dimension, and (ii) permutations are tracked classically via on-the-fly relabeling, avoiding costly SWAP networks. Unlike Clifford or matchgate simulation, our method accepts a broad class of initial states, including dense entangled, magic, and non-Gaussian inputs, provided the encoded state retains an efficient MPS representation. We also release an exact phase-polynomial backend for monomial subfamilies, whose cost is set by higher-degree phase terms rather than entanglement growth. We demonstrate the method on an infinite polar CSS code family, showing bond dimension stays bounded by the encoding cost regardless of circuit depth. These results show that for some circuit families, standard resource measures are individually insufficient to indicate simulation hardness. As a near-term application, we use the compiled MPS as a classical reference for direct fidelity estimation of a quantum device running nontrivial logical circuits. Pauli sampling on the encoded reference, with a Clifford pushback through the known encoder, provides the ideal expectation values, so the logical output fidelity can be estimated from local Pauli readout alone, without costly state tomography.

</details>


### [140] [The Geometry of Quantum Complexity in Open Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08411)
*Ezra Acalapati,Kausik Ghosh,Giuseppe Policastro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Nielsen的量子复杂性几何方法从封闭系统扩展到开放量子系统，提出了基于次芬斯勒几何的耗散量子复杂性理论框架，并通过多个物理实例验证了该框架的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子复杂性理论主要针对封闭系统的幺正演化，而实际量子系统不可避免地与环境耦合，存在耗散。因此需要建立能够处理开放量子系统（由林德布拉德演化描述）的复杂性几何框架。

Method: 将复杂性定义为混合态空间上的最优控制问题，对幺正和非幺正生成元均赋予代价；采用次芬斯勒几何（sub-Finslerian）作为自然几何结构，允许非可逆测地线并限制允许的切方向；通过改变代价函数中的惩罚因子来调节几何性质（如旗曲率）。

Result: 开放系统的自然几何结构是次芬斯勒几何，与封闭系统的黎曼几何有根本性差异；耗散导致测地线不可逆；通过分析退极化、振幅阻尼通道和阻尼谐振子等例子，验证了惩罚因子可改变旗曲率等几何性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为耗散量子系统的复杂性量化提供了几何框架，并与实验可实现的体系有潜在联系，为理解开放量子系统的复杂性提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We extend Nielsen's geometric approach for quantum complexity from closed to open quantum systems, whose dynamics is governed by Lindbladian evolution. In this framework, complexity is defined through an optimal-control problem on the space of mixed states, with a cost assigned to both unitary and non-unitary generators. We show that the resulting geometric structure differs fundamentally from the Riemannian geometry that emerges in the case of unitary evolution. In the open-system setting, the natural geometry is typically sub-Finslerian. Dissipation makes the geodesics non-reversible, while the admissible tangent directions are restricted by the physically allowed controls. We analyze several physically motivated examples, including a single qubit subject to depolarizing and amplitude-damping channels, as well as the damped harmonic oscillator. We show that, similarly to the unitary case, varying the penalty factors in the cost functional modifies the geometric properties through changes in the flag curvature, the Finslerian analog of sectional curvature. Our results provide a geometric framework for quantifying the abstract notion of complexity in dissipative quantum systems, with potential connections to experimentally realizable setups.

</details>


### [141] [Quantum and Classical Potts Criticality in Driven-Dissipative Bosonic Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08425)
*Jacopo Tosca,Zejian Li,Cristiano Ciuti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了受三光子参数驱动并存在耗散的Bose-Hubbard晶格，发现其非平衡稳态自发破缺ℤ₃对称性，实现三态Potts模型的临界行为。在二维单光子损耗下稳态相变属于经典2D三态Potts普适类；在一维三光子损耗下则属于量子1D三态Potts普适类，揭示了多光子耗散将临界行为从经典推向量子普适类的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索内在非平衡动力学如何涌现平衡态普适性这一基本开放问题，并寻找实现三态Potts模型临界性的物理平台。

Method: 构建受三光子参数驱动的Bose-Hubbard晶格，采用基于变分多高斯近似的变分相空间方法，在1D和2D晶格中进行有限尺寸标度分析。

Result: 二维晶格中单光子损耗下非平衡稳态相变属于经典2D三态Potts普适类；一维晶格中三光子损耗下相变属于量子1D三态Potts普适类。

Conclusion: 驱动-耗散玻色子晶格可作为实现Potts临界性的平台，多光子耗散是促使非平衡临界行为从经典普适类跃迁至量子普适类的关键机制。

Abstract: The emergence of equilibrium universality from intrinsically nonequilibrium dynamics is a fundamental open problem. Bose-Hubbard lattices realized in photonic and circuit-QED platforms provide a versatile setting to engineer nonlinear interactions, dissipation, and multiphoton processes. Here we investigate a Bose-Hubbard lattice subject to three-photon parametric driving, whose nonequilibrium steady state spontaneously breaks a $\mathbb Z_3$ symmetry and realizes the criticality of the three-state Potts model, a three-state generalization of the Ising model. Using a variational phase-space approach with systematically controllable accuracy based on a Variational Multi-Gaussian ansatz, we perform finite-size scaling analyses in one and two spatial dimensions. We find that, in two-dimensional lattices with single-photon losses, the nonequilibrium steady-state transition belongs to the universality class of the 2D classical three-state Potts model. In contrast, in one-dimensional lattices with three-photon losses, the transition is governed by the one-dimensional quantum three-state Potts universality class. These results establish driven-dissipative bosonic lattices as a platform for emergent Potts criticality and identify multiphoton dissipation as a mechanism that promotes nonequilibrium critical behavior from classical to quantum universality classes.

</details>


### [142] [Global Precision Limits in Critical Quantum Metrology: From Cramér-Rao to Ziv-Zakai](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08431)
*Neng Zeng,Tao Liu,Yu-Ran Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过量子Ziv-Zakai界证明，临界量子计量学中的超海森堡极限灵敏度并非源于临界性的真正增益，而是依赖于精确的先验知识，在不考虑测量时仅能实现相对于先验标准差的常数倍精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示临界量子计量学中精度极限的根本限制，澄清超海森堡极限灵敏度是否真正源于临界性，而非先验知识带来的假象。

Method: 采用量子Ziv-Zakai界替代传统的Cramér-Rao界，推导临界量子计量学中均方误差的极限，分析二阶量子相变场景下的精度表现。

Result: Cramér-Rao界预测的精度相对于先验标准差并无实质性提升；临界量子传感器的精度仅能达到相对于先验标准差的常数增益；超海森堡极限灵敏度源于精确先验知识，而非临界性本身。

Conclusion: 临界量子计量学在局域传感场景下存在根本性精度限制，即使不考虑态制备成本和噪声，也无法实现真正的超海森堡增益；该工作为评估临界量子计量学提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Critical quantum metrology with equilibrium states predicts quantum-enhanced sensitivity only in the vicinity of criticality, where large prior information about the parameter is required. By employing quantum Ziv-Zakai bounds, we derive a limit on the mean-square error in critical quantum metrology. For second-order quantum phase transitions, we show that the precision predicted by the Cramér-Rao bound offers no substantial improvement over the prior standard deviation. Thus, the critical quantum sensor's precision can only achieve a constant gain compared to the prior standard deviation, even without performing any measurement. We elucidate the fundamental limitation on the achievable precision in critical quantum metrology in the context of local sensing, even without considering state-preparation costs or noise. Thus, the super-Heisenberg-limited sensitivity at criticality arises from precise prior knowledge rather than a genuine gain due to criticality. Our work provides a practical framework for assessing critical quantum metrology and a routine for studying quantum sensing with many-body systems.

</details>


### [143] [Simulation of exchange coupling effects in double quantum dot FinFET-like structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08447)
*Ilan Bouquet,Alexander Maeder,Mathieu Luisier*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用GPU加速的薛定谔-泊松(SP)求解器，结合Luttinger-Kohn 6×6 k·p哈密顿量，对硅鳍式场效应晶体管(FinFET)中形成的双量子点(DQD)空穴自旋双量子比特器件的交换耦合进行表征。通过组态相互作用(CI)方法构建并对角化双粒子哈密顿量，获得DQD系统的本征态和交换耦合。结果表明该框架能用少量基态重现器件的磁电行为，并可用于确定Si FinFET结构中双量子比特逻辑门的最优工作条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现对硅基FinFET结构中空穴自旋双量子比特器件交换耦合的精确表征，为量子逻辑门的最优工作条件提供理论指导。

Method: 采用GPU加速的有限差分SP求解器求解3D泊松方程；使用Luttinger-Kohn 6×6 k·p哈密顿量描述空穴能带结构（含磁场和应变）；通过CI方法基于单粒子Slater行列式构建双粒子哈密顿量并对其对角化，得到交换耦合值。

Result: 使用少量基态即可准确再现器件的磁电行为，与理论和实验结果吻合；成功确定了双量子比特逻辑门的最优工作条件。

Conclusion: 该仿真框架是设计和优化硅基空穴自旋量子比特器件的有效工具，能够在减小计算代价的同时保持精度，有助于推动硅基量子计算的实验实现。

Abstract: By leveraging a GPU-accelerated Schrödinger-Poisson (SP) solver, we characterize exchange coupling in a hole spin double-qubit device involving a double quantum dot (DQD) system formed inside a 5-gate silicon fin field-effect transistor (FinFET) similar to real experimental structures. The self-consistent SP simulations rely on a finite difference discretization of the 3D volume and on a Luttinger-Kohn 6x6 kp Hamiltonian accounting for magnetic fields and strain distribution. They return the gate-induced confined electronic states and the corresponding electrostatic potential hosting the DQD. These quantities serve as inputs to a two-particle Hamiltonian that is constructed from single-particle Slater determinants through the configuration interaction (CI) method. By diagonalizing this two-particle Hamiltonian, the eigenstates and eigenenergies of the DQD system are obtained, together with their exchange coupling. We show that our simulation framework, using a reduced number of basis states, is capable of reproducing the magneto-electrostatic behavior of the devices of interest, as predicted from theory and observed experimentally. We finally leverage our approach to determine the optimal operating conditions of a two-qubit quantum logic gate implemented in a Si FinFET structure.

</details>


### [144] [Exactly solved Schrödinger equations with time-dependent Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08450)
*Michael Warnock,Antônio Francisco Neto,Pierre-Louis Giscard,Omid Faizy,Christian Joachim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了四种与量子自旋电池相关的含时哈密顿量演化算符的解析、精确、显式且无假设的公式，包括两种随机情况。通过结合三种数学技术（星代数、路径和与Omega演算）获得精确解，并展示了如何从精确解中恢复并超越已有的展开和近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子自旋电池中涉及的含时哈密顿量提供精确的演化算符公式，克服现有展开和近似的局限性。

Method: 创新性地结合了三种数学技术：星代数（$\star$-algebra）、路径和（path-sums）与Omega演算（Omega calculus），得到精确解。

Result: 得到了四种含时哈密顿量（包括两个随机情况）的精确演化算符公式；展示了如何从精确解恢复并超越已有展开和近似，例如得到了Floquet哈密顿量在所有阶数的显式精确公式。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法不仅适用于量子自旋电池，还可广泛应用于其他非自治微分系统。

Abstract: We present the analytical, exact, explicit, and assumption free formulas for the evolution operators corresponding to four instances of time-dependent Hamiltonians relevant to quantum spin batteries including two stochastic cases. We demonstrate how to recover and go beyond existing expansions and approximations directly from the exact solutions giving, for example, an explicit exact formula for Floquet Hamiltonians at all orders. The exact solutions are obtained through a completely novel combination of three mathematical techniques, the $\star$-algebra, path-sums and Omega calculus, which we briefly overview. These are widely applicable to other non-autonomous differential systems.

</details>


### [145] [Optimizing and Certifying Multipartite Permutationally Invariant Bell Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08462)
*Jin-Fu Chen,Mengyao Hu,Jordi Tura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的方法来构造和验证置换不变的贝尔不等式，仅使用单体和两体关联函数。该方法适用于任意测量设置数m，在大N极限下优化量子值与经典界限的比值，并通过半正定规划认证量子违背。优化后的不等式能高效检测多体贝尔非局域性，且测量设置越多违背越强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体贝尔非局域性为多体量子关联提供了设备无关的探测手段，但经典和量子优化问题的复杂性随系统规模快速增长，限制了其表征。现有方法难以扩展到大规模多体系统，亟需可扩展的构造和验证方案。

Method: 利用置换对称性，仅基于单体和两体关联函数构造贝尔不等式；在大N极限下优化量子值与经典界限的比值；采用半正定规划(SDP)认证量子违背。

Result: 在大N极限下，优化后的量子-经典比值对于有限m取简单有理数，当m→∞时收敛于coth(1)；更多测量设置导致更强的量子违背。

Conclusion: 该方法可扩展地构造和认证多体贝尔不等式，有效检测多体贝尔非局域性，集体测量和更多测量设置能增强违背强度。

Abstract: Multipartite Bell nonlocality provides a device-independent probe of many-body quantum correlations, but its characterization is limited by the rapid growth of the underlying classical and quantum optimization problems. We develop a scalable method for constructing and certifying permutationally invariant Bell inequalities using only one- and two-body correlators. The construction gives families of inequalities with robust quantum violations for general $m$ measurements as the number of parties $N$ becomes large. To improve robustness against noise, we optimize the ratio of the quantum value to the classical bound for these families in the large-$N$ limit. We then certify the resulting quantum violation using semidefinite programming. For the broad class of Bell inequalities studied here, the infinite-$N$ ratios take simple rational values for finite $m$ and converge to $\coth(1)$ as $m\to\infty$. The optimized inequalities efficiently detect many-body Bell nonlocality with collective measurements, with more measurement settings leading to stronger violations.

</details>


### [146] [Non-Hermitian topology driven by an identity term: An exactly solvable paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08469)
*Lingfang Li,Yating Wei,Yang Ruan,Gangzhou Wu,Jun Wang,Shihua Chen,Tong Lin,Ching Hua Lee,Zhenhua Ni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现非厄米趋肤泵浦下，哈密顿量中的恒等项（identity term）不再谱惰性，而是能动量依赖地形变广义布里渊区。通过在Hatano-Nelson链中引入自旋轨道耦合，我们给出了开边界下整个非厄米本征系统的精确解析解，揭示了元胞间自旋轨道耦合与非平凡恒等项协同作用，在完全缺乏手征对称性时诱导拓扑边缘态和稳健零模。这项工作建立了超越对称性保护的非厄米拓扑精确可解范式，并为检验含动量依赖恒等项的拓扑不变量提供了严格基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认为哈密顿量中的恒等项只改变能量而不影响本征态拓扑，但本文发现非厄米趋肤泵浦下这一范式失效，亟需理解动量依赖恒等项对广义布里渊区和拓扑性质的影响。

Method: 在Hatano-Nelson链中引入自旋轨道耦合，构建包含动量依赖恒等项的非厄米模型，并通过解析方法精确求解开边界条件下的整个非厄米本征系统。

Result: 1) 动量依赖恒等项在非厄米趋肤泵浦下主动形变广义布里渊区，挑战了依赖固定复值轮廓的传统拓扑判据；2) 元胞间自旋轨道耦合与恒等项协同作用，可在完全缺乏手征对称性时诱导拓扑边缘态和稳健零模。

Conclusion: 建立了超越对称性保护的非厄米拓扑精确可解范式，为检验含动量依赖恒等项的拓扑不变量提供了严格基准。

Abstract: An identity term in the Hamiltonian is conventionally regarded as spectrally inert-it shifts energies but does not alter eigenstate topology. We show that under non-Hermitian skin pumping, this paradigm fails: a momentum-dependent identity term actively deforms the generalized Brillouin zone, thereby challenging established topological criteria that rely on fixed complex contours. Here, by introducing spin-orbit coupling into a Hatano-Nelson chain, we present an exact analytical solution for the entire non-Hermitian eigensystem under open boundary conditions. Our solution reveals how inter-cell spin-orbit coupling, synergizing with this non-trivial identity term, induces topological edge states and robust zero modes in the complete absence of chiral symmetry. This work establishes an exactly solvable paradigm for non-Hermitian topology beyond symmetry protection, and provides a rigorous benchmark for testing topological invariants in systems with momentum-dependent identity terms.

</details>


### [147] [Magic Gate Teleportation: Structure, Useful Resource States, and Simpler Feedforward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08508)
*Yunzhe Zheng,Allen Zang,Aleksander Kubica*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种被称为"魔法门隐形传态"(MGT)的量子门隐形传态协议理论，该协议可在不泄露输入状态信息的情况下实现非Clifford门操作。作者揭示了MGT的隐藏结构——Pauli测量反向传播后，MGT协议可视为将输入态编码到由测量结果宣告的稳定子码中，随后执行逻辑非Clifford门。基于此结构，他们为任意由交换Pauli旋转作用于稳定子态得到的资源态构建了MGT协议，并提供了有效算法。同时证明有用的MGT资源态必须Clifford等价于对角态，且从[5,1,3]码协议中蒸馏的输出态不适用于MGT。此外，他们识别了前馈算子可由Pauli算子实现的条件，为算法容错范式提供了洞见并简化了量子计算所需的前馈操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子门隐形传态是容错量子计算中的关键技术，但现有方法在实现非Clifford门时存在信息泄露或资源开销大的问题。作者希望开发一种既能实现非Clifford门、又不对输入状态泄露任何信息的通用协议理论，并揭示其深层次结构以指导资源态的构造与优化。

Method: 通过分析Pauli测量反向传播的隐藏结构，将MGT协议重新解释为"编码到稳定子码+逻辑非Clifford门"的框架。利用这一结构，为任意由交换Pauli旋转作用于稳定子态得到的资源态构造MGT协议，并设计合成电路的高效算法。同时通过证明资源态必须Clifford等价于对角态来刻画有用资源态的充要条件。

Result: (1) 揭示了MGT协议可统一理解为编码-逻辑门操作的隐藏结构；(2) 给出了任意交换Pauli旋转资源态对应的MGT协议构造方法及电路合成算法；(3) 证明了有用的MGT资源态必须Clifford等价于对角态，且[5,1,3]码蒸馏态不适用；(4) 识别了前馈算子可简化为Pauli算子的条件。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了MGT协议的系统理论，揭示了其与稳定子编码的内在联系，界定了有用资源态的范围，并简化了前馈操作。这为容错量子计算中非Clifford门的实现提供了理论指导，推动了算法容错范式的发展。

Abstract: Quantum gate teleportation is a key technique in fault-tolerant quantum computation that uses resource states to implement logical gates. Here, we develop a theory of quantum gate teleportation protocols that implement non-Clifford gates on arbitrary input states without revealing any information about them; we refer to these protocols as magic gate teleportation (MGT). We uncover a hidden structure within MGT -- after backpropagating the Pauli measurements, MGT protocols can be viewed as encoding the input state into a stabilizer code heralded by the measurement outcomes, followed by a logical non-Clifford gate. Using this structure, we construct MGT protocols for any resource state obtained by applying commuting Pauli rotations to a stabilizer state, and provide an efficient algorithm for synthesizing their circuit implementations. Conversely, we prove that useful resource states for MGT, i.e., states that can be used for non-Clifford gates through MGT protocols, are necessarily Clifford-equivalent to diagonal states; in particular, the output state distilled from the $[\![5, 1, 3]\!]$ protocol is not useful for MGT. Finally, we identify conditions under which the feedforward operators can be implemented by Pauli operators, shedding light on the paradigm of algorithmic fault tolerance and simplifying the feedforward operations needed for quantum computing.

</details>


### [148] [Metropolitan entanglement distribution between an atom and a near-visible photon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08513)
*Maya Büki,Pooja Malik,Florian Fertig,Tobias Frank,Marvin Scholz,Tommy Block,Gianvito Chiarella,Yiru Zhou,Emanuele Distante,Pau Farrera,Gerhard Rempe,Harald Weinfurter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过两个低噪声量子频率转换器，将原子与780nm光子的纠缠转换为电信S波段，经24km商用光纤和14km视线传播后，实现1.7%的光子传输效率，且纠缠保真度损失小于1%，证明了原子量子节点与现有长距离光纤网络的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络需要在长距离上分发纠缠，但先进的量子网络节点的工作波长与电信波段不匹配，限制了其与现有光纤基础设施的集成。

Method: 使用两个特制的低噪声量子频率转换器：首先将原子与780nm光子纠缠，然后将光子频率转换到电信S波段，经24km商用光纤和14km视线传播后，再转换回原始波长。

Result: 实现了1.7%的光子传输效率，原子-光子纠缠保真度损失小于1%。

Conclusion: 该技术将原子量子节点与现有长距离光纤网络集成变为可能，为量子信息处理中的新型应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Entanglement distribution is the overarching purpose of quantum networks. While communication over long distances can use deployed fiber infrastructure, it requires photons in the telecom band. However, advanced quantum network nodes do not operate at such wavelengths. Here we overcome this limitation with two tailor-made low-noise quantum-frequency converters to distribute entanglement between a single atom and a resonant photon over 14km line-of-sight via 24km of a deployed commercial fiber. The photon at wavelength 780nm is first entangled with the atom, then converted to the telecom S-band, and finally back-converted after propagation through the fiber. This link enables a photon transfer efficiency of 1.7% while affecting the atom-photon entanglement fidelity by less than 1%. This brings integration of atomic quantum nodes with existing long-distance fiber networks into reach, enabling novel applications in quantum information processing.

</details>


### [149] [Quantum Communication Lower Bounds for Search Problems via Matrix Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08517)
*Minbo Gao,Chenghua Liu,Guangxu Yang,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为搜索问题提出了一种基于矩阵差异性的新方法，以证明单向量子通信下界，并首次在碰撞查找和三角形查找问题上取得了紧的下界结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索问题可以有多个有效输出，这一特性对标准量子下界技术构成了根本性障碍，亟需新的方法来突破这一限制。

Method: 提出了一种基于矩阵差异性的新方法，能够联合界定量子协议中的输出测量，从而绕过搜索问题多输出的障碍。

Result: 1) 碰撞查找问题：证明了紧的Ω(N^{1/4})单向量子通信下界（此前最佳为Ω(N^{1/12})）；2) 三角形查找问题：在特定参数范围内证明了Ω(√Δ_V)的一趟量子流空间下界，匹配经典上界。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅首次为两个重要搜索问题确立了紧的量子下界，还能通过完全不同的论证恢复经典下界，避免了对量子协议失效的Boolean-Hidden-Matching归约。

Abstract: We study one-way quantum communication lower bounds for search problems. Unlike decision problems, search problems can have many valid outputs, which pose a fundamental barrier to standard quantum lower-bound techniques. We overcome this by developing a novel method based on matrix discrepancy, which allows us to bound the output measurements of a quantum protocol jointly.
  As applications of our method, we establish the first tight quantum lower bounds for two fundamental search problems in some natural parameter regimes: collision finding and triangle finding. For collision finding, we prove a tight $Ω(N^{1/4})$ one-way quantum communication lower bound. Previously, the best-known quantum communication lower bound for collision finding was $Ω(N^{1/12})$ due to Göös and Jain (RANDOM 2022), and no stronger bound was known even under the one-way restriction. For triangle finding in graph streams, we prove a one-pass quantum streaming space lower bound of $Ω\left(\sqrt{Δ_V}\right)$ for graphs with $m$ edges, $Θ(m)$ triangles, and constant $Δ_E$, where $Δ_V$ and $Δ_E$ denote the maximum number of triangles sharing a common vertex and edge, respectively, under the condition that $1\le Δ_V\le m^{2/3}$. This constitutes the first nontrivial quantum space lower bound in this regime, matching the classical upper bound of Jayaram and Kallaugher (RANDOM 2021) up to logarithmic factors. Notably, our method also recovers the classical lower bound of Kallaugher and Price (SODA 2017) through an entirely different argument, avoiding their Boolean-Hidden-Matching reduction that breaks down for quantum protocols.

</details>


### [150] [The Langevin-equation description of optomechanics with the dispersive and dissipative optomechanical coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08530)
*Alexander K. Tagantsev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于经典波动方程的输入-输出关系方法，对光力学系统中的量子Langevin方程形式进行了批判性评估。研究发现，在耗散光力耦合存在的情况下，Langevin方程形式存在显著问题：腔衰减率虽依赖于腔长但不产生耗散光力耦合，输入-输出关系存在不准确性，且遗漏了关键的输入场相位因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对基于量子Langevin方程的光力学系统形式框架进行批判性评估，检验其在处理含半透明机械膜的法布里-珀罗腔和改良迈克尔孙-萨格纳克干涉仪时的有效性和适用范围。

Method: 采用基于经典波动方程的输入-输出关系方法（引力波领域常用），推导腔内场阶梯算符、随机反作用力及输入镜面场关系，并将结果简化至Langevin方程形式的适用范围后进行比较分析。

Result: (1) 腔衰减率依赖于腔长，但不产生耗散光力耦合，这与标准Langevin方程预测相反；(2) 在Langevin方程形式适用范围内，输入镜面场关系可能不正确；(3) Langevin方程形式遗漏了输入场相位因子，该因子对耗散光力耦合情形至关重要。

Conclusion: Langevin方程形式在处理耗散光力耦合时存在根本性问题，其适用范围需要修正，输入-输出关系方法能提供更准确的描述。

Abstract: The description of the optomechanical system is commonly based on the quantum Langevin equation formalism. This framework is introduced phenomenologically or based on a model Hamiltonian. However, once dealing with the optomechanical Fabry-Perot cavity or the modified Michelson-Sagnac interferometer with a semitransparent mechanically active membrane inside, a model-free consideration is also possible by using an alternative approach. Such an approach, which is based on the classical wave equations in the systems, is popular in the gravitational-wave community where it is termed as input-output relations approach. In this work, using the aforementioned approach, we derived the equations for the ladder operator of the intracavity field, stochastic back-action force, and the relation between the fields at the input mirror. Then we simplified the obtained results down to the range of applicability of the Langevin equation formalism and compared these with the corresponding predictions of the latter formalism. This enabled us to critically assess the validity of the Langevin equation formalism and rectify its range of applicability. In the case where the dissipative optomechanical coupling is involved we identified appreciable problems with this formalism. We found that, disregarding the fact that decay rate of the optomechanical Fabry-Perot cavity depends on its length, no dissipative optomechanical coupling is generated. This is in contrast with the prediction of the standard Langevin-equation based treatment. We found that, staying inside the range of applicability of the Langevin equation formalism, the relation between the fields at the input mirror may not be correct. We found that the Langevin equation formalism misses a phase factor at the input field, this factor turns out to be important for the situation involving the dissipative optomechanical coupling.

</details>


### [151] [An Effective Quantum Hoare Logic for Hybrid Quantum Programs with Unbounded Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08548)
*Christophe Chareton,Jad Issa,Romain Péchoux*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于混合量子程序的半自动化静态分析方案，通过引入整数混合路径和（IHPS）来处理含非有界循环的量子程序，实现了功能验证与资源（终止性/代价）估计的有机结合，并开发了Haskell原型工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合量子程序分析存在空白：符号执行缺乏对混合化和非有界递归的处理，而支持它们的量子Hoare逻辑则表达力不足且缺乏高效的等式推理能力，亟需一种兼具功能验证和资源估计的半自动化静态分析方案。

Method: 提出整数混合路径和（IHPS, Integer Hybrid Path-Sums），扩展传统路径和（path-sums）以处理非有界while循环；设计基于循环不变量的通用策略来确定终止性和期望资源消耗；采用Haskell实现半自动化工具。

Result: 通过多个示例验证了所提策略的有效性，实现了首个半自动化静态分析方案，能够同时处理功能验证和资源（终止性或代价）估计。

Conclusion: 该工作是迈向完整混合量子程序静态资源分析工具的第一步，对实际量子计算应用开发具有重要意义。

Abstract: While quantum hardware remains limited, hybrid quantum-classical algorithms with complex control structures, including unbounded loops, are emerging, posing new challenges for quantum program analysis, including the accurate estimation of the resource consumption of a given program. Meanwhile, precise analysis techniques such as symbolic execution have largely left out hybridization and unbounded recursion. On the other hand, current quantum Hoare logics that generally support them are lacking in expressiveness and miss out on efficient computational equational reasoning that could be implemented in a semi-automated tool. This leaves a gap awaiting to be filled. In this work, we answer this challenge with the first semi-automated static analysis solution combining effective functional verification and resource (termination or cost) estimation for hybrid quantum programs with unbounded loops. Towards that end, we introduce integer hybrid path-sums (IHPS), extending path-sums to handle unbounded while loops, as a representation of possible executions of a program. A generic strategy for determining termination and expected resource consumption via loop invariants is also proposed and illustrated on several examples. Finally, the solution is implemented as a semi-automatic Haskell program. This work is the first step toward the design of a complete static resource analysis tool for hybrid quantum programs, essential for the development of real-world quantum computing.

</details>


### [152] [QSCOUT's Qubit-Boson Gate Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08560)
*Edward C. Tortorici,Ethan C. McGarrigle,Brian K. McFarland,Wes L. Johnson,Daniel S. Lobser,Melissa C. Revelle,Brandon P. Ruzic,Susan M. Clark,Christopher G. Yale*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QSCOUT开发了用于CV-DV量子计算的量子-玻色子门集，并介绍了如何使用Jaqal语言在QSCOUT上实现这些门操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为混合连续-离散变量量子计算提供可操作的量子-玻色子门集，并降低用户使用门槛。

Method: 在QSCOUT平台上开发了混合CV-DV量子计算所需的量子比特-玻色子门集，并通过Jaqal语言为用户提供编程接口。

Result: 形成了可供用户使用的、基于Jaqal语言的QSCOUT量子-玻色子门集编程方案。

Conclusion: QSCOUT成功实现了CV-DV混合量子计算的门集，并通过Jaqal语言降低了用户编程复杂度，推动了混合量子计算平台的实用化。

Abstract: The Quantum Scientific Computing Open User Testbed (QSCOUT) has developed a qubit-boson gate set for hybrid continuous-discrete variable (CV-DV) quantum computing. This document outlines how to utilize these gates on QSCOUT using Just Another Quantum Assembly Language, Jaqal\textsuperscript{TM}.

</details>


### [153] [Distributed Monogamy of Entanglement limits Quantum Channel Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08591)
*Rabsan Galib Ahmed,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入分数可扩展性(fractional extendibility)概念，精细刻画量子关联泄漏到环境中的程度，证明其在张量积下不变且局部处理下单调。建立了分布式多夫纠缠性质：在AB₁B₂...Bₙ态中，从随机k个B系统中提取EPR对的最大平均概率为k/n。利用这些工具证明，擦除概率超过1/2的量子擦除信道无法模拟更少噪声的擦除信道，即使渐近多次使用更嘈杂的信道也不行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统k-可扩展性只能离散量化环境模拟B副本的数量，缺乏更精细的刻画。作者希望引入连续版本的分数可扩展性,以更精确地表征量子关联的泄漏程度，并基于此建立更强大的纠缠单配性约束，进而解决信道模拟能力问题。

Method: 理论推导：定义分数可扩展性概念；证明其在张量积下的不变性和局部处理下的单调性；建立分布式单配性关系；应用这些工具分析量子擦除信道的可模拟性。

Result: 分数可扩展性在张量积下不变、局部处理下单调；分布式单配性成立：最大平均EPR提取概率为k/n；擦除概率>1/2的信道无法模拟噪声更小的擦除信道，即使在渐近意义下。

Conclusion: 分数可扩展性为量子关联的单配性提供了更精细的刻画，并导出了信道模拟的严格限制条件，对量子通信和量子密码学有重要理论意义。

Abstract: Entanglement is monogamous: if it is shared among more than two parties, the entanglement between any pair cannot be very strong. For an integer $k\geq 2$, $k$-extendibility of a state $ρ_{AB}$ quantifies this as the number of copies of $B$ that can be simulated by the state's environment. We introduce fractional extendibility, which gives a finer characterization of the quantum correlation that is leaked to the environment, and prove that it is invariant under tensor products and monotonic under local processing. We also establish the distributed monogamy of entanglement: for any state on $AB_1B_2\dots B_n$, the maximum average probability of extracting an EPR pair from a random subset of $k \leq n/2$ systems among the $B_i$'s is the fraction $k/n$. With these tools we show that any quantum erasure channel with erasure probability more than a half cannot simulate a less noisy erasure channel, even with asymptotically many uses of the more noisy channel.

</details>


### [154] [Operational meaning of Markov gap in tripartite entanglement of quantum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08615)
*Zongsheng Zhou,Riqiang Zhang,Yu-Xiang Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了不可约三方纠缠（IrTE）在远离平衡的短程动力学系统中如何涌现，发现自由费米子链中马尔可夫间隙表现出准线性增长或阶梯式跳跃两种行为，其饱和时间尺度为t~L²，远慢于双体纠缠的弹道传播。通过引入“本质三方费米子”（ETF）和三方零矩阵概念，揭示了马尔可夫间隙与小奇异值数量之间的对应关系，并在相互作用的XXZ链中验证了关键动力学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不可约多方纠缠（特别是三方纠缠）作为长程关联，如何在短程动力学系统中从非平衡态涌现出来，以及其动力学行为与双体纠缠有何本质区别。

Method: 以自由费米子链为模型，以马尔可夫间隙作为不可约三方纠缠的探针；利用Lieb-Robinson界推导出IrTE起始时间的上下界；引入“本质三方费米子”（ETF）和三方零矩阵的概念，通过奇异值分解建立与马尔可夫间隙的定量联系；并在相互作用XXZ链中验证结论的普适性。

Result: (1) 马尔可夫间隙根据初始状态不同呈现准线性增长或阶梯式跳跃行为；(2) IrTE起始时间可由Lieb-Robinson界约束；(3) 马尔可夫间隙在t~L²时间尺度上饱和至体积律值，远慢于双体纠缠的弹道传播；(4) 马尔可夫间隙与三方零矩阵的小奇异值数量密切相关；(5) 关键动力学特征在XXZ链中依旧成立。

Conclusion: 不可约三方纠缠的建立时间远慢于双体纠缠，揭示了一类新的动力学普适类；马尔可夫间隙可以通过三方零矩阵的奇异值谱获得清晰的物理解释，且该结论在相互作用系统中具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We investigate how irreducible multipartite entanglement, a long-range correlation by nature, can emerge from short-range dynamics far from equilibrium. Focusing on the Markov gap as a probe of irreducible tripartite entanglement (IrTE) in free-fermion chains, we uncover qualitatively distinct dynamical behaviors: the Markov gap grows either quasi-linearly or in staircase-like jumps depending on the initial state. We also propose attainable upper and lower bounds for the onset time of IrTE based on the Lieb-Robinson bound. Strikingly, the Markov gap saturates to a volume-law value on a timescale $t\sim\! L^2$, much slower than the ballistic spreading of bipartite correlations. To understand what information about the wavefunctions is revealed by the Markov gap calculation, we introduce the concept of essential tripartite fermion (ETF) and an associated tripartite null matrix. The value of Markov gap closely tracks the number of small singular values of this tripartite null matrix, yielding a transparent, operational physical interpretation of the measure. We further demonstrate that several dynamical signatures persist in the interacting XXZ chain.

</details>


### [155] [A Nonstabilizerness Resource Law for Universal Quantum State Purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08626)
*Keming He,Enji Xiong,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子态纯化所需的魔法资源（magic resources），证明在奇素数维度下存在精确的线性 mana 定律，在多量子比特系统中存在双侧线性鲁棒性定律（单比特时精确），并给出了显式的成功纯化映射，建立了通用纯化任务中魔法与保真度之间的定量权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期"No-go"结果表明经典可模拟操作无法实现非平凡的通用保真度增益，这促使研究者定量刻画纯化过程中所需的魔法资源（非Clifford资源），以理解魔法资源在保真度提升中的根本作用。

Method: 采用资源理论框架，针对两输入副本的通用纯化任务，在奇素数维度下推导精确的线性 mana 定律，在多量子比特系统中推导双侧线性鲁棒性定律，并构造显式纯化映射以揭示魔法-保真度权衡的透明性。

Result: 1) 在奇素数维度下证明了精确的线性 mana 定律；2) 在多量子比特系统中证明了双侧线性鲁棒性定律（单比特时精确）；3) 给出了一个显式的成功纯化映射，使魔法与保真度的权衡关系变得透明。

Conclusion: 通用纯化是一个服从定量魔法-保真度定律的任务，将魔法资源与误差缓解及容错量子信息处理联系起来。

Abstract: Quantum state purification aims to recover higher-fidelity quantum states from multiple noisy copies and is a fundamental primitive for quantum information processing. Magic resources enable operations beyond classically simulable dynamics and are central to universal fault-tolerant quantum computation. Recent no-go results show that classically simulable operations cannot achieve a nontrivial universal fidelity gain. This motivates a quantitative theory of the magic required for purification at prescribed success probability and target fidelity. For universal purification with two input copies, we prove an exact linear mana law in odd dimensions and a two-sided linear robustness law for multi-qubit systems, which becomes exact for a single qubit. We also identify an explicit successful purification map that makes the tradeoff transparent. These results establish universal purification as a task obeying a quantitative magic-fidelity law and link magic resources to error mitigation and fault-tolerant quantum information processing.

</details>


### [156] [Triangulene-based diradicals as a blueprint for molecular quantum platforms with optical addressability and long spin coherence times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08634)
*Arup Sarkar,Cathal Hogan,Conor Ryan,Lorenzo A. Mariano,Alessandro Lunghi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算，揭示了三角烯及其衍生物这类有机双自由基分子具有接近0.5 eV的三重态-单重态能隙、长自旋相干时间（氘代分子在10K下T₂=0.21 ms）以及高自旋选择性的系间穿越特性，表明它们是具有光学可寻址能力的高相干分子量子比特候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找兼具长自旋相干时间和高效自旋-光学界面的分子体系，并希望在室温下实现，以推动分子量子技术的发展。

Method: 采用先进的第一性原理方法，计算了三种有机双自由基（三角烯1、其氮杂阳离子衍生物2、晶体3）的电子结构、自旋退相干时间（声子与核自旋相互作用）、以及双声子贡献的系间穿越速率。

Result: 1) 三种分子均具有约0.5 eV的三重态-单重态能隙，类固态缺陷（如NV色心）；2) 氘代分子3在无核自旋环境中于10K下T₂=0.21 ms；3) 工程化低能振动可显著提升T₂，分子2在300K下T₁=27 ms；4) 双声子系间穿越在300K下具有高自旋选择性。

Conclusion: 建立了自旋退相干与自旋选择性激发态过程的统一第一性原理理论基础，提出了具有光学可寻址能力的高相干分子量子比特的新型化学设计策略。

Abstract: The identification of molecules that combine long spin coherence times and efficient spin-optical interfaces, ideally at room temperature, is pivotal towards the development of molecular quantum technology. By means of advanced first-principles methods, we here unravel the electronic structure for triangulene (1), its aza-cation derivative (2), and the crystal of 2,6,10-tri-tert-butyl-4,8,12-trimesityl-triangulene (3), and show that these organic diradicals possess a triplet ground state well separated from the first singlet excited state approaching 0.5 eV, closely resembling solid-state defects like nitrogen vacancy centers. In addition, we compute spin decoherence times due to the interaction with phonons and surrounding nuclear spins, showing that a deuterated molecule of 3 in a nuclear spin-free environment would support $T_2 = 0.21$ ms at 10 K. Importantly, we show that the engineering of specific low-energy vibrations could significantly improve $T_2$ toward the limit imposed by the molecular core spin relaxation, here estimated to be as long as $T_1=27$ ms at 300 K for 2. Finally, we compute two-phonon contributions to inter-system crossing at 300 K for2 as a luminescent prototype, and find that it is highly spin-selective, supporting the possibility to engineer optical read out and spin initialization. These results advance a unified first-principles theoretical foundation of spin decoherence and spin-selective excited-state processes and point to novel chemical design strategies for optically addressable, highly coherent molecular qubits.

</details>


### [157] [GroverFigureOfMerit: An Agnostic Figure of Merit for Quantum Backend Characterization in the NISQ Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08636)
*Tiago Restucha,Marcos Guillermo Lammers,Alejandro Fernández*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对NISQ时代量子硬件后端性能评估难题，提出基于Grover算法的统一性能指标（FoM），结合成功概率、均匀性和泄漏惩罚，通过Qonscious框架在IBM、IonQ等后端上实现跨架构评估，实验验证了对噪声、拓扑和编译开销的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ时代量子硬件提供商通过异构接口和专有指标暴露能力，静态表征指标（相干时间T1/T2、门错误率）存在局限性：无法捕获动态变异性、忽视编译影响、缺乏跨物理架构的可比性，阻碍了知情后端选择。

Method: 提出GroverFigureOfMerit算法级压力测试指标，组合目标态成功概率、非均匀放大惩罚和泄漏惩罚（改编自GRADE），通过Qonscious框架（条件执行平台+多态适配器）在IBM、IonQ后端及模拟器上无感执行。

Result: 指标能在统一评分下区分后端性能，捕获内在算法极限；在理想模拟器和真实处理器噪声模型上验证了对噪声、拓扑和编译开销的敏感性。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了基于Grover算法的跨架构量子后端统一性能评估指标，物理QPUs验证为自然下一步。

Abstract: The Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) era poses a challenge for developers: hardware providers expose capabilities through heterogeneous interfaces with proprietary metrics varying widely across providers, hindering informed backend selection. Static characterization metrics - coherence times T1/T2, gate error rates - exhibit limitations: they fail to capture dynamic variability across successive executions, overlook the impact of transpilation, and lack architectural comparability across physically distinct technologies. We propose a Figure of Merit (FoM) based on Grover's algorithm as an algorithmic stress test evaluating quantum backend performance holistically. The metric combines success probability on target states with penalties for non-uniform amplification and leakage to non-marked states, yielding a unified score across hardware architectures. Implemented via the Qonscious framework - a conditional execution platform using polymorphic adapters, it executes agnostically on IBM, IonQ backends, and simulators. Main contributions: (1) proposal and validation of GroverFigureOfMerit, incorporating uniformity and leakage penalties (adapted from GRADE) with emphasis on noise, transpilation, and topological constraints; (2) systematic analysis of heterogeneity across nine quantum providers motivating agnostic metrics; and (3) experimental demonstration via ideal simulators and real-processor noise models, confirming sensitivity to noise, topology, and transpilation overhead. Results confirm the metric distinguishes backend performance under a unified score, capturing intrinsic algorithmic limits. Validation on physical QPUs is identified as a natural next step.

</details>


### [158] [Symmetry as a route to generalized bosonic Kitaev chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08638)
*Gideon Lee,Tony Jin,Aashish A. Clerk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了玻色子Kitaev链（BKC）模型中对称性的关键作用，证明任何具有手征对称性的二次玻色子配对哈密顿量必然具有动力学矩阵的有效时间反演对称性（与物理时间反演无关），并探讨了与正交解耦相关的有效粒子-空穴对称性。作者推广了BKC-like模型，对一维平移不变配对哈密顿量进行了分类，并建立了BKC与辛Hatano-Nelson模型之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管BKC模型看似简单且为厄米系统，却展现出非厄米拓扑现象（如趋肤效应），其背后的对称性机制尚不清晰。本文旨在阐明对称性在其中的核心作用，并以此为框架构建广义模型。

Method: 通过分析BKC模型的动力学矩阵对称性（有效时间反演对称性和有效粒子-空穴对称性），利用手征对称性推导出一般性结论；对一维平移不变玻色子配对哈密顿量进行对称性分类；将BKC映射到非厄米费米子系统（辛Hatano-Nelson模型）进行比较研究。

Result: 发现了手征对称性必然诱导有效时间反演对称性（非物理时间反演），该对称性支持非平凡拓扑不变量；正交解耦特性与有效粒子-空穴对称性相关而非时间反演对称性；构建了仅保留其中一种有效对称性的广义BKC模型；完成了1D配对哈密顿量的完全分类。

Conclusion: BKC模型中的非厄米拓扑现象根植于其隐藏的有效对称性结构，该对称性框架可用于系统性地构造和研究广义玻色子拓扑系统，并揭示了玻色子系统与非厄米费米子系统之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: The bosonic Kitaev chain (BKC) model is a deceptively simple looking quadratic pairing Hamiltonian. Despite being purely Hermitian, it exhibits a number of striking non-Hermitian topological phenomena, including skin effects. We show here how symmetries play a key role in this model, and how identifying these allows one to develop generalized BKC-like models. We emphasize the surprising fact that any quadratic bosonic pairing Hamiltonian with a sublattice (chiral) symmetry necessarily has a dynamical matrix with an effective time reversal symmetry. This symmetry is unrelated to physical time-reversal, but enables non-trivial topological invariants. We also discuss how this symmetry is unrelated to another key property of the BKC, the decoupling of quadrature dynamics. This feature can instead be connected to a distinct symmetry, namely an effective particle-hole symmetry of the dynamical matrix. We discuss non-trivial generalized BKC models that only keep one of these two effective symmetries intact. We also provide a classification of all translationally-invariant 1D pairing Hamiltonians, and show connections between the BKC and a well-studied non-Hermitian fermionic system, the symplectic Hatano-Nelson model.

</details>


### [159] [Extracting conformal data from Loschmidt echoes after critical quenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08649)
*Aleix Bou-Comas,Stefano Carignano,Sergio Cerezo-Roquebrún,Esperanza Lopez,Luca Tagliacozzo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从实时间动力学中提取共形场论数据的系统方法，无需制备临界低能态，通过分析Loschmidt振幅及其相关量，可重建边界算符谱、中心荷及临界指数等共形数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形场论为从乘积态淬火到临界哈密顿量后的Loschmidt振幅提供了普适预言，但如何从实际动力学过程中提取共形数据（如边界算符谱、中心荷、临界指数）仍缺乏系统方法。

Method: 通过解析延拓将Loschmidt振幅映射为边界共形场论在条带上的配分函数，其横向转移矩阵编码边界算符谱和中心荷；利用局域时空扰动给出平衡关联函数以获取临界指数；通过有限链Loschmidt回波的系统尺寸依赖重建低能边界谱（阻尼振荡编码边界标度维数差）；提出有限尺寸标度协议以从实验或模拟中提取这些量。

Result: 建立了Loschmidt振幅与边界共形场论配分函数的精确对应；证明了广义时间熵的时间标度行为与空间纠缠熵的平衡标度行为类似；展示了低能边界谱可从有限链Loschmidt回波的尺寸依赖中重建；提出了可在当前量子平台上实现的有限尺寸标度提取协议。

Conclusion: 该工作为从量子动力学实验中直接提取共形数据提供了一套完整而实用的理论框架，无需制备复杂的临界基态，适用于当前最先进的量子模拟和计算平台。

Abstract: Conformal field theory provides universal predictions for Loschmidt amplitudes following quenches from product states to critical Hamiltonians. Building on this observation, we develop a route to extracting conformal data from real-time dynamics without preparing critical low-energy states. After analytic continuation, the Loschmidt amplitude is described by a boundary-CFT partition function on a strip, whose transverse transfer matrix encodes both the boundary operator spectrum and the central charge. Local space-time perturbations of the amplitude are governed by equilibrium correlation functions, and therefore provide access to critical exponents. In parallel, generalized temporal entropies exhibit scaling with time analogous to the equilibrium scaling of spatial entanglement entropy. We show that the low-lying boundary spectrum can be reconstructed from the system-size dependence of finite-chain Loschmidt echoes, whose damped oscillations encode differences of boundary scaling dimensions. Finally, we propose a finite-size scaling protocol that can extract these quantities from simulations or experiments on state-of-the-art quantum platforms.

</details>


### [160] [Temperature Beyond Equilibrium in Isolated Quantum Many-Body Systems and Their Subsystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08655)
*Maurizio Fagotti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在非平衡量子多体系统中定义温度的新方法，通过能量相干结构来定位正则状态族中的状态，并用最小判别信息原理替代最大熵原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温度在远离平衡态的孤立量子多体系统中含义模糊，传统热力学概念无法处理系统保持能量相干性（无经典热力学类比）的非平衡态。

Method: 提出温度在非平衡态下定位状态于与其能量相干结构相容的正则状态族中；用最小判别信息原理替代最大熵原理来定义逆温度；对子系统发展了局部热力学结构理论。

Result: 得到了非平衡正则状态的自然温度定义；逆温度一般不再是热力学熵对能量的导数；子系统温度不能单从约化态推断，需由诱导的局部热力学结构决定，边界歧义在热力学极限下消除。

Conclusion: 非平衡态温度定义依赖于能量相干结构，经典热力学关系（熵-能量导数）不成立；子系统温度需考虑局部热力学结构而非孤立约化态。

Abstract: Temperature is one of the central concepts of thermodynamics, yet its meaning away from equilibrium remains elusive. This problem is particularly acute in isolated quantum many-body systems: their states evolve unitarily, need not be close to equilibrium, and can retain energy coherence, a feature with no classical thermodynamic analogue. A non-stationary quantum state contains two kinds of energy fluctuations. One is associated with energy populations and has the usual thermodynamic interpretation; the other arises from coherence between energy sectors and drives time dependence. We propose that temperature, also out of equilibrium, locates the state within the family of regular states compatible with its energy-coherence structure. This leads to a natural definition of temperature for regular nonequilibrium states. The resulting inverse temperature is not generally the derivative of thermodynamic entropy with respect to energy. Indeed the principle of maximum entropy does not extend in its usual form; it is replaced by a principle of minimum discrimination information. We also develop the corresponding theory for subsystems, where temperature cannot in general be inferred from the reduced state alone. Instead, it is determined by the induced local thermodynamic structure, with boundary ambiguities removed in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [161] [Instability of the undecidable behavior of the spectral gap in 1D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08686)
*Laura Castilla-Castellano,Angelo Lucia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一维谱隙不可判定性构造对微扰极度敏感：任意小的局部扰动即可使原本不可判定的谱隙问题变为可判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明格点量子自旋系统中谱隙的存在性在一维和多维情况下是不可判定的，但这类构造是否具有鲁棒性尚不清楚，即微扰是否会改变其不可判定性质。

Method: 针对Bausch等人一维谱隙不可判定性构造，引入1-局域、秩1的扰动，扰动范数为O(ε)，分析扰动后谱隙问题的可判定性。

Result: 对于任意ε>0，存在范数为O(ε)的1-局域秩1扰动，使得原始构造中的谱隙问题变为可判定。

Conclusion: 谱隙不可判定性构造具有极端敏感性——任意小的微扰即可消除其不可判定性。这表明不可判定性在物理意义上可能不是"稳健"的，该现象对理解量子多体系统的可计算性具有重要意义。

Abstract: The problem of determining the existence of a spectral gap in a lattice quantum spin system was previously shown to be undecidable for one [J. Bausch et al., "Undecidability of the spectral gap in one dimension", Physical Review X 10 (2020)] or more dimensions [T. S. Cubitt et al., "Undecidability of the spectral gap", Nature 528 (2015) and Forum of Mathematics Pi, 10 (2022)]. In this work, we focus on the 1-dimensional result, showing that the constructed family with undecidable behavior is extremely sensitive to perturbations. In particular, for any $\varepsilon > 0$, there exists a 1-local, rank 1, perturbation with norm $O(\varepsilon)$, such that the spectral gap problem for the family in [J. Bausch et al., "Undecidability of the spectral gap in one dimension", Physical Review X 10 (2020)] now becomes decidable.

</details>


### [162] [Low-latency FPGA-based electronic control system for fast preparation of defect-free atom arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08687)
*Ya-Dong Hu,Dong-Qi Ma,Tian-Yang Zhang,Liang Chen,Yi-Chen Zhang,Xiao-Kang Zhong,Wen-Yi Zhu,Hong-Jie Fan,Qing-Xuan Jie,Yan-Lei Zhang,Gang Li,Xi-Feng Ren,Xu-Liang Zhang,Guang-Can Guo,Zhu-Bo Wang,Chang-Ling Zou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于FPGA的电子控制系统，消除了PC在反馈回路中的参与，在统一的PXIe架构中集成了光子计数、实时决策和波形生成功能。系统实现了282微秒的总反馈延迟，并在实验中验证了从24个随机装载的光镊中组装无缺陷原子阵列的能力。单轮重排实现约96%的填充率，五轮迭代反馈重排将生成10原子无缺陷阵列的成功概率从65.7%提升至95.4%。该系统为中性原子平台上的电路中测量和实时量子纠错建立了电子基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子量子计算的可扩展性需求驱动了具有低延迟、模块化架构和实时反馈能力的集成电子控制系统的开发。现有系统因PC参与反馈回路导致延迟过高，无法满足实时量子纠错的要求。

Method: 采用FPGA作为核心处理器，基于PXIe统一架构集成光子计数、实时决策和波形生成功能，完全消除PC在反馈回路中的参与，实现全硬件化的实时反馈控制。

Result: 总反馈延迟为282微秒；从24个随机装载光镊中，单轮重排实现约96%填充率；五轮迭代反馈重排将10原子无缺陷阵列的成功概率从65.7%提升至95.4%。

Conclusion: 该系统建立了中性原子平台上进行电路中测量和实时量子纠错所需的电子基础设施，为大规模中性原子量子计算提供了可行的控制方案。

Abstract: The scalability of neutral atom quantum computing demands integrated electronic control systems with low latency, modular architecture, and real-time feedback capability. Here, we present an FPGA-based electronic control system that eliminates the PC from the feedback loop, integrating photon counting, real-time decision-making, and waveform generation within a unified PXIe architecture. The system achieves a total feedback latency of $282\,\mathrm{μs}$ and is validated in practical experiments by assembling defect-free atom arrays from 24 stochastically loaded optical tweezers. A single-round rearrangement achieves a filling fraction of $\sim96\%$, while feedback-controlled iterative rearrangement over five rounds boosts the success probability for generating a 10-atom defect-free array from $65.7\%$ to $95.4\%$. This system establishes the electronic infrastructure necessary for mid-circuit measurement and real-time quantum error correction on neutral-atom platforms.

</details>


### [163] [Quantifying randomness with measurement incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08697)
*Sebastian Schlösser,Pauli Jokinen,Martin Plávala,Leevi Leppäjärvi,Leonardo S. V. Santos,Roope Uola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在制备-测量场景中，观测到的测量不相容性与经典窃听者能力之间存在权衡关系，并建立了测量不相容性与随机性生成之间的量化联系，通过不相容性见证作为随机性证书，利用广义鲁棒性这一几何度量通过半定规划限制Eve的策略，同时提供了从任意不相容测量集生成随机性的明确协议。该结果可推广至量子导引和具有量子存储的Eve场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立测量不相容性与随机性生成之间的量化关系，利用不相容性见证作为随机性证书来限制窃听者能力。

Method: 通过广义鲁棒性这一几何度量，结合半定规划来限制Eve的策略，并提供明确的随机性生成协议。

Result: 发现了测量不相容性与经典窃听者能力之间的权衡关系，并将结果推广到量子导引场景，建立了有限测量输入下导引能力与随机性生成之间的紧密联系。

Conclusion: 该技术可推广至Eve具有量子存储的场景，通过联合可测量性的维度推广实现。

Abstract: We present a trade-off between the amount of observed measurement incompatibility and the capabilities of a classical Eavesdropper in a prepare-and-measure scenario. The result is based on a qualitative connection between measurement incompatibility and randomness generation together with the utilization of incompatibility witnesses as randomness certificates. This allows one to use a geometric measure of incompatibility, the generalised robustness, to bound Eve's strategies through a semi-definite program, while providing an explicit protocol for generating randomness from any set of incompatible measurements. By translating the result to quantum steering, we find a tight connection between steerability and randomness generation in a setting using any finite number of measurement inputs. We further show how our techniques can be generalised to scenarios where Eve has a quantum memory by using a dimensional generalisation of joint measurability.

</details>


### [164] [Absence of quantum advantage for approximate spin glass optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08708)
*Dries Sels,Flaviano Morone*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用半经典大自旋S分析和截断Wigner近似，研究了QAOA在SK模型上的表现。发现最终能量随自旋S呈非单调变化，存在最优平衡点S~p，使能量收敛到Parisi值且收敛速度为log(p)/p。半经典方法略优于真实自旋-1/2的QAOA，两者收敛方式相同。去除初始噪声并重新优化参数后，性能提升至1/p收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QAOA在SK模型上的行为，特别是自旋大小S如何影响算法性能，以及半经典近似与真实量子系统的关系。

Method: 采用半经典大自旋S分析和截断Wigner近似，固定QAOA角度为先前确定的S=1/2最优值，研究不同自旋S下的最终能量表现。

Result: 最终能量随自旋S呈非单调变化：小S时半经典受噪声主导，大S时受初始波动指数增长限制。深度p的QAOA在S~p处达到最优平衡，能量收敛到Parisi值的速度为log(p)/p。半经典方法略优于自旋-1/2的QAOA。去除初始噪声并重新优化参数后，收敛速度提升至1/p。

Conclusion: 半经典方法和大自旋分析为理解QAOA性能提供了有效视角，揭示了自旋大小、噪声与收敛速度之间的权衡关系，为改进量子近似优化算法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We perform a semiclassical, large-spin S, analysis of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) on the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick (SK) model, using the truncated Wigner approximation. Fixing the QAOA angles to their previously determined optimal S=1/2 values, we observe a non-monotonic dependence of the final energy on the spin. At small S the semiclassics is dominated by noise, while the large-S limit is constrained by the exponential growth of the initial fluctuations. For a depth-p QAOA one achieves the optimal balance at S of order p, resulting in a convergence of the final energy to the Parisi value like log(p)/p. We find that the semiclassics slightly outperforms the true spin-1/2 QAOA, and thus suggest they both converge to the Parisi value in the same way. Finally, removing all the initial noise, and re-optimizing the parameters to account for that change, results in superior performance with 1/p convergence.

</details>


### [165] [Robust One-Sided Device-Independent Quantum Key Distribution via High-Dimensional Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08709)
*Monika Mothsara,Suraj Goel,Bohnishikha Ghosh,Vatshal Srivastav,Will McCutcheon,Mehul Malik,Gláucia Murta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了一种高维单边设备无关量子密钥分发（HD 1sDI-QKD）协议，利用光子横向空间自由度的纠缠，通过量子导引认证安全性，证明增加维度可增强抗噪和抗损能力，并在维度d=7时获得最高密钥率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子密钥分发虽具信息论安全性，但在实际中易受噪声、损耗和设备不完美的影响，亟需提出更鲁棒的协议来克服这些限制。

Method: 提出高维单边设备无关QKD协议，利用光子横向空间自由度的纠缠，通过量子导引进行安全性分析，采用反向协调计算密钥率，并在维度最高11的系统中实现可编程多输出测量装置。

Result: 增加维度可增强对噪声和损耗的鲁棒性；在公平采样假设下所有维度均获得正密钥率，其中维度d=7时密钥率最高。

Conclusion: 该工作在理论上证明了高维1sDI-QKD的优势，实验上展示了关键构建模块，并讨论了在现实损耗和噪声条件下实现无漏洞1sDI-QKD的后续步骤。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) brings the promise of communication with information-theoretic security but is limited in practice due to its susceptibility to noise, losses, and device imperfections. To address these challenges, we propose a robust high-dimensional (HD) one-sided device-independent QKD (1sDI-QKD) protocol and present a proof-of-principle experimental implementation using photons entangled in the transverse-spatial degree-of-freedom. We develop a systematic security analysis of HD 1sDI-QKD protocols, leveraging quantum steering to certify security, and evaluate achievable secret key rates for different measurement configurations and system dimensions using reverse reconciliation. Our analysis shows that increasing the dimension enhances robustness against both noise and loss. We then demonstrate the key experimental building blocks required for implementing the protocol: (a) a high-quality source of high-dimensional photonic entanglement, and (b) a fully programmable, high-dimensional multi-outcome measurement device operating in up to dimension 11. Using these components, we obtain positive key rates for all investigated dimensions under the fair-sampling assumption, with the highest key rates achieved for dimension d=7. Finally, we discuss the steps required for a practical, loophole-free implementation of 1sDI-QKD in realistic regimes of loss and noise.

</details>


### [166] [Approaching Carnot Efficiency at Finite Power in an Experimentally Feasible Quantum Heat Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08713)
*Shogo Toma,Atsushi Noguchi,Ken Funo,Hiroyasu Tajima*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种超导电路热机，通过模拟集体跳跃机制，在有限功率下渐进接近卡诺效率，突破了经典马尔可夫热机的功率-效率权衡限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典马尔可夫热机受限于功率-效率权衡，无法在有限功率下渐进接近卡诺效率。量子系统中理论上预测简并、对称性和集体跳跃可以增强活性从而实现这一目标，但尚缺乏实验可实现的模型验证。

Method: 提出一种超导电路热机设计，通过电路量子电动力学平台模拟量子系统中的集体增强耗散机制，实现活性增强。

Result: 该超导电路热机能够在有限功率下渐进接近卡诺效率，证明了增强耗散机制可以规避经典马尔可夫热机的功率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 本工作将有限时间热力学的抽象界与具体电路QED平台联系起来，为基于集体增强耗散过程的量子器件设计提供了新路径。

Abstract: Whether a heat engine can approach Carnot efficiency while maintaining finite power is a fundamental question in finite-time thermodynamics. For classical Markovian heat engines with local interactions, the power-efficiency trade-off forbids an asymptotic approach to Carnot efficiency at finite power. In quantum systems, by contrast, degeneracy, symmetry, and collective jumps have been theoretically predicted to enable such an asymptotic attainment by enhancing activity. It has remained open, however, whether this mechanism can be realized in an experimentally implementable heat engine. In this Letter, we propose a superconducting-circuit heat engine that emulates the collective enhancement, thereby enabling an asymptotic approach to Carnot efficiency at finite power. This result demonstrates that, in an implementable model, such an enhanced dissipative mechanism circumvents the power-efficiency trade-off of classical Markovian engines. Our work connects abstract bounds in finite-time thermodynamics to a concrete circuit-QED platform and suggests a route toward quantum-device design based on collectively enhanced dissipative processes.

</details>


### [167] [Hockey stick $f$-divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08760)
*Fumio Hiai,Milán Mosonyi,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对量子冰球棒散度定义的量子f-散度进行了系统且统一的处理与扩展，涵盖非归一化态、一般冯·诺依曼代数框架，以及Neyman-Pearson误差概率表示、信道可逆性检测、正则化Rényi散度等价性等核心结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一和扩展近年来基于量子冰球棒散度的量子f-散度理论，将其推广到非归一化态、更一般的积分表示形式以及一般冯·诺依曼代数框架。

Method: 发展了非归一化态和更一般量子冰球棒散度定义的冰球棒f-散度；引入了带额外实参数的更一般积分表示形式；将理论扩展到一般冯·诺依曼代数。

Result: 1) 在一般冯·诺依曼代数上建立了冰球棒f-散度的Neyman-Pearson误差概率表示；2) 扩展了Jenčová关于量子信道可逆性检测的结果；3) 证明了正则化冰球棒Rényi α-散度在α∈(0,1)时等同于Petz型Rényi散度，在α>1时等同于夹层Rényi散度；4) 给出了不同量子f-散度在量子态对上取值相等的部分刻画结果。

Conclusion: 完成了量子冰球棒散度理论的系统化统一和扩展，为量子信息论中的散度理论提供了更一般化的数学框架。

Abstract: In this paper we give a systematic and unified treatment and extensions of various results on a new notion of quantum $f$-divergences defined from quantum hockey stick divergences, the theory of which has been developed recently in \cite{BHT_fdiv,HircheTomamichel_integral,LiuHircheCheng2025}. In particular, we consider non-normalized states and hockey stick $f$-divergences defined from more general notions of quantum hockey stick divergences, as well as a somewhat more general form of the integral representation defined in terms of an additional real parameter. We also consider the extension of the theory to general von Neumann algebras, and extend various results from \cite{HircheTomamichel_integral,LiuHircheCheng2025} to this setting. Our main results here are the representation of the hockey stick $f$-divergences in terms of Neyman-Pearson error probabilities, which was given in the finite-dimensional case in \cite{LiuHircheCheng2025}, an extension of Jen\v cová's result \cite{Jencova2023} on the detection of reversibility of a quantum channel on a pair of states in terms of the hockey stick divergences, and an extension of a result in \cite{HircheTomamichel_integral} showing that the regularized hockey stick Rényi $α$-divergences coincide with the Petz-type Rényi divergences for $α\in(0,1)$ and with the sandwiched Rényi divergences for $α>1$. Moreover, we give some partial results on the characterization of when different notions of quantum $f$-divergences give the same value on a pair of quantum states.

</details>


### [168] [Irreducible Geometry of Higher-Order Correlator Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08761)
*Kaito Kobayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于高阶关联子族集体分析的几何框架，通过将关联子表示为算子词之间的内积，将每个关联子族重构为算子空间中的几何结构。引入条件子空间将信息分为可约信息（已被选定分辨部分解释）和不可约信息（残差关联子几何），并定义不可约体积轮廓来量化不可解释部分在独立几何方向上的分布广度。框架包括正则条件化、目标条件化、Krylov条件化和交叉条件化等多种形式，揭示了单个关联子值层面隐藏的不可约结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程量子模拟器已能获取从四点非时序关联子到高阶多体关联子等日益复杂的关联子，但解读这些数据的理论框架相对滞后。高阶关联子的物理含义难以推断，且不同关联子间存在冗余或独立等复杂关系，需要将关联子作为结构化族而非孤立量进行分析。

Method: 将关联子表示为算子词之间的内积，每个关联子族对应算子空间中的几何结构；引入条件子空间将几何分离为可约信息（已解释部分）和不可约信息（残差部分）；定义不可约体积轮廓量化残差部分在独立几何方向上的分布广度；提出正则条件化、目标条件化、Krylov条件化和交叉条件化等多种形式。

Result: 建立了关联子几何作为量子多体动力学的高层次描述框架，揭示了单个关联子值层面隐藏的不可约结构，提供了多种互补的条件化形式用于关联子族分析。

Conclusion: 该几何框架为集体分析高阶关联子族提供了系统化方法，能够识别关联子之间的冗余与独立关系，揭示隐藏的不可约结构，为量子多体动力学的理解提供了更高层次的理论描述。

Abstract: Programmable quantum simulators are beginning to access correlators of increasing complexity, ranging from four-point out-of-time-ordered correlators to even higher-order many-body correlators. The theoretical framework for interpreting such data, however, remains comparatively underdeveloped. Although a variety of higher-order correlators can be constructed straightforwardly, their physical meaning is often difficult to infer. A further complication is that different correlators are generally not independent: some may be mutually redundant, while others may encode genuinely distinct information. These features make it necessary to analyze correlators not as isolated quantities, but as a structured family. In this work, we develop a geometric framework for the collective analysis of higher-order correlator families. By representing correlators as inner products between operator words, we recast each family as a geometry in operator space. The key idea is to introduce conditioning subspaces that separate this geometry into reducible information, already explained by a chosen resolved sector, and irreducible information, encoded in the residual correlator geometry. Focusing on the latter component, we define irreducible volume profiles that quantify how broadly the unexplained part of a correlator family spreads over independent geometric directions. This perspective leads to several complementary forms of conditioning. Canonical conditioning optimally explains a correlator family. Targeted conditioning fixes the resolved sector to isolate a chosen physical feature. Krylov and cross conditioning extend the framework from a single correlator family to comparisons among correlator geometries. Our framework reveals irreducible structures hidden at the level of individual correlator values and establishes correlator geometry as a higher-level description of quantum many-body dynamics.

</details>


### [169] [Typicality of Steering for Two-qubit States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08762)
*Gerard Anglès Munné,Paweł Cieśliński,Tamás Vértesi,Wiesław Laskowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子导引（quantum steering）这一非经典现象的典型性行为。针对一般双量子比特态和m次Haar随机投影测量，推导了Werner态在两测量设置和三测量设置（限制在Bloch球共面投影测量）下导引概率的解析表达式。通过数值分析发现，随着测量设置数增加，导引概率系统性地增大，并显著超过贝尔非定域性对应的概率。随机态在有限m下表现出高导引概率，随测量设置数增加趋近于100%。研究还表征了不同态系综（包括Bell对角态和Werner态）的导引概率，识别出具有最大非经典潜力的态，并强调了其在以导引为关键资源的协议中的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子导引是介于纠缠和贝尔非定域性之间的量子关联现象，在量子通信与计算中具有重要应用价值。然而，对于一般随机量子态，观测到导引行为的概率（即导引的"典型性"）尚缺乏系统研究。本文旨在回答：给定一个一般双量子比特态和若干随机投影测量，观察到导引的概率是多少？

Method: 采用解析推导与数值模拟相结合的方法。解析上，针对Werner态推导了两测量设置和三测量设置（共面投影测量）下导引概率的精确表达式。数值上，对不同测量设置数和多种随机态系综（包括一般随机态、Bell对角态等）进行Monte Carlo模拟，计算导引概率并对比贝尔非定域性概率。

Result: 导引概率随测量设置数增加而系统性地增大，且显著高于贝尔非定域性概率。对于与环境耦合最小的随机态，有限测量设置下即表现出高导引概率，随设置数增加趋近于100%。不同态系综的导引概率存在差异，识别出具有最大非经典潜力的态（如特定Werner态和Bell对角态）。

Conclusion: 量子导引在随机双量子比特态中具有典型性，尤其是在测量设置足够多的情况下几乎必然出现。这一结果表明导引作为一种量子资源比贝尔非定域性更为普遍和鲁棒，对于基于导引的量子信息协议具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Phenomena that slip beyond the grasp of our classical intuition reveal uniquely quantum effects that deepen our understanding of the physical world and enable advances in information processing, particularly in quantum communication and computation. One such phenomenon is quantum steering, whereby measurements performed by one party influence the conditional states of another when the two share an entangled quantum system. If the observed correlations cannot be explained by a local hidden state model, the state is said to be steerable. In this work, we investigate the typicality of this behavior: given a generic two-qubit state and $m$ Haar-random projective measurements, what is the probability of observing steering? We derive analytical expressions for the steering probability $\mathcal{P}_S$ of Werner states in two- and three-setting scenarios, the latter restricted to coplanar projective measurements on the Bloch sphere. For larger numbers of settings and various random states ensembles, we perform numerical analyses showing that $\mathcal{P}_S$ increases systematically with the number of measurements and substantially exceeds the corresponding probabilities associated with Bell nonlocality. Our results demonstrate that random states with minimal environmental coupling exhibit a high probability of steering for finite $m$ and approach genuine typicality, $\mathcal{P}_S=100\%$, as the number of settings increases. We provide a detailed characterization of $\mathcal{P}_S$ across different state ensembles and specific families, including Bell-diagonal and Werner states, identifying those with the greatest non-classical potential and highlighting their relevance for protocols in which steering serves as a key resource.

</details>


### [170] [Plaquette: A hardware-aware design platform for fault-tolerant quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08767)
*Raul Conchello Vendrell,Carlos Díaz López,Ish Dhand,Kshitij Kapoor,Davide Laureti,Marcello Massaro,Pranjal Nayak,Ivan Ogloblin,Martin B. Plenio,Shreya Prasanna Kumar,Matteo Santandrea,Varun Seshadri,Antal Száva,Trevor Vincent,Raphael Weber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Plaquette框架，将硬件噪声模型（Kraus算子、Lindblad动力学等）自动编译为四种采样器（稳定子采样、XPauli采样、近Clifford采样、全态模拟）所需的表示，实现从物理层噪声到容错量子计算机逻辑性能的直接计算，并在超导量子比特泄漏、中性原子散射、离子阱加热三种噪声模型上验证了其准确性优于传统Clifford-only模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件团队构建容错量子计算机时需决定抑制哪些不完美因素，但硬件噪声往往偏离可扩展稳定子模拟器使用的随机Pauli模型（如超导transmon泄漏、中性原子散射、离子阱加热、相干过旋转等），缺乏从实际物理噪声直接计算逻辑性能的理论框架和软件工具。

Method: 提出Plaquette框架，核心流程为：将硬件误差模型（Kraus算子、Hamiltonian-Lindblad动力学或实验重构的量子信道）一次性指定后，自动编译为四种采样器所需的确切或近似表示——稳定子采样（Pauli噪声）、XPauli采样（泄漏和环境扇区）、近Clifford采样（相干误差）和全态模拟（精确参考计算）。

Result: XPauli和近Clifford采样器在统计不确定性内与全态模拟匹配，而Pauli twirling在某些误差模型下偏差较大。在超导量子比特泄漏、中性原子中间态散射和离子阱加热三种模型上的演示表明，Plaquette与Clifford-only模拟之间的差异随平台和噪声过程而变化。

Conclusion: 可靠的容错阈值、误差预算和开销估计需要最准确的模拟方法，Plaquette提供了从器件开放系统物理到FTQC逻辑性能的直接路径，弥补了现有可扩展模拟器仅支持随机Pauli模型的不足。

Abstract: Hardware teams building fault-tolerant quantum computers (FTQCs) must decide which imperfections to suppress, and that decision requires the logical performance of the architecture under the device's actual noise. Hardware noise often departs from the stochastic Pauli models used by scalable stabilizer simulators: superconducting transmons leak out of the computational subspace, neutral atoms scatter through intermediate states, trapped ions heat as their motional modes absorb phonons, and miscalibrated controls over-rotate coherently. We present Plaquette, a theoretical framework and software suite that computes the logical performance of fault-tolerant architectures directly from the physics of such imperfections. In Plaquette, a hardware error model is specified once, as Kraus operators, Hamiltonian-Lindblad dynamics, or an experimentally reconstructed quantum channel, and is compiled automatically into the exact or approximate representation required by each of four sampler classes: stabilizer sampling for Pauli noise, the new XPauli sampler for leakage and environment sectors, near-Clifford samplers for coherent errors, and full-state simulation for exact reference calculations. We validate the XPauli and near-Clifford samplers against full-state simulation, which they can match within statistical uncertainty while Pauli twirling can fall short depending on the error model. We demonstrate the framework on three error models: leakage in superconducting qubits, intermediate-state scattering in neutral atoms, and heating in trapped ions. The size of the discrepancy between Plaquette and Clifford-only simulations varies with platform and noise process, so reliable thresholds, error budgets, and overhead estimates require the most accurate simulation available. Plaquette provides a direct path from the open-system physics of a device to the logical performance of the FTQC built on it.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Gravitational perturbations of a regular T-duality inspired black hole: Quasinormal modes, excitation factors, and time-domain evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07715)
*Bekir Can Lütfüoğlu,Mardon Abdullaev,Javlon Rayimbaev,Shokhzod Jumaniyozov,Shavkat Karshiboev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由非局域、T-对偶性启发零长导出的中性规则黑洞的轴向引力扰动，发现当零长参数开启时，ADM标度下的准正则模实部增大，环信号振荡加快，阻尼率变化较平缓，有效势垒升高，激发因子变化幅度远小于频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨非局域、T-对偶性启发的零长对规则黑洞引力扰动的影响，理解其与Schwarzschild极限的偏离。

Method: 使用高阶WKB-Padé方法计算基态准正则模及多个泛音，并通过时域演化验证主导模。

Result: 零长参数开启后，ℓ=2,3,4引力模的实部增大（环信号加快），阻尼率先小幅增加后在高变形参数下减小；有效势垒升高；激发因子变化幅度远小于频率。

Conclusion: 零长参数显著改变准正则模频率（实部增大），但对激发因子影响较弱，表明该非局域修正主要影响环信号的振荡频率而非激发强度。

Abstract: We study axial gravitational perturbations of the neutral regular black hole generated by a non-local, T-duality-inspired zero-point length and the associated gravitational self-energy. In this geometry, the usual point source is replaced by a regular core, and the zero-point length controls the departure from the Schwarzschild limit. We compute the fundamental quasinormal modes and several overtones using high-order WKB--Padé methods, and we verify the dominant mode via direct time-domain evolution. When the zero-point length is turned on, the real parts of the ADM-scaled frequencies increase for the gravitational modes with $\ell=2,3,4$, so the ringdown oscillates faster than in the Schwarzschild limit. The damping rates change more gradually: they initially increase slightly and then decrease near the largest deformation values considered here. This behavior is consistent with the effective potential, whose barrier becomes higher as the deformation parameter increases. We also compute the corresponding excitation factors and find that their magnitudes vary much less strongly than the quasinormal frequencies.

</details>


### [172] [Deformed Compact Objects in General Relativity and Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07736)
*Jonathan Tejeda Quartuccio*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本论文研究了中子星等致密天体在广义相对论、变形TOV（D-TOV）形式以及f(R,T)引力下的流体静力学平衡，发现变形参数和物质-几何耦合显著影响致密星的质量-半径关系和平衡结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密星是研究超核密度物质和强场引力的独特实验室，作者旨在探索不同引力理论和几何变形场景下致密星的平衡结构。

Method: 推导了广义相对论下的标准TOV方程，引入单参数变形方案得到D-TOV形式，并扩展到f(R,T)引力框架；采用GM1状态方程、MIT袋模型及多方状态方程，计算了中子星和奇异星在不同引力理论下的平衡序列。

Result: 在广义相对论中，变形参数D显著影响星体性质：扁球构型支持更大的质量和半径，长球构型导致质量更小、星体更致密；在f(R,T)模型中，依赖于迹的物质-几何耦合进一步改变了质量-半径关系和最大支持质量。

Conclusion: 有效变形和依赖于迹的物质-几何耦合可以显著影响致密星的平衡结构。

Abstract: Neutron stars and related compact objects are unique laboratories for probing matter at supranuclear densities and gravity in the strong-field regime. In this thesis, we investigate the hydrostatic equilibrium of compact stars in different geometric and gravitational scenarios, combining relativistic stellar structure, dense-matter microphysics, and numerical modeling. We derive the standard Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equation in General Relativity, introduce an effective one-parameter deformation scheme leading to the deformed TOV (D-TOV) formalism, and extend the hydrostatic equilibrium framework to f(R,T) gravity.
  Using these structure equations, we compute equilibrium sequences for neutron stars and strange stars described by the GM1 equation of state, the MIT Bag Model, and a polytropic equation of state. In General Relativity, the deformation parameter D significantly affects the global stellar properties: oblate configurations generally support larger masses and radii than the spherical case, while prolate configurations lead to less massive and more compact stars. In optimized and linear f(R,T) models, trace-dependent matter-geometry couplings further modify the mass-radius relation and the maximum supported mass. Overall, the results indicate that effective deformation and trace-dependent matter-geometry coupling can significantly affect the equilibrium structure of compact stars.

</details>


### [173] [Systematic Light Propagation Bias from the Heliosphere and Its Impact on the Hubble Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07741)
*Behnam Pourhassan,İzzet Sakallı,Sudhaker Upadhyay,Kairat Myrzakulov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出哈勃张力可能源于太阳系日球层环境对本地距离测量的系统效应，通过理论计算表明该效应可解释约3-8%的观测差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决哈勃常数本地测量值（73.5±1.4 km/s/Mpc）与CMB预测值（67.4±0.5 km/s/Mpc）之间约8-9%的显著 discrepancy，即哈勃张力问题。

Method: 理论分析日球层中加热等离子体和能量中性原子对造父变星和Ia型超新星观测的影响，考虑各向异性、部分校准抵消和色散抑制等因素，计算对哈勃常数测量的系统偏差。

Result: 日球层效应可解释约8%的观测差异，在考虑各向异性和校准抵消后，实际贡献范围约3-8%。

Conclusion: 哈勃张力可能部分源于日球层环境引入的系统误差，而非完全需要新物理，并提出了验证该假说的观测检验方案。

Abstract: The Hubble tension (HT) represents one of the most significant discrepancies in modern cosmology, with local distance measurements yielding $H_0 = 73.5 \pm 1.4$ km/s/Mpc while cosmic microwave background (CMB) observations predict $H_0 = 67.4 \pm 0.5$ km/s/Mpc. We propose that this tension arises from systematic effects introduced by our Solar System's heliospheric (HS) environment on local distance measurements. The HS creates a complex medium of heated plasma and energetic neutral atoms (ENAs) beyond the heliopause (HP), where interstellar medium (ISM) temperatures rise significantly. This thermal gradient and particle environment may systematically affect observations of Cepheid variables and Type Ia supernovae (SNe~Ia) used in the local cosmic distance ladder (CDL), biasing distance measurements and artificially inflating the measured Hubble constant. We present theoretical calculations showing how HS effects could account for up to $\sim 8\%$ of the observed 8--9\% discrepancy, with the realistic contribution lying in the $\sim 3$--$8\%$ range once anisotropy, partial calibration cancellation, and chromatic suppression are included, and discuss observational tests to validate this hypothesis.

</details>


### [174] [Observational Limits on Einasto Dark Matter Parameters from Event Horizon Telescope Images of Sgr A$^{*}$ and M87$^{*}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07752)
*A. Errehymy,S. Hansraj,C. Hansraj*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用事件视界望远镜(EHT)对Sgr A*和M87*的阴影测量数据，结合Einasto型暗物质晕模型，通过贝叶斯参数估计约束了黑洞周围内部暗物质分布，发现对Sgr A*的暗物质密度上限约为10^{-11} M⊙/pc³，而Einasto指数ν约束较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHT提供了对超大质量黑洞的直接成像，使检验引力理论成为可能。任何扩展的质量分布（如暗物质晕）都会扰动光子环区域附近的零测地线，因此阴影测量可以作为探测内晕结构的探针。本文旨在利用EHT阴影数据约束Einasto型暗物质晕的参数空间。

Method: 从Einasto密度轮廓出发，构造度规函数f(r)=1-2M/r+2M_∞ g̃(r)，该函数在黑洞视界与渐近晕之间插值。分析了光子势、零测地线和作为黑洞质量函数的阴影半径。利用EHT测量的无量纲阴影直径d_sh进行贝叶斯参数估计，识别Einasto参数空间的允许区域。

Result: 对于Sgr A*，采用恒星轨道质量先验，在1σ置信度下得到ϱ0 ≲ 10^{-11} M⊙/pc³；对于M87*，由于距离不确定性，约束较弱。Einasto指数ν约束不强，表明EHT精度主要限制光子球附近所包围的质量，而非轮廓斜率。

Conclusion: EHT阴影数据能够对黑洞周围的暗物质分布提供有意义的约束，未来EHT观测将进一步改进这些约束，并区分不同的暗物质模型。

Abstract: The Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) has provided images of the supermassive black-holes Sgr A$^{*}$ and M87$^{*}$, enabling direct tests of gravity. Any extended mass-distribution, such as a dark-matter halo, perturbs null-geodesics in the photon-ring regime, making shadow-measurements a probe of inner-halo structure. In this work, we investigate static, spherically-symmetric black-holes surrounded by Einasto-type dark-matter halos and derive constraints from EHT shadow-data. Starting from the Einasto density-profile with parameters ${\varrho_0, \tildeα, \tildeν}$, we construct a metric-function $f(r)=1-2M/r+2M_\infty \tilde{g}(r)$ that interpolates between the black-hole horizon and the asymptotic halo, following the approach of Xu et al. (2018) but adapted specifically to the Einasto scenario. We analyze the photon-potential, null-geodesics, and shadow-radius as functions of black-hole mass $M$, in the non-spinning limit. Using the dimensionless shadow-diameter $d_{\text{sh}}\equiv Dθ/M$ measured by the EHT -- $d_{\text{sh}}^{M87*}=11.0\pm1.5$ and $d_{\text{sh}}^{SgrA*}=9.5\pm1.4$ -- we perform Bayesian parameter-estimation to identify allowed regions in the Einasto parameter-space. Combined with the independently-measured black-hole masses from stellar-dynamics, our results place constraints on the inner dark-matter distribution: for Sgr A$^{*}$, adopting the stellar-orbit mass-prior, we find $\varrho_0 \lesssim 10^{-11},M_\odot/\text{pc}^3$ at $1σ$ confidence, while for M87$^{*}$ the bounds are weaker due to distance-uncertainties. The Einasto-index $\tildeν$ is weakly-constrained, indicating that EHT precision primarily limits the mass enclosed near the photon-sphere rather than the profile-slope. Future EHT observations will refine these constraints and distinguish between competing dark-matter descriptions.

</details>


### [175] [Regular Black Holes in Nonlocal Quasitopological Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07790)
*Pablo Bueno,Pablo A. Cano,Robie A. Hennigar,Ángel J. Murcia*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了无限导数版本的拟拓扑引力理论，该理论无鬼、避免强耦合不稳定性，并允许精确的球对称真空规则黑洞解，同时满足微扰比克霍夫定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟拓扑引力中的高阶导数项通常会导致鬼魅态和强耦合不稳定性，且难以获得规则（无奇点）的黑洞解。本文旨在通过引入无限导数修正来克服这些问题，构造一个自洽的引力理论。

Method: 采用无限导数（非局部）算子替代拟拓扑引力中的有限高阶导数项，通过对理论进行色散关系分析和微扰分析，确保无鬼魅态和强耦合不稳定性。然后寻找精确的球对称真空解，并验证微扰比克霍夫定理。

Result: 成功构造了无鬼、避免强耦合不稳定性的无限导数拟拓扑引力理论，得到了精确的球对称真空规则黑洞解，并且该解满足微扰比克霍夫定理——即稳态解在微扰下保持稳定性。

Conclusion: 无限导数修正可以有效解决拟拓扑引力中的鬼魅和强耦合问题，同时允许规则黑洞解的存在，为非奇点黑洞物理提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We present infinite-derivative completions of Quasitopological gravities that are ghost-free, avoid strong coupling instabilities and admit exact, spherically symmetric vacuum regular-black-hole solutions satisfying a perturbative Birkhoff theorem.

</details>


### [176] [Singularity resolution and unitarity in two-dimensional dilaton black holes with negative central charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07806)
*César García-Pérez,F. Javier Marañón-González,José Navarro-Salas,Silvia Pla*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类一阶环修正的二维CGHS模型，通过组合Polyakov型作用量和局域抵消项，在负总中心荷条件下经典曲率奇点被消除，视界内出现渐近平坦区域，外部霍金辐射通量保持不变并与内部辐射通量相关联，后者短暂呈现负值，暗示了幺正性的可能保留，但完整的能量平衡尚未建立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一阶环修正的CGHS双引力模型，探索在量子修正下经典曲率奇点的消除机制，以及视界内外辐射通量的关联性，旨在揭示黑洞信息丢失问题的可能解。

Method: 将非局域Polyakov作用量（物质涨落）与基于辅助平直度规的Polyakov型项（Faddeev-Popov鬼影重参数化）及局域抵消项相结合，在负总中心荷条件下求解后反应方程。

Result: 经典曲率奇点被消除，视界内部出现渐近平坦区域；外部霍金辐射通量保持不变，与内部辐射通量存在关联，内部通量出现短暂负值区间。

Conclusion: 辐射通量的相关性暗示了幺正性的可能保留（需相关零曲面距坍塌物质轨迹为有限仿射距离），但当前框架下无法建立完全一致的能量平衡，需进一步研究。

Abstract: We study a one-loop corrected extension of the classical Callan-Giddings-Harvey-Strominger (CGHS) model of two-dimensional dilaton gravity. The effective action combines the non-local Polyakov action for matter fluctuations, a Polyakov-type term built from an auxiliary flat metric that implements Strominger's mechanism for the Faddeev-Popov reparametrization ghosts, and a local counterterm that simultaneously preserves the flatness of the auxiliary metric, ensures exact solvability, and keeps two-dimensional Minkowski spacetime as an exact solution of the backreacted equations. In the regime of negative total central charge, the classical curvature singularity is resolved and gives way to asymptotically flat regions inside the horizon. The exterior Hawking flux is preserved and turns out to be correlated with an internal radiation flux supported on null surfaces that approach null infinity from beyond the horizon; this internal flux, in particular, presents a short interval of negative values. These correlations point to the preservation of unitarity, provided the relevant null surfaces remain at a finite affine distance from the collapsing matter trajectory. Within the present formulation, however, a fully consistent energy balance cannot yet be established. We discuss possible strategies to overcome this issue.

</details>


### [177] [Causal Horizons, Geodesic Completeness and Stability in Slow Contraction Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07815)
*Mariam Khaldieh,Anna I. Rosenzweig,Paul J. Steinhardt*

Main category: gr-qc
